# jobpilot Kernel Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build the deterministic multi-agent kernel that every jobpilot agent plugs into: contracts, the handoff protocol, the capability broker, fail-closed guardrails, two-tier policy, a wiring compiler, the step executor, the supervisor with team breakers, a hash-chained audit log, contract drift detection and an eval runner. It is proven end to end by a two-agent demo pipeline.

**Architecture:** Agents are pure `run(input, ctx)` functions behind Zod contracts. A supervisor routes typed envelopes between them using a wiring graph compiled and checked at boot, and drains a SQLite work queue in a total order. Every step goes through one executor that checks guardrails, memoises by a content hash, enforces budgets and timeouts, and commits envelopes, step status and cost in one transaction. An LLM is only ever called inside an agent, through a broker-issued tool.

**Tech Stack:** Node 24 · TypeScript 5.9 (strict, NodeNext) · Zod 4.6 · better-sqlite3 13 · commander 15 · tsx 4 · vitest 5 · pnpm 10 (install only).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-jobpilot-architecture-design.md`: §0.3 two-tier policy, §5 contract model, §5.5 handoff protocol, §6 supervisor and state machine, §7.2 team leads, §8 guardrails, §11 data model, §14 testing, §15 evals.

**Verified:** every code block below was built and run in a scratch copy before this plan was written: 18 test files, 243 tests, typecheck clean, `node --run verify` green. (Hardened during execution after review: Task 3 contracts — governance fields in the hash, deep freeze, two-way capability and retry rules; Task 5 policy — host normalisation, own-property egress lookups, deep freeze; Task 6 broker — redirects, ports and credentials refused, provider failures mapped, one model call in flight, per-step spend cap; Task 7 guardrails — fail closed on any throw, misregistered mandatory checks, invalid verdicts and missing output; Task 8 wiring — one type id may not carry two schemas, the seed must be consumed; Task 9 job machine — QA retry bounded to one regeneration, NEEDS_HUMAN exits; Task 10 store — anchored audit-chain head with optional expected head, atomic version-guarded migrations, steps finish exactly once; Task 11 executor — input guardrails before replay, door quarantines not cached, spend summed across retries, events chained in the step transaction, never throws; broker refuses calls after a timeout; Task 12 supervisor — rejected transitions audited not fatal, open obligations and replay counts derived from run events, per-run breakers, run lifecycle guards; Task 13 drift check — stale exports, hand-edited shapes, newer versions, unreadable files and a missing directory are reported; Task 14 evals — no vacuous gates, production no-coercion, enforced timeouts, strict JSONL; Task 15 — Node guard in a separate entry file, broader containment and no-evasion scans incl. the lockfile.) After the final whole-branch review, five fix waves closed further gaps found with live probes: past-version lineage, quarantine release (including door trips), hard budget reservation with over-priced calls stopping an agent for the run, enforced obligations, rising taint with seed classes and a redactor allow-list, recorded broker denials, an AST containment scan of agent code, several trackers with separate entity namespaces, and a refusal of effect agents until G-APPROVAL. The known bounds that remain are documented at the top of `src/kernel/executor.ts`.

## Global Constraints

- Node **24** or newer (`"engines": { "node": ">=24" }`, `"volta": { "node": "24.15.0" }`, `.npmrc` `engine-strict=true`).
- **Run scripts with `node --run <script>`, never `pnpm <script>`.** On this machine Volta's pnpm runs under Node 20.20, and the better-sqlite3 native addon segfaults there (exit 139, no message). Use pnpm only for `pnpm install` / `pnpm add`.
- Exact dependency versions: `zod@4.6.5`, `better-sqlite3@13.0.3`, `commander@15.0.0`; dev: `typescript@5.9.3`, `vitest@5.0.3`, `tsx@4.23.15`, `@types/better-sqlite3@7.6.13`, `@types/node@24`.
- ESM only (`"type": "module"`); relative imports end in `.js`.
- TypeScript `strict`, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`, `verbatimModuleSyntax`.
- **An LLM is never on the wire.** Only an agent's `run()` may call `ctx.llm`; the supervisor, executor, wiring and guardrails are plain code.
- **Fail closed.** Invalid output is quarantined, never coerced (`G-SCHEMA` trips if parsing would change the payload).
- **Determinism.** No timestamps, run ids or `Math.random` in envelopes, step keys or ordering; hash with `canonicalJson`; queues drain `ORDER BY stage, agent_id, envelope_id`.
- **One agent, one skill.** A contract description containing the word "and" is rejected.
- **No evasion capability exists.** The broker has no proxy, user-agent, fingerprint or CAPTCHA option. Stealth and CAPTCHA packages fail the build (Task 15).
- Repository is local-only. Commit after every task; **never push**.
- Every commit message ends with the trailer: `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`

`tests/kernel/canonical.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { canonicalJson, hashOf, sha256 } from '../../src/kernel/canonical.js';

describe('canonicalJson', () => {
  it('sorts keys at every depth so insertion order never changes the output', () => {
    expect(canonicalJson({ b: 1, a: { d: 2, c: 3 } })).toBe('{"a":{"c":3,"d":2},"b":1}');
    expect(canonicalJson({ a: { c: 3, d: 2 }, b: 1 })).toBe(canonicalJson({ b: 1, a: { d: 2, c: 3 } }));
  });

  it('sorts integer-like keys as strings, unlike JSON.stringify', () => {
    expect(canonicalJson({ '2': 'x', '10': 'y', a: 'z' })).toBe('{"10":"y","2":"x","a":"z"}');
  });

  it('drops undefined properties and nulls undefined array items', () => {
    expect(canonicalJson({ a: undefined, b: [1, undefined] })).toBe('{"b":[1,null]}');
  });

  it('normalises -0 to 0', () => {
    expect(canonicalJson(-0)).toBe('0');
  });

  it('throws on values without a stable JSON form', () => {
    expect(() => canonicalJson({ x: Number.NaN })).toThrow(/non-finite number at \$\.x/);
    expect(() => canonicalJson({ d: new Date(0) })).toThrow(/unsupported object at \$\.d/);
    expect(() => canonicalJson({ f: () => 1 })).toThrow(/unsupported function/);
  });

  it('hashes the canonical form', () => {
    expect(hashOf({ b: 1, a: 2 })).toBe(sha256('{"a":2,"b":1}'));
    expect(hashOf({ a: 2, b: 1 })).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the implementation**

`src/kernel/canonical.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

/**
 * Deterministic JSON: object keys sorted by code unit, `undefined` object
 * properties dropped, `undefined` array items become null. Throws on values
 * that have no stable JSON form (NaN, Infinity, Date, Map, class instances).
 */
export function canonicalJson(value: unknown): string {
  return serialise(value, '$');
}

function serialise(v: unknown, path: string): string {
  if (v === null) return 'null';
  switch (typeof v) {
    case 'string':
      return JSON.stringify(v);
    case 'boolean':
      return v ? 'true' : 'false';
    case 'number':
      if (!Number.isFinite(v)) throw new TypeError(`canonicalJson: non-finite number at ${path}`);
      return JSON.stringify(Object.is(v, -0) ? 0 : v);
    case 'object': {
      if (Array.isArray(v)) {
        return `[${v.map((x, i) => serialise(x === undefined ? null : x, `${path}[${i}]`)).join(',')}]`;
      }
      const proto: unknown = Object.getPrototypeOf(v);
      if (proto !== Object.prototype && proto !== null) {
        throw new TypeError(`canonicalJson: unsupported object at ${path}`);
      }
      const rec = v as Record<string, unknown>;
      const parts: string[] = [];
      for (const k of Object.keys(rec).sort()) {
        const x = rec[k];
        if (x === undefined) continue;
        parts.push(`${JSON.stringify(k)}:${serialise(x, `${path}.${k}`)}`);
      }
      return `{${parts.join(',')}}`;
    }
    default:
      throw new TypeError(`canonicalJson: unsupported ${typeof v} at ${path}`);
  }
}

export function sha256(text: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(text, 'utf8').digest('hex');
}

export function hashOf(value: unknown): string {
  return sha256(canonicalJson(value));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`src/kernel/clock.ts`:

```ts
export interface Clock {
  now(): string;
}

export const systemClock: Clock = { now: () => new Date().toISOString() };

export function fixedClock(iso: string): Clock {
  return { now: () => iso };
}
```

`src/kernel/errors.ts`:

```ts
export const ERROR_CODES = [
  'rate_limit',
  'overloaded',
  'timeout',
  'network',
  'invalid_output',
  'policy_denied',
  'budget_exceeded',
  'unknown',
] as const;
export type ErrorCode = (typeof ERROR_CODES)[number];

/** Only these may appear in a contract's retry.on list. */
export const RETRYABLE: ReadonlySet<ErrorCode> = new Set(['rate_limit', 'overloaded', 'timeout', 'network']);

export class KernelError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly code: ErrorCode,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'KernelError';
  }
}

export class WiringError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly problems: readonly string[]) {
    super(`pipeline wiring is invalid:\n- ${problems.join('\n- ')}`);
    this.name = 'WiringError';
  }
}

export class PolicyViolation extends Error {
  constructor(readonly violations: readonly string[]) {
    super(`user policy may only tighten product policy:\n- ${violations.join('\n- ')}`);
    this.name = 'PolicyViolation';
  }
}

export class InvalidTransition extends Error {
  constructor(machine: string, from: string, on: string) {
    super(`${machine}: no transition from ${from} on ${on}`);
    this.name = 'InvalidTransition';
  }
}

export function classify(err: unknown): ErrorCode {
  if (err instanceof KernelError) return err.code;
  if (typeof err === 'object' && err !== null && (err as { name?: unknown }).name === 'TimeoutError') return 'timeout';
  return 'unknown';
}
```

`src/kernel/data-class.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * P0 public job data · P1 contact PII · P2 offers and salary · P3 mailbox bodies.
 * Ordered by sensitivity for taint propagation: an output inherits the most
 * sensitive class among its inputs unless its agent is a declared redactor.
 */
export const DATA_CLASSES = ['P0', 'P1', 'P2', 'P3'] as const;
export type DataClass = (typeof DATA_CLASSES)[number];

export function maxClass(classes: readonly DataClass[]): DataClass {
  let max: DataClass = 'P0';
  for (const c of classes) if (DATA_CLASSES.indexOf(c) > DATA_CLASSES.indexOf(max)) max = c;
  return max;
}
```

`src/kernel/types.ts`:

```ts
import type { z } from 'zod';

export interface MessageType<T = unknown> {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly version: number;
  /** `Name@version`, the routing key. */
  readonly id: string;
  readonly schema: z.ZodType<T>;
}

export function defineType<T>(name: string, version: number, schema: z.ZodType<T>): MessageType<T> {
  if (!/^[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*$/.test(name)) throw new TypeError(`message type name must be PascalCase: ${name}`);
  if (!Number.isInteger(version) || version < 1) throw new TypeError(`message type version must be a positive integer: ${version}`);
  return Object.freeze({ name, version, id: `${name}@${version}`, schema });
}
```

`src/kernel/predicates.ts`:

```ts
export interface Predicate<T = unknown> {
  /** Dotted lower-case name, e.g. `text.sanitised`. Guarantees and requirements match by id. */
  readonly id: string;
  readonly description: string;
  readonly test: (payload: T) => boolean;
}

export function definePredicate<T>(id: string, description: string, test: (payload: T) => boolean): Predicate<T> {
  if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*(\.[a-z][a-z0-9-]*)+$/.test(id)) {
    throw new TypeError(`predicate id must be dotted lower-case, e.g. text.sanitised: ${id}`);
  }
  return Object.freeze({ id, description, test });
}
```

### Task 3: Agent contracts

The contract is the unit of governance: types in and out, promises, capabilities, egress, data classes, budget, retry, breaker, guardrails. `defineContract` enforces the specialisation rule by rejecting descriptions containing "and".

**Files:**
- Create: `src/kernel/contract.ts`
- Test: `tests/kernel/contract.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `MessageType`, `Predicate`, `DataClass`, `ErrorCode`, `RETRYABLE`, `hashOf`.
- Produces: `Contract<I,O>`, `AnyContract`, `ContractSpec<I,O>`, `AgentKind`, `Capabilities`, `ObligationSpec`, `defineContract(spec)`, `contractMajor(c)`, `contractShape(c)`, `contractHash(c)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`tests/kernel/contract.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { contractHash, contractMajor, contractShape, defineContract, type ContractSpec } from '../../src/kernel/contract.js';
import { definePredicate } from '../../src/kernel/predicates.js';
import { defineType } from '../../src/kernel/types.js';

const In = defineType('ThingIn', 1, z.object({ n: z.number() }).strict());
const Out = defineType('ThingOut', 1, z.object({ n: z.number() }).strict());

const base: ContractSpec<{ n: number }, { n: number }> = {
  id: 'thing-doubler',
  version: '1.0.0',
  description: 'Doubles a number deterministically',
  skills: ['doubling'],
  team: 'demo',
  kind: 'deterministic',
  consumes: In,
  produces: Out,
  dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0'] },
  budget: { usdPerCall: 0, usdPerRun: 0 },
  timeoutMs: 1000,
};

describe('defineContract', () => {
  it('fills defaults and freezes the contract', () => {
    const c = defineContract(base);
    expect(c.retry).toEqual({ max: 0, on: [], backoffMs: [] });
    expect(c.breaker.consecutiveFailures).toBe(5);
    expect(Object.isFrozen(c)).toBe(true);
    expect(contractMajor(c)).toBe('1');
  });

  it('rejects a description containing "and": one agent, one skill', () => {
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, description: 'Parses and scores a posting' })).toThrow(/split it/);
  });

  it('rejects non-semver versions and non-kebab ids', () => {
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, version: '1.0' })).toThrow(/semver/);
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, id: 'Thing_Doubler' })).toThrow(/kebab-case/);
  });

  it('only lets retryable codes into retry.on, with one backoff per retry', () => {
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, retry: { max: 1, on: ['invalid_output'], backoffMs: [10] } })).toThrow(/retryable/);
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, retry: { max: 2, on: ['timeout'], backoffMs: [10] } })).toThrow(/one entry per retry/);
  });

  it('ties capabilities to kind', () => {
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, capabilities: { llm: { model: 'm', maxTokens: 10 } } })).toThrow(/may not request capabilities.llm/);
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, kind: 'llm', budget: { usdPerCall: 0.01, usdPerRun: 1 } })).toThrow(/requires capabilities.llm/);
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, capabilities: { http: true } })).toThrow(/may not request capabilities.http/);
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, egress: ['example.com'] })).toThrow(/may not declare egress/);
  });

  it('changes its hash when the produced schema changes', () => {
    const a = defineContract(base);
    const Out2 = defineType('ThingOut', 1, z.object({ n: z.number(), extra: z.string() }).strict());
    const b = defineContract({ ...base, produces: Out2 });
    expect(contractHash(a)).not.toBe(contractHash(b));
    expect(contractHash(a)).toBe(contractHash(defineContract(base)));
  });

  it('changes its hash when a guarantee is added', () => {
    const g = definePredicate<{ n: number }>('n.even', 'n is even', (p) => p.n % 2 === 0);
    expect(contractHash(defineContract({ ...base, guarantees: [g] }))).not.toBe(contractHash(defineContract(base)));
  });

  it('changes its hash when budget, guardrails or retry change', () => {
    const h = contractHash(defineContract(base));
    expect(contractHash(defineContract({ ...base, kind: 'io', capabilities: { http: true }, egress: ['example.com'], budget: { usdPerCall: 0, usdPerRun: 1 } }))).not.toBe(h);
    const io: ContractSpec<{ n: number }, { n: number }> = { ...base, kind: 'io', capabilities: { http: true }, egress: ['example.com'] };
    const ioHash = contractHash(defineContract(io));
    expect(contractHash(defineContract({ ...io, budget: { usdPerCall: 0.1, usdPerRun: 1 } }))).not.toBe(ioHash);
    expect(contractHash(defineContract({ ...io, guardrails: { in: ['G-PII'], out: [] } }))).not.toBe(ioHash);
    expect(contractHash(defineContract({ ...io, retry: { max: 1, on: ['timeout'], backoffMs: [10] } }))).not.toBe(ioHash);
    expect(contractHash(defineContract({ ...io, timeoutMs: 2000 }))).not.toBe(ioHash);
    expect(contractHash(defineContract({ ...io, breaker: { consecutiveFailures: 2 } }))).not.toBe(ioHash);
  });

  it('does not change its hash when obligations.adds are reordered', () => {
    const a = defineContract({ ...base, obligations: { adds: [{ id: 'a.one', dischargedBy: 'x-agent' }, { id: 'b.two', dischargedBy: 'y-agent' }], discharges: [] } });
    const b = defineContract({ ...base, obligations: { adds: [{ id: 'b.two', dischargedBy: 'y-agent' }, { id: 'a.one', dischargedBy: 'x-agent' }], discharges: [] } });
    expect(contractHash(a)).toBe(contractHash(b));
  });

  it('deep-freezes nested structures', () => {
    const c = defineContract({
      ...base,
      kind: 'llm',
      capabilities: { llm: { model: 'm', maxTokens: 10 } },
      egress: ['api.anthropic.com'],
      budget: { usdPerCall: 0.01, usdPerRun: 1 },
      obligations: { adds: [{ id: 'a.one', dischargedBy: 'x-agent' }], discharges: [] },
    });
    expect(() => (c.egress as string[]).push('x')).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => {
      (c.capabilities as any).llm = { model: 'z', maxTokens: 1 };
    }).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => {
      (c.capabilities.llm as any).model = 'z';
    }).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => (c.dataClasses.in as string[]).push('P1')).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => {
      (c.obligations.adds[0] as any).id = 'zzz';
    }).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => {
      (c.budget as any).usdPerRun = 99;
    }).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => (c.retry.on as string[]).push('timeout')).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => (c.guardrails.in as string[]).push('G-X')).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => (c.requires as unknown[]).push(1)).toThrow(TypeError);
  });

  it('does not alias the caller spec', () => {
    const egress = ['example.com'];
    const classes: ('P0' | 'P1')[] = ['P0'];
    const spec: ContractSpec<{ n: number }, { n: number }> = {
      ...base,
      kind: 'io',
      capabilities: { http: true },
      egress,
      dataClasses: { in: classes, out: ['P0'] },
    };
    const c = defineContract(spec);
    const before = contractHash(c);
    egress.push('evil.com');
    classes.push('P1');
    expect(c.egress).toEqual(['example.com']);
    expect(c.dataClasses.in).toEqual(['P0']);
    expect(contractHash(c)).toBe(before);
  });

  it('requires egress for http and http or llm for egress', () => {
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, kind: 'io', capabilities: { http: true } })).toThrow(/capabilities.http requires at least one egress host/);
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, kind: 'io', egress: ['example.com'] })).toThrow(/egress requires capabilities.http or capabilities.llm/);
  });

  it('accepts a valid io contract and a valid llm contract', () => {
    const io = defineContract({ ...base, kind: 'io', capabilities: { http: true }, egress: ['example.com'] });
    expect(io.egress).toEqual(['example.com']);
    const llm = defineContract({
      ...base,
      kind: 'llm',
      capabilities: { llm: { model: 'claude-x', maxTokens: 100 } },
      egress: ['api.anthropic.com'],
      budget: { usdPerCall: 0.01, usdPerRun: 1 },
    });
    expect(llm.capabilities.llm?.maxTokens).toBe(100);
  });

  it('validates the llm capability shape', () => {
    const llm = { ...base, kind: 'llm' as const, egress: ['api.anthropic.com'], budget: { usdPerCall: 0.01, usdPerRun: 1 } };
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...llm, capabilities: { llm: { model: 'm', maxTokens: -1 } } })).toThrow(/maxTokens/);
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...llm, capabilities: { llm: { model: '', maxTokens: 10 } } })).toThrow(/model/);
  });

  it('rejects usdPerRun below usdPerCall', () => {
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, kind: 'io', capabilities: { http: true }, egress: ['example.com'], budget: { usdPerCall: 1, usdPerRun: 0.5 } })).toThrow(
      /usdPerRun must be at least budget.usdPerCall/,
    );
  });

  it('rejects inconsistent retry settings', () => {
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, retry: { max: 1, on: [], backoffMs: [10] } })).toThrow(/needs at least one code in retry.on/);
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, retry: { max: 1, on: ['timeout'], backoffMs: [10, 20] } })).toThrow(/exactly one entry per retry/);
  });
});

describe('emits (minimum output data class)', () => {
  it('must be one of the classes the contract may emit', () => {
    expect(() => defineContract({ ...base, emits: 'P1' })).toThrow(/emits P1 is not in dataClasses.out/);
    expect(defineContract({ ...base, dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0', 'P1'] }, emits: 'P1' }).emits).toBe('P1');
  });

  it('is part of the shape when set, and leaves existing hashes alone when not', () => {
    const plain = defineContract({ ...base, dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0', 'P1'] } });
    const emitting = defineContract({ ...plain, emits: 'P1' });
    expect(contractShape(plain)).not.toHaveProperty('emits');
    expect(contractShape(emitting)).toMatchObject({ emits: 'P1' });
    expect(contractHash(emitting)).not.toBe(contractHash(plain));
  });
});

describe('contractShape ordering is locale independent', () => {
  it('sorts obligations by code unit, and no source file uses localeCompare', () => {
    const c = defineContract({ ...base, obligations: { adds: [{ id: 'a.b', dischargedBy: 'x' }, { id: 'a-b', dischargedBy: 'x' }, { id: 'a0', dischargedBy: 'x' }], discharges: [] } });
    const ids = (contractShape(c).obligations as { adds: { id: string }[] }).adds.map((a) => a.id);
    expect(ids).toEqual(['a-b', 'a.b', 'a0']);
    const walk = (dir: string): string[] => readdirSync(dir).flatMap((f) => (statSync(join(dir, f)).isDirectory() ? walk(join(dir, f)) : [join(dir, f)]));
    const users = walk(join(import.meta.dirname, '../../src')).filter((f) => /\blocaleCompare\b/.test(readFileSync(f, 'utf8')));
    expect(users).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`src/kernel/contract.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { canonicalJson, hashOf } from './canonical.js';
import { DATA_CLASSES, type DataClass } from './data-class.js';
import { ERROR_CODES, RETRYABLE, type ErrorCode } from './errors.js';
import type { Predicate } from './predicates.js';
import type { MessageType } from './types.js';

export const AGENT_KINDS = ['deterministic', 'llm', 'io', 'io-session', 'human', 'effect'] as const;
export type AgentKind = (typeof AGENT_KINDS)[number];

export interface Capabilities {
  readonly http?: true;
  readonly llm?: { readonly model: string; readonly maxTokens: number };
}

export interface ObligationSpec {
  readonly id: string;
  /** Agent id that must discharge it before the item may finish. */
  readonly dischargedBy: string;
}

export interface Contract<I = unknown, O = unknown> {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly version: string;
  readonly description: string;
  readonly skills: readonly string[];
  readonly team: string;
  readonly kind: AgentKind;
  readonly consumes: MessageType<I>;
  readonly produces: MessageType<O>;
  readonly requires: readonly Predicate<I>[];
  readonly guarantees: readonly Predicate<O>[];
  readonly capabilities: Capabilities;
  readonly egress: readonly string[];
  readonly dataClasses: { readonly in: readonly DataClass[]; readonly out: readonly DataClass[] };
  readonly redactor: boolean;
  /** The least sensitive class this agent's outputs carry, whatever its input (e.g. P1 for an agent that attaches contact details). */
  readonly emits?: DataClass;
  readonly obligations: { readonly adds: readonly ObligationSpec[]; readonly discharges: readonly string[] };
  readonly budget: { readonly usdPerCall: number; readonly usdPerRun: number };
  readonly timeoutMs: number;
  readonly retry: { readonly max: number; readonly on: readonly ErrorCode[]; readonly backoffMs: readonly number[] };
  readonly breaker: { readonly consecutiveFailures: number };
  readonly guardrails: { readonly in: readonly string[]; readonly out: readonly string[] };
}

/** Predicates are contravariant in their payload, so heterogeneous collections use this. */
export type AnyContract = Contract<any, any>;

type Defaulted = 'capabilities' | 'egress' | 'redactor' | 'obligations' | 'retry' | 'breaker' | 'guardrails' | 'requires' | 'guarantees';
export type ContractSpec<I, O> = Omit<Contract<I, O>, Defaulted> & Partial<Pick<Contract<I, O>, Defaulted>>;

const kebab = /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$/;
const dotted = /^[a-z][a-z0-9.-]*$/;
const guardrailId = /^G-[A-Z0-9-]+$/;

const Meta = z.object({
  id: z.string().regex(kebab, 'id must be kebab-case'),
  version: z.string().regex(/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/, 'version must be semver x.y.z'),
  description: z
    .string()
    .min(10, 'description must say what the agent does')
    .refine((d) => !/\band\b/i.test(d), 'description contains "and": one agent does one skill, so split it'),
  skills: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1),
  team: z.string().regex(kebab, 'team must be kebab-case'),
  kind: z.enum(AGENT_KINDS),
  capabilities: z
    .object({
      http: z.literal(true).optional(),
      llm: z.object({ model: z.string().min(1), maxTokens: z.number().int().positive() }).strict().optional(),
    })
    .strict(),
  egress: z.array(z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9.-]+$/, 'egress entries are bare host names')),
  dataClasses: z.object({ in: z.array(z.enum(DATA_CLASSES)).min(1), out: z.array(z.enum(DATA_CLASSES)).min(1) }),
  redactor: z.boolean(),
  emits: z.enum(DATA_CLASSES).optional(),
  obligations: z.object({
    adds: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string().regex(dotted), dischargedBy: z.string().regex(kebab) }).strict()),
    discharges: z.array(z.string().regex(dotted)),
  }),
  guardrails: z.object({ in: z.array(z.string().regex(guardrailId)), out: z.array(z.string().regex(guardrailId)) }),
  budget: z
    .object({ usdPerCall: z.number().nonnegative(), usdPerRun: z.number().nonnegative() })
    .refine((b) => b.usdPerRun >= b.usdPerCall, 'budget.usdPerRun must be at least budget.usdPerCall'),
  timeoutMs: z.number().int().positive().max(600_000),
  retry: z
    .object({
      max: z.number().int().min(0).max(5),
      on: z.array(z.enum(ERROR_CODES)),
      backoffMs: z.array(z.number().int().nonnegative()),
    })
    .refine((r) => r.backoffMs.length === r.max, 'retry.backoffMs needs exactly one entry per retry')
    .refine((r) => r.max === 0 || r.on.length > 0, 'retry.max > 0 needs at least one code in retry.on')
    .refine((r) => r.on.every((c) => RETRYABLE.has(c)), 'retry.on may only list retryable codes'),
  breaker: z.object({ consecutiveFailures: z.number().int().min(1) }),
});

export function defineContract<I, O>(spec: ContractSpec<I, O>): Contract<I, O> {
  const c: Contract<I, O> = {
    capabilities: {},
    egress: [],
    redactor: false,
    obligations: { adds: [], discharges: [] },
    retry: { max: 0, on: [], backoffMs: [] },
    breaker: { consecutiveFailures: 5 },
    guardrails: { in: [], out: [] },
    requires: [],
    guarantees: [],
    ...spec,
  };
  const parsed = Meta.safeParse(c);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    const msg = parsed.error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join('.') || '(root)'}: ${i.message}`).join('; ');
    throw new TypeError(`contract ${spec.id}: ${msg}`);
  }
  checkKindCapabilities(c);
  return deepFreeze(snapshot(c));
}

function deepFreeze<T>(v: T): T {
  if (v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Object.isFrozen(v)) {
    Object.freeze(v);
    for (const child of Object.values(v)) deepFreeze(child);
  }
  return v;
}

/** Copies every array and plain object the spec owns. consumes/produces/predicates are shared by reference. */
function snapshot<I, O>(c: Contract<I, O>): Contract<I, O> {
  const { capabilities } = c;
  return {
    ...c,
    skills: [...c.skills],
    requires: [...c.requires],
    guarantees: [...c.guarantees],
    capabilities: {
      ...(capabilities.http ? { http: capabilities.http } : {}),
      ...(capabilities.llm ? { llm: { ...capabilities.llm } } : {}),
    },
    egress: [...c.egress],
    dataClasses: { in: [...c.dataClasses.in], out: [...c.dataClasses.out] },
    obligations: { adds: c.obligations.adds.map((a) => ({ ...a })), discharges: [...c.obligations.discharges] },
    budget: { ...c.budget },
    retry: { max: c.retry.max, on: [...c.retry.on], backoffMs: [...c.retry.backoffMs] },
    breaker: { ...c.breaker },
    guardrails: { in: [...c.guardrails.in], out: [...c.guardrails.out] },
  };
}

function checkKindCapabilities(c: AnyContract): void {
  const problems: string[] = [];
  if (c.kind === 'llm' && !c.capabilities.llm) problems.push('kind llm requires capabilities.llm');
  if (c.kind !== 'llm' && c.capabilities.llm) problems.push(`kind ${c.kind} may not request capabilities.llm`);
  const httpKinds: readonly AgentKind[] = ['io', 'io-session', 'effect'];
  if (c.capabilities.http && !httpKinds.includes(c.kind)) problems.push(`kind ${c.kind} may not request capabilities.http`);
  if (c.capabilities.http && c.egress.length === 0) problems.push('capabilities.http requires at least one egress host');
  if (c.egress.length > 0 && !c.capabilities.http && !c.capabilities.llm) problems.push('egress requires capabilities.http or capabilities.llm');
  if ((c.kind === 'deterministic' || c.kind === 'human') && c.egress.length > 0) problems.push(`kind ${c.kind} may not declare egress`);
  if (c.kind === 'deterministic' && c.budget.usdPerCall > 0) problems.push('kind deterministic has no spend, so budget must be 0');
  if (c.emits !== undefined && !c.dataClasses.out.includes(c.emits)) problems.push(`emits ${c.emits} is not in dataClasses.out`);
  if (problems.length > 0) throw new TypeError(`contract ${c.id}: ${problems.join('; ')}`);
}

export function contractMajor(c: Pick<Contract, 'version'>): string {
  return c.version.split('.')[0] ?? '0';
}

/** The interface (types, predicates, capabilities, data classes) and the governance settings (budget, timeout, retry, breaker, guardrails, obligations). Changing any of it without a version bump is drift. */
export function contractShape(c: AnyContract): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    id: c.id,
    version: c.version,
    kind: c.kind,
    team: c.team,
    consumes: { type: c.consumes.id, schema: z.toJSONSchema(c.consumes.schema) },
    produces: { type: c.produces.id, schema: z.toJSONSchema(c.produces.schema) },
    requires: c.requires.map((p) => p.id).sort(),
    guarantees: c.guarantees.map((p) => p.id).sort(),
    capabilities: c.capabilities,
    egress: [...c.egress].sort(),
    dataClasses: { in: [...c.dataClasses.in].sort(), out: [...c.dataClasses.out].sort() },
    redactor: c.redactor,
    // Only present when declared, so contracts without it keep their hash.
    ...(c.emits !== undefined ? { emits: c.emits } : {}),
    obligations: {
      adds: [...c.obligations.adds].sort((a, b) => (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0)).map((a) => ({ id: a.id, dischargedBy: a.dischargedBy })),
      discharges: [...c.obligations.discharges].sort(),
    },
    budget: c.budget,
    timeoutMs: c.timeoutMs,
    breaker: c.breaker,
    retry: { max: c.retry.max, on: [...c.retry.on].sort(), backoffMs: c.retry.backoffMs },
    guardrails: { in: c.guardrails.in, out: c.guardrails.out },
  };
}

/** The fields that decide what an agent may touch and how tainted its outputs are. Changing any of them needs a major version. */
export const SECURITY_FIELDS = ['kind', 'capabilities', 'egress', 'dataClasses', 'redactor', 'emits'] as const;

/** Security-relevant fields that differ between two contract shapes, in SECURITY_FIELDS order. */
export function securityDiff(a: Record<string, unknown>, b: Record<string, unknown>): string[] {
  return SECURITY_FIELDS.filter((f) => canonicalJson({ v: a[f] }) !== canonicalJson({ v: b[f] }));
}

export function contractHash(c: AnyContract): string {
  return hashOf(contractShape(c));
}
```

### Task 4: Envelopes and the handoff

What one agent passes to the next: payload plus guarantees, data class, obligations and policy pin. Envelopes contain no run id or timestamp, which is what makes replay byte-identical.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/kernel/envelope.ts`
- Test: `tests/kernel/envelope.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `sha256`, `Contract`, `DataClass`.
- Produces: `Envelope<T>`, `Handoff`, `Obligation`, `Producer`, `GuardrailVerdict`, `Cost`, `ZERO_COST`, `makeEnvelopeId(stepKey, index)`, `nextObligations(input, contract)`, `openObligations(env)`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`src/kernel/envelope.ts`:

```ts
import { sha256 } from './canonical.js';
import type { Contract } from './contract.js';
import type { DataClass } from './data-class.js';

export interface Obligation {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly dischargedBy: string;
  readonly discharged: boolean;
}

/** What one agent passes to the next, besides the payload. */
export interface Handoff {
  readonly guarantees: readonly string[];
  readonly dataClass: DataClass;
  readonly obligations: readonly Obligation[];
  readonly pins: { readonly policyHash: string };
}

export interface Producer {
  readonly agentId: string;
  readonly version: string;
  readonly contractHash: string;
}

export interface GuardrailVerdict {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly verdict: 'pass' | 'warn' | 'trip';
  readonly detail?: string;
}

export interface Cost {
  readonly usd: number;
  readonly inputTokens: number;
  readonly outputTokens: number;
}

export const ZERO_COST: Cost = Object.freeze({ usd: 0, inputTokens: 0, outputTokens: 0 });

/** Deliberately free of run ids and timestamps, so the same input yields byte-identical envelopes. */
export interface Envelope<T = unknown> {
  readonly envelopeId: string;
  readonly stepKey: string;
  readonly index: number;
  readonly type: string;
  readonly producer: Producer;
  readonly parents: readonly string[];
  readonly payload: T;
  readonly handoff: Handoff;
  readonly guardrails: readonly GuardrailVerdict[];
  readonly cost: Cost;
}

export function makeEnvelopeId(stepKey: string, index: number): string {
  return sha256(`${stepKey}:${index}`);
}

/** Discharge what this agent may discharge, add what it adds; sorted by id. */
export function nextObligations(input: readonly Obligation[], contract: Pick<Contract, 'id' | 'obligations'>): Obligation[] {
  const byId = new Map<string, Obligation>();
  for (const o of input) {
    const discharge = o.dischargedBy === contract.id && contract.obligations.discharges.includes(o.id);
    byId.set(o.id, discharge ? { ...o, discharged: true } : o);
  }
  for (const a of contract.obligations.adds) {
    if (!byId.has(a.id)) byId.set(a.id, { id: a.id, dischargedBy: a.dischargedBy, discharged: false });
  }
  return [...byId.values()].sort((a, b) => (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0));
}

export function openObligations(env: Pick<Envelope, 'handoff'>): string[] {
  return env.handoff.obligations.filter((o) => !o.discharged).map((o) => o.id);
}
```

### Task 5: Two-tier policy

Product policy is the safety floor; a user policy may only tighten it (G-POLICY-MONOTONIC). The merged policy is hashed and pinned on every envelope.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/kernel/policy.ts`
- Test: `tests/kernel/policy.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `hashOf`, `DataClass`, `DATA_CLASSES`, `PolicyViolation`.
- Produces: `Policy`, `PRODUCT_POLICY_V1`, `UserPolicy`, `mergePolicy(product, userRaw): Policy`, `policyHash(p)`, `hostMatches(host, pattern)`, `egressAllowed(policy, host, dataClass)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`tests/kernel/policy.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PolicyViolation } from '../../src/kernel/errors.js';
import { PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, egressAllowed, hostMatches, mergePolicy, normalizeHost, policyHash } from '../../src/kernel/policy.js';

describe('mergePolicy (G-POLICY-MONOTONIC)', () => {
  it('lets a user tighten caps and add denied hosts', () => {
    const p = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { maxUsdPerRun: 2, maxSubmitsPerDay: 3, deniedSubmitHosts: ['foundit.in'] });
    expect(p.maxUsdPerRun).toBe(2);
    expect(p.maxSubmitsPerDay).toBe(3);
    expect(p.deniedSubmitHosts).toEqual(['foundit.in', 'indeed.com', 'linkedin.com', 'naukri.com']);
  });

  it('refuses every attempt to loosen, listing all of them', () => {
    try {
      mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, {
        maxUsdPerRun: 50,
        maxSubmitsPerDay: 100,
        requireApprovalForEffects: false,
        egressClasses: { 'api.anthropic.com': ['P0', 'P1'] },
      });
      expect.unreachable();
    } catch (e) {
      expect(e).toBeInstanceOf(PolicyViolation);
      const v = (e as PolicyViolation).violations;
      expect(v).toHaveLength(4);
      for (const key of ['maxUsdPerRun', 'maxSubmitsPerDay', 'requireApprovalForEffects', 'egressClasses.api.anthropic.com']) {
        expect(v.some((m) => m.includes(key))).toBe(true);
      }
    }
  });

  it('rejects unknown keys in the user file', () => {
    expect(() => mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { maxUsdPerDay: 1 })).toThrow(PolicyViolation);
  });

  it('hashes differently once a user tightens anything', () => {
    expect(policyHash(mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, {}))).not.toBe(policyHash(mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { maxUsdPerRun: 1 })));
  });
});

describe('egressAllowed (G-PII-EGRESS)', () => {
  const p = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { deniedEgressHosts: ['tracker.example'] });

  it('allows public data to an allowed host', () => {
    expect(egressAllowed(p, 'api.anthropic.com', 'P0')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('never lets contact PII reach the model provider', () => {
    expect(egressAllowed(p, 'api.anthropic.com', 'P1')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('blocks denied hosts and their subdomains', () => {
    expect(egressAllowed(p, 'tracker.example', 'P0')).toBe(false);
    expect(egressAllowed(p, 'cdn.tracker.example', 'P0')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('policy hardening', () => {
  it('normalizeHost lower-cases and strips trailing dots', () => {
    expect(normalizeHost('Tracker.Example.')).toBe('tracker.example');
    expect(hostMatches('CDN.Tracker.Example.', 'tracker.example')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('matches the deny list regardless of case or trailing dot', () => {
    const p = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { deniedEgressHosts: ['tracker.example'] });
    for (const h of ['Tracker.Example', 'tracker.example.', 'CDN.tracker.example']) {
      expect(egressAllowed(p, h, 'P0')).toBe(false);
    }
  });

  it('normalises user host entries before merging', () => {
    const p = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { deniedEgressHosts: ['Tracker.Example.'] });
    expect(p.deniedEgressHosts).toEqual(['tracker.example']);
  });

  it('survives prototype-named hosts', () => {
    const p = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, {});
    for (const h of ['constructor', 'toString', 'hasOwnProperty']) {
      expect(egressAllowed(p, h, 'P1')).toBe(false);
      expect(egressAllowed(p, h, 'P0')).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('rejects __proto__ and malformed host keys', () => {
    expect(() => mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, JSON.parse('{"egressClasses":{"__proto__":["P0"]}}'))).toThrow(PolicyViolation);
    expect(() => mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { deniedEgressHosts: ['a b'] })).toThrow(PolicyViolation);
    expect(() => mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { deniedSubmitHosts: ['x/y'] })).toThrow(PolicyViolation);
    expect(() => mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { egressClasses: { 'a_b': ['P0'] } })).toThrow(PolicyViolation);
  });

  it('stores a user egress key named constructor as an own entry and uses it', () => {
    const p = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { egressClasses: { constructor: [] } });
    expect(Object.hasOwn(p.egressClasses, 'constructor')).toBe(true);
    expect(egressAllowed(p, 'constructor', 'P0')).toBe(false);
    expect(egressAllowed(p, 'toString', 'P0')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('reports schema failures as PolicyViolation with path: message', () => {
    try {
      mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { maxUsdPerRun: 'x' });
      expect.unreachable();
    } catch (e) {
      expect(e).toBeInstanceOf(PolicyViolation);
      expect((e as PolicyViolation).violations[0]).toMatch(/^maxUsdPerRun: /);
    }
  });

  it('is deeply frozen', () => {
    const merged = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { deniedSubmitHosts: ['foundit.in'] });
    expect(() => (PRODUCT_POLICY_V1.deniedSubmitHosts as string[]).push('x.com')).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => (merged.egressClasses['api.anthropic.com'] as string[]).push('P1')).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => ((merged.egressClasses as Record<string, unknown>)['new.host'] = [])).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(merged.egressClasses['api.anthropic.com']).not.toBe(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1.egressClasses['api.anthropic.com']);
    expect(merged.defaultEgressClasses).not.toBe(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1.defaultEgressClasses);
  });

  it('lets a user tighten a host to nothing', () => {
    const p = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { egressClasses: { 'api.anthropic.com': [] } });
    expect(egressAllowed(p, 'api.anthropic.com', 'P0')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('hashes identically for order, case and duplicate differences', () => {
    const a = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { deniedEgressHosts: ['a.example', 'b.example'] });
    const b = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { deniedEgressHosts: ['B.Example.', 'a.example', 'A.EXAMPLE', 'b.example'] });
    expect(policyHash(a)).toBe(policyHash(b));
  });
});

describe('redactors (agents allowed to lower a data class)', () => {
  it('the product policy allows none, and a user policy may not add one', () => {
    expect(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1.redactors).toEqual([]);
    expect(mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { redactors: [] }).redactors).toEqual([]);
    expect(() => mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, { redactors: ['pii-scrubber'] })).toThrow(/redactors may not add pii-scrubber/);
  });

  it('a user policy may keep a subset of what the product allows', () => {
    const product = { ...PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, redactors: ['a-scrubber', 'b-scrubber'] };
    expect(mergePolicy(product, { redactors: ['b-scrubber'] }).redactors).toEqual(['b-scrubber']);
    expect(mergePolicy(product, {}).redactors).toEqual(['a-scrubber', 'b-scrubber']);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`src/kernel/policy.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { hashOf } from './canonical.js';
import { DATA_CLASSES, type DataClass } from './data-class.js';
import { PolicyViolation } from './errors.js';

export interface Policy {
  readonly version: string;
  /** Hosts no agent may contact. */
  readonly deniedEgressHosts: readonly string[];
  /** Hosts no effect agent may submit to. */
  readonly deniedSubmitHosts: readonly string[];
  /** Data classes allowed to leave the machine, per host; hosts not listed use the default. */
  readonly egressClasses: Readonly<Record<string, readonly DataClass[]>>;
  readonly defaultEgressClasses: readonly DataClass[];
  readonly maxUsdPerRun: number;
  readonly maxPayloadBytes: number;
  readonly maxSubmitsPerDay: number;
  readonly requireApprovalForEffects: boolean;
  /** Agent ids allowed to lower a data class (declared `redactor: true`). */
  readonly redactors: readonly string[];
}

/** Lower-case and strip trailing dots, so `Tracker.Example.` and `tracker.example` are one host. */
export function normalizeHost(host: string): string {
  return host.toLowerCase().replace(/\.+$/, '');
}

function deepFreeze<T>(value: T): T {
  if (typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Object.isFrozen(value)) {
    Object.freeze(value);
    for (const v of Object.values(value)) deepFreeze(v);
  }
  return value;
}

/** The product safety floor. A user policy may tighten it, never loosen it. */
export const PRODUCT_POLICY_V1: Policy = deepFreeze<Policy>({
  version: 'product.v1',
  deniedEgressHosts: [],
  deniedSubmitHosts: ['linkedin.com', 'naukri.com', 'indeed.com'],
  egressClasses: { 'api.anthropic.com': ['P0'] },
  defaultEgressClasses: ['P0'],
  maxUsdPerRun: 5,
  maxPayloadBytes: 262_144,
  maxSubmitsPerDay: 10,
  requireApprovalForEffects: true,
  redactors: [],
});

const HostName = z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9.-]+$/);

const UserPolicy = z
  .object({
    deniedEgressHosts: z.array(HostName).optional(),
    deniedSubmitHosts: z.array(HostName).optional(),
    egressClasses: z.record(HostName, z.array(z.enum(DATA_CLASSES))).optional(),
    maxUsdPerRun: z.number().nonnegative().optional(),
    maxSubmitsPerDay: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional(),
    requireApprovalForEffects: z.boolean().optional(),
    redactors: z.array(z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$/)).optional(),
  })
  .strict();
export type UserPolicy = z.infer<typeof UserPolicy>;

const union = (a: readonly string[], b: readonly string[] = []): string[] =>
  [...new Set([...a, ...b].map(normalizeHost))].sort();

function classesFor(p: Policy, host: string): readonly DataClass[] {
  return Object.hasOwn(p.egressClasses, host) ? (p.egressClasses[host] ?? p.defaultEgressClasses) : p.defaultEgressClasses;
}

/** effective = product ∧ user. Throws PolicyViolation listing every rule the user file tries to relax. */
export function mergePolicy(product: Policy, userRaw: unknown): Policy {
  const parsed = UserPolicy.safeParse(userRaw ?? {});
  const issues = parsed.success ? [] : parsed.error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.map(String).join('.')}: ${i.message}`);
  // Zod's record silently drops a `__proto__` key, so check the raw keys ourselves.
  const rawClasses = (userRaw as { egressClasses?: unknown } | null | undefined)?.egressClasses;
  if (typeof rawClasses === 'object' && rawClasses !== null) {
    for (const k of Object.keys(rawClasses)) {
      if (!HostName.safeParse(k).success) issues.push(`egressClasses.${k}: invalid host name`);
    }
  }
  if (!parsed.success || issues.length > 0) throw new PolicyViolation(issues);
  const user = parsed.data;
  const violations: string[] = [];
  if (user.maxUsdPerRun !== undefined && user.maxUsdPerRun > product.maxUsdPerRun) {
    violations.push(`maxUsdPerRun may only be lowered (product ${product.maxUsdPerRun}, user ${user.maxUsdPerRun})`);
  }
  if (user.maxSubmitsPerDay !== undefined && user.maxSubmitsPerDay > product.maxSubmitsPerDay) {
    violations.push(`maxSubmitsPerDay may only be lowered (product ${product.maxSubmitsPerDay}, user ${user.maxSubmitsPerDay})`);
  }
  if (user.requireApprovalForEffects === false && product.requireApprovalForEffects) {
    violations.push('requireApprovalForEffects cannot be turned off');
  }
  for (const id of user.redactors ?? []) {
    if (!product.redactors.includes(id)) violations.push(`redactors may not add ${id}`);
  }
  const entries = new Map<string, readonly DataClass[]>(
    Object.entries(product.egressClasses).map(([h, c]) => [normalizeHost(h), [...c]]),
  );
  for (const [rawHost, classes] of Object.entries(user.egressClasses ?? {})) {
    const host = normalizeHost(rawHost);
    const allowed = classesFor(product, host);
    const extra = classes.filter((c) => !allowed.includes(c));
    if (extra.length > 0) violations.push(`egressClasses.${host} may not add ${extra.join(', ')}`);
    entries.set(host, [...new Set(classes)].sort());
  }
  if (violations.length > 0) throw new PolicyViolation(violations);
  return deepFreeze<Policy>({
    ...product,
    version: `${product.version}+user`,
    deniedEgressHosts: union(product.deniedEgressHosts, user.deniedEgressHosts),
    deniedSubmitHosts: union(product.deniedSubmitHosts, user.deniedSubmitHosts),
    egressClasses: Object.fromEntries(entries),
    defaultEgressClasses: [...product.defaultEgressClasses],
    maxUsdPerRun: user.maxUsdPerRun ?? product.maxUsdPerRun,
    maxSubmitsPerDay: user.maxSubmitsPerDay ?? product.maxSubmitsPerDay,
    redactors: [...new Set(user.redactors ?? product.redactors)].sort(),
  });
}

export function policyHash(p: Policy): string {
  return hashOf(p);
}

export function hostMatches(host: string, pattern: string): boolean {
  const h = normalizeHost(host);
  const pat = normalizeHost(pattern);
  return h === pat || h.endsWith(`.${pat}`);
}

export function egressAllowed(p: Policy, host: string, cls: DataClass): boolean {
  const h = normalizeHost(host);
  if (p.deniedEgressHosts.some((d) => hostMatches(h, d))) return false;
  return classesFor(p, h).includes(cls);
}
```

### Task 6: Providers, fakes, capability broker, agents

Agents never touch fetch or a model client. The broker issues only the tools a contract grants and enforces G-EGRESS, G-PII-EGRESS and G-BUDGET at the door. There is no proxy, fingerprint or CAPTCHA capability to grant.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/kernel/providers.ts`
- Create: `src/kernel/fakes.ts`
- Create: `src/kernel/broker.ts`
- Create: `src/kernel/agent.ts`
- Test: `tests/kernel/broker.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Contract`/`AnyContract`, `Policy`, `egressAllowed`, `KernelError`, `Clock`, `Cost`.
- Produces: `FetchLike`, `LlmProvider`, `LlmRequest`, `LlmResponse`, `Providers`, `nodeFetch`; `fakeFetch(routes)`, `fakeLlm(respond, opts)`; `AgentContext` (`clock`, `signal`, optional `http.getText(url)`, optional `llm.complete({system, prompt})`, `spent()`), `issueContext(args)`; `Agent<I,O>`, `AnyAgent`, `defineAgent(contract, run)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`tests/kernel/broker.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { issueContext } from '../../src/kernel/broker.js';
import { fixedClock } from '../../src/kernel/clock.js';
import { defineContract } from '../../src/kernel/contract.js';
import { KernelError } from '../../src/kernel/errors.js';
import { fakeFetch, fakeLlm } from '../../src/kernel/fakes.js';
import type { FetchLike, LlmProvider } from '../../src/kernel/providers.js';
import { PRODUCT_POLICY_V1 } from '../../src/kernel/policy.js';
import { defineType } from '../../src/kernel/types.js';

const T = defineType('Blob', 1, z.object({ s: z.string() }).strict());
const common = {
  version: '1.0.0',
  skills: ['testing'],
  team: 'demo',
  consumes: T,
  produces: T,
  dataClasses: { in: ['P0' as const, 'P1' as const], out: ['P0' as const, 'P1' as const] },
  timeoutMs: 1000,
};
const fetcher = defineContract({
  ...common,
  id: 'fetcher',
  description: 'Fetches a page from one host',
  kind: 'io',
  capabilities: { http: true },
  egress: ['boards-api.greenhouse.io'],
  budget: { usdPerCall: 0, usdPerRun: 0 },
});
const thinker = defineContract({
  ...common,
  id: 'thinker',
  description: 'Asks the model one question',
  kind: 'llm',
  capabilities: { llm: { model: 'claude-sonnet-5-5', maxTokens: 100 } },
  egress: ['api.anthropic.com'],
  budget: { usdPerCall: 0.01, usdPerRun: 1 },
});
const plain = defineContract({ ...common, id: 'plain', description: 'Pure transformation only', kind: 'deterministic', budget: { usdPerCall: 0, usdPerRun: 0 } });

const issue = (contract: typeof fetcher, inputClass: 'P0' | 'P1', providers: Parameters<typeof issueContext>[0]['providers']) =>
  issueContext({ contract, policy: PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, inputClass, providers, clock: fixedClock('2026-10-08T00:00:00Z'), signal: new AbortController().signal });

describe('capability broker', () => {
  it('gives a deterministic agent no tools at all', () => {
    const ctx = issue(plain, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}) });
    expect(ctx.http).toBeUndefined();
    expect(ctx.llm).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('lets an io agent reach only its declared hosts (G-EGRESS)', async () => {
    const f = fakeFetch({ 'https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/x/jobs': { body: '{}' } });
    const ctx = issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: f });
    await expect(ctx.http!.getText('https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/x/jobs')).resolves.toEqual({ status: 200, body: '{}' });
    await expect(ctx.http!.getText('https://evil.example/x')).rejects.toThrow(/G-EGRESS/);
    await expect(ctx.http!.getText('http://boards-api.greenhouse.io/x')).rejects.toThrow(/only https/);
    expect(f.calls).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('maps 429 and 5xx to retryable codes', async () => {
    const f = fakeFetch({
      'https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/a': { status: 429, body: '' },
      'https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/b': { status: 503, body: '' },
    });
    const ctx = issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: f });
    await expect(ctx.http!.getText('https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/a')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'rate_limit' });
    await expect(ctx.http!.getText('https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/b')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'overloaded' });
  });

  it('refuses to send P1 data to the model provider (G-PII-EGRESS)', async () => {
    const llm = fakeLlm(() => 'ok');
    const ctx = issue(thinker, 'P1', { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm });
    await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toThrow(/G-PII-EGRESS/);
    expect(llm.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('meters spend and enforces the per-call budget (G-BUDGET)', async () => {
    const ctx = issue(thinker, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm: fakeLlm(() => 'yes', { usdPerCall: 0.002 }) });
    await ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'hello' });
    expect(ctx.spent()).toEqual({ usd: 0.002, inputTokens: 5, outputTokens: 3 });

    const pricey = issue(thinker, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm: fakeLlm(() => 'yes', { usdPerCall: 0.5 }) });
    const err = await pricey.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' }).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(KernelError);
    expect((err as KernelError).code).toBe('budget_exceeded');
    expect(pricey.spent().usd).toBe(0.5);
  });

  it('passes the contract model and token cap, not the agent choice', async () => {
    const llm = fakeLlm(() => 'ok');
    const ctx = issue(thinker, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm });
    await ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' });
    expect(llm.calls[0]).toMatchObject({ model: 'claude-sonnet-5-5', maxTokens: 100 });
  });
  describe('hardening', () => {
    const H = 'https://boards-api.greenhouse.io';
    const thrown = (p: Promise<unknown>) => p.catch((e: unknown) => e);
    const stubLlm = (fail: unknown, usd = 0.001): LlmProvider & { n: number } => {
      const p = {
        host: 'api.anthropic.com',
        n: 0,
        async complete() {
          p.n++;
          if (fail !== undefined) throw fail;
          return { text: 'x', inputTokens: 1, outputTokens: 1, usd };
        },
      };
      return p;
    };
    const capped = defineContract({
      ...common,
      id: 'capped',
      description: 'Asks the model under a run cap',
      kind: 'llm',
      capabilities: { llm: { model: 'claude-sonnet-5-5', maxTokens: 100 } },
      egress: ['api.anthropic.com'],
      budget: { usdPerCall: 0.002, usdPerRun: 0.003 },
    });

    it.each([301, 302])('refuses a %i redirect and never follows it', async (status) => {
      const f = fakeFetch({ [`${H}/r`]: { status, body: '' } });
      const err = await thrown(issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: f }).http!.getText(`${H}/r`));
      expect(err).toMatchObject({ code: 'policy_denied' });
      expect((err as Error).message).toMatch(/redirect/);
      expect(f.calls).toHaveLength(1);
    });

    it('always passes redirect: manual to fetch', async () => {
      const inits: unknown[] = [];
      const f: FetchLike = async (_u, init) => {
        inits.push(init);
        return { status: 200, text: async () => 'ok' };
      };
      await issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: f }).http!.getText(`${H}/x`);
      expect(inits[0]).toMatchObject({ redirect: 'manual' });
    });

    it('rejects explicit ports and unparseable URLs', async () => {
      const f = fakeFetch({});
      const ctx = issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: f });
      await expect(ctx.http!.getText(`${H}:8443/x`)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'policy_denied', message: expect.stringMatching(/port/) });
      await expect(ctx.http!.getText('not a url')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'policy_denied', message: expect.stringMatching(/invalid URL/) });
      expect(f.calls).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('judges the real host, and URL parsing lowercases it', async () => {
      const f = fakeFetch({ [`${H}/x`]: { body: 'ok' } });
      const ctx = issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: f });
      await expect(ctx.http!.getText('https://BOARDS-API.greenhouse.io/x')).resolves.toMatchObject({ status: 200 });
      await expect(ctx.http!.getText('https://boards-api.greenhouse.io@evil.example/x')).rejects.toThrow(/G-EGRESS/);
    });

    it('refuses credentials in URLs before any fetch', async () => {
      const f = fakeFetch({});
      const ctx = issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: f });
      for (const url of ['https://evil.example@boards-api.greenhouse.io/x', 'https://u:p@boards-api.greenhouse.io/x', 'https://:p@boards-api.greenhouse.io/x']) {
        await expect(ctx.http!.getText(url)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'policy_denied', message: 'G-EGRESS: credentials in URLs are not allowed' });
      }
      expect(f.calls).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('maps an external abort to unknown and a body read failure to network', async () => {
      const aborted: FetchLike = async () => {
        throw { name: 'AbortError' };
      };
      const badBody: FetchLike = async () => ({
        status: 200,
        text: async () => {
          throw new Error('socket hang up');
        },
      });
      expect(await thrown(issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: aborted }).http!.getText(`${H}/x`))).toMatchObject({ code: 'unknown' });
      expect(await thrown(issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: badBody }).http!.getText(`${H}/x`))).toMatchObject({ code: 'network' });
    });

    it('maps provider status failures to retryable codes', async () => {
      const a = issue(thinker, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm: stubLlm({ status: 429 }) });
      const b = issue(thinker, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm: stubLlm({ status: 529 }) });
      expect(await thrown(a.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' }))).toMatchObject({ code: 'rate_limit' });
      expect(await thrown(b.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' }))).toMatchObject({ code: 'overloaded' });
    });

    it('blocks every call after one exceeded usdPerCall, without calling the provider', async () => {
      const llm = stubLlm(undefined, 0.5);
      const ctx = issue(thinker, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm });
      await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'budget_exceeded' });
      await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toThrow(/previous call exceeded usdPerCall/);
      expect(llm.n).toBe(1);
    });

    it('refuses a call once cumulative usd plus a reserved usdPerCall would pass usdPerRun', async () => {
      const llm = stubLlm(undefined, 0.002);
      const ctx = issue(capped, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm });
      await ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' });
      expect(ctx.spent().usd).toBeCloseTo(0.002);
      await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toThrow(/usdPerRun reached/);
      expect(llm.n).toBe(1);
    });

    it('allows one model call in flight per step', async () => {
      let n = 0;
      const slow: LlmProvider = {
        host: 'api.anthropic.com',
        async complete() {
          n++;
          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));
          return { text: 'x', inputTokens: 1, outputTokens: 1, usd: 0.001 };
        },
      };
      const ctx = issue(thinker, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm: slow });
      const rs = await Promise.allSettled([1, 2, 3].map(() => ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })));
      expect(n).toBe(1);
      expect(rs.filter((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled')).toHaveLength(1);
      const rejected = rs.filter((r): r is PromiseRejectedResult => r.status === 'rejected');
      expect(rejected).toHaveLength(2);
      for (const r of rejected) expect(r.reason).toMatchObject({ code: 'policy_denied', message: 'G-BUDGET: one model call at a time per step' });
      await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).resolves.toBeDefined();
    });

    it('does not echo the raw URL in the port error', async () => {
      const err = await thrown(issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}) }).http!.getText('https://u:secret@boards-api.greenhouse.io:8443/x'));
      expect((err as Error).message).toMatch(/port/);
      expect((err as Error).message).not.toMatch(/secret/);
    });

    it('refuses new model calls once the signal has aborted, and keeps refusing', async () => {
      const llm = fakeLlm(() => 'ok');
      const ac = new AbortController();
      const ctx = issueContext({ contract: thinker, policy: PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, inputClass: 'P0', providers: { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm }, clock: fixedClock('2026-10-08T00:00:00Z'), signal: ac.signal });
      ac.abort();
      await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'timeout' });
      await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'timeout' });
      expect(llm.calls).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('refuses new requests once the signal has aborted', async () => {
      const f = fakeFetch({ 'https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/x': { body: '{}' } });
      const ac = new AbortController();
      const ctx = issueContext({ contract: fetcher, policy: PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, inputClass: 'P0', providers: { fetch: f }, clock: fixedClock('2026-10-08T00:00:00Z'), signal: ac.signal });
      ac.abort();
      await expect(ctx.http!.getText('https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/x')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'timeout' });
      expect(f.calls).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('fails with policy_denied when no LLM provider is configured', async () => {
      const ctx = issue(thinker, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}) });
      expect(ctx.llm).toBeDefined();
      await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'policy_denied' });
    });
  });
});

describe('capability broker: prior run spend (G-BUDGET)', () => {
  const issueWith = (priorSpend: { agentUsd: number; runUsd: number; maxCallUsd?: number }) => {
    const llm = fakeLlm(() => 'yes', { usdPerCall: 0.001 });
    const ctx = issueContext({
      contract: thinker, policy: PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, inputClass: 'P0', providers: { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm },
      clock: fixedClock('2026-10-08T00:00:00Z'), signal: new AbortController().signal, priorSpend,
    });
    return { ctx, llm };
  };

  it('refuses a call once the agent has spent its usdPerRun earlier in the run', async () => {
    const { ctx, llm } = issueWith({ agentUsd: 1, runUsd: 1 });
    await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'budget_exceeded', message: 'G-BUDGET: run budget reached' });
    expect(llm.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('refuses a call once the run has reached the policy cap', async () => {
    const { ctx, llm } = issueWith({ agentUsd: 0, runUsd: PRODUCT_POLICY_V1.maxUsdPerRun });
    await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'budget_exceeded' });
    expect(llm.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('reserves usdPerCall: refuses a call that could carry the agent past usdPerRun', async () => {
    const { ctx, llm } = issueWith({ agentUsd: 1 - 0.005, runUsd: 0 });
    await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'budget_exceeded', message: 'G-BUDGET: run budget reached' });
    expect(llm.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('reserves usdPerCall: refuses a call that could carry the run past the policy cap', async () => {
    const { ctx, llm } = issueWith({ agentUsd: 0, runUsd: PRODUCT_POLICY_V1.maxUsdPerRun - 0.005 });
    await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'budget_exceeded' });
    expect(llm.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('allows a call whose reservation fits exactly', async () => {
    const { ctx, llm } = issueWith({ agentUsd: 1 - 0.01, runUsd: 0 });
    await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).resolves.toBeDefined();
    expect(llm.calls).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('reserves the largest call already charged in the run when it is above usdPerCall', async () => {
    const { ctx, llm } = issueWith({ agentUsd: 0.7, runUsd: 0, maxCallUsd: 0.4 });
    await expect(ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'budget_exceeded', message: 'G-BUDGET: run budget reached' });
    expect(llm.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('reports the largest single call it paid', async () => {
    const { ctx } = issueWith({ agentUsd: 0, runUsd: 0 });
    expect(ctx.maxCall()).toBe(0);
    await ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' });
    expect(ctx.maxCall()).toBeCloseTo(0.001, 10);
  });
});

describe('capability broker: denials are recorded', () => {
  const H = 'https://boards-api.greenhouse.io';
  const swallow = (p: Promise<unknown>) => p.catch(() => undefined);

  it('records every policy denial, even when the agent catches it', async () => {
    const f = fakeFetch({ [`${H}/r`]: { status: 302, body: '' } });
    const ctx = issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: f });
    expect(ctx.denials()).toEqual([]);
    await swallow(ctx.http!.getText('https://evil.example/x'));
    await swallow(ctx.http!.getText('http://boards-api.greenhouse.io/x'));
    await swallow(ctx.http!.getText(`https://boards-api.greenhouse.io:8443/x`));
    await swallow(ctx.http!.getText('https://u:p@boards-api.greenhouse.io/x'));
    await swallow(ctx.http!.getText('not a url'));
    await swallow(ctx.http!.getText(`${H}/r`));
    expect(ctx.denials()).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(ctx.denials()[0]).toMatch(/^G-EGRESS: fetcher may not contact evil\.example/);
    expect(ctx.denials()[5]).toMatch(/redirect/);
  });

  it('records PII egress and a missing provider', async () => {
    const pii = issue(thinker, 'P1', { fetch: fakeFetch({}), llm: fakeLlm(() => 'ok') });
    await swallow(pii.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' }));
    expect(pii.denials()).toEqual([expect.stringMatching(/^G-PII-EGRESS/)]);
    const none = issue(thinker, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}) });
    await swallow(none.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' }));
    expect(none.denials()).toEqual(['no LLM provider is configured']);
  });

  it('does not record ordinary failures', async () => {
    const f = fakeFetch({ [`${H}/a`]: { status: 503, body: '' } });
    const ctx = issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: f });
    await swallow(ctx.http!.getText(`${H}/a`));
    expect(ctx.denials()).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('capability broker: error messages', () => {
  it('the only-https denial names the scheme and host, never the path, query or credentials', async () => {
    const ctx = issue(fetcher, 'P0', { fetch: fakeFetch({}) });
    const err = (await ctx.http!.getText('http://boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/secret-path?token=abc123').catch((e: unknown) => e)) as Error;
    expect(err.message).toBe('G-EGRESS: only https is allowed (http://boards-api.greenhouse.io)');
    expect(ctx.denials()).toEqual([err.message]);
  });
});
```

`src/kernel/providers.ts`:

```ts
export interface FetchResponse {
  readonly status: number;
  text(): Promise<string>;
}

export type FetchLike = (url: string, init: { signal: AbortSignal; redirect: 'manual' }) => Promise<FetchResponse>;

export interface LlmRequest {
  readonly model: string;
  readonly system: string;
  readonly prompt: string;
  readonly maxTokens: number;
}

export interface LlmResponse {
  readonly text: string;
  readonly inputTokens: number;
  readonly outputTokens: number;
  readonly usd: number;
}

export interface LlmProvider {
  /** Host the provider talks to; checked against contract egress and data-class policy. */
  readonly host: string;
  complete(req: LlmRequest, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<LlmResponse>;
}

/** The only doors to the outside world. Agents never see these directly, only broker tools. */
export interface Providers {
  readonly fetch: FetchLike;
  readonly llm?: LlmProvider;
}

export const nodeFetch: FetchLike = (url, init) => fetch(url, { signal: init.signal, redirect: init.redirect });
```

`src/kernel/fakes.ts`:

```ts
import type { FetchLike, LlmProvider, LlmRequest } from './providers.js';

export function fakeFetch(routes: Record<string, { status?: number; body: string }>): FetchLike & { calls: string[] } {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  const f = async (url: string) => {
    calls.push(url);
    const r = routes[url];
    if (!r) return { status: 404, text: async () => 'not found' };
    return { status: r.status ?? 200, text: async () => r.body };
  };
  return Object.assign(f, { calls });
}

/** A deterministic stand-in for a model: same request, same answer, fixed cost. */
export function fakeLlm(
  respond: (req: LlmRequest) => string,
  opts: { usdPerCall?: number; host?: string } = {},
): LlmProvider & { calls: LlmRequest[] } {
  const calls: LlmRequest[] = [];
  return {
    host: opts.host ?? 'api.anthropic.com',
    calls,
    async complete(req) {
      calls.push(req);
      const text = respond(req);
      return { text, inputTokens: req.prompt.length, outputTokens: text.length, usd: opts.usdPerCall ?? 0.001 };
    },
  };
}
```

`src/kernel/broker.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock } from './clock.js';
import type { AnyContract } from './contract.js';
import type { DataClass } from './data-class.js';
import type { Cost } from './envelope.js';
import { KernelError } from './errors.js';
import { egressAllowed, type Policy } from './policy.js';
import type { LlmResponse, Providers } from './providers.js';

export interface HttpTool {
  getText(url: string): Promise<{ status: number; body: string }>;
}

export interface LlmTool {
  complete(req: { system: string; prompt: string }): Promise<LlmResponse>;
}

/**
 * Everything an agent may touch. Tools exist only when the contract grants
 * them. There is deliberately no proxy, user-agent, fingerprint or CAPTCHA
 * capability: those are absent, not disabled (G-NO-EVASION).
 */
export interface AgentContext {
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly signal: AbortSignal;
  readonly http?: HttpTool;
  readonly llm?: LlmTool;
  spent(): Cost;
  /** The largest single model call paid in this context. */
  maxCall(): number;
  /** Every policy denial the broker raised in this context, in order, whether or not the agent caught it. */
  denials(): readonly string[];
}

export interface IssueArgs {
  readonly contract: AnyContract;
  readonly policy: Policy;
  readonly inputClass: DataClass;
  readonly providers: Providers;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly signal: AbortSignal;
  /** What the run had already paid before this context was issued: by this agent, and in total. */
  readonly priorSpend?: { readonly agentUsd: number; readonly runUsd: number; readonly maxCallUsd?: number };
}

function mapProviderError(e: unknown): unknown {
  if (e instanceof KernelError) return e;
  const o = typeof e === 'object' && e !== null ? (e as { name?: unknown; status?: unknown; message?: unknown }) : {};
  if (o.name === 'TimeoutError') return e;
  if (typeof o.status === 'number') {
    if (o.status === 429) return new KernelError('rate_limit', `provider returned ${o.status}`);
    if (o.status >= 500) return new KernelError('overloaded', `provider returned ${o.status}`);
  }
  if (o.name === 'AbortError') return new KernelError('unknown', 'request aborted');
  return new KernelError('network', typeof o.message === 'string' ? o.message : 'provider call failed');
}

/** Sums of decimal dollar amounts drift (0.1 + 0.2 > 0.3); a cap is only exceeded beyond that noise. */
const exceeds = (total: number, cap: number): boolean => total > cap + 1e-9;

export function issueContext(a: IssueArgs): AgentContext {
  let usd = 0;
  let inputTokens = 0;
  let outputTokens = 0;
  let maxCallUsd = 0;
  const prior = a.priorSpend ?? { agentUsd: 0, runUsd: 0 };
  const denied: string[] = [];
  /** A policy denial is recorded before it is thrown, so an agent cannot hide it by catching. */
  const deny = (message: string): KernelError => {
    denied.push(message);
    return new KernelError('policy_denied', message);
  };

  const checkHost = (host: string): void => {
    if (!a.contract.egress.includes(host)) {
      throw deny(`G-EGRESS: ${a.contract.id} may not contact ${host}`);
    }
    if (!egressAllowed(a.policy, host, a.inputClass)) {
      throw deny(`G-PII-EGRESS: ${a.inputClass} data may not be sent to ${host}`);
    }
  };

  const ctx: { -readonly [K in keyof AgentContext]: AgentContext[K] } = {
    clock: a.clock,
    signal: a.signal,
    spent: () => ({ usd, inputTokens, outputTokens }),
    maxCall: () => maxCallUsd,
    denials: () => [...denied],
  };

  if (a.contract.capabilities.http) {
    ctx.http = {
      async getText(url) {
        let u: URL;
        try {
          u = new URL(url);
        } catch {
          throw deny('G-EGRESS: invalid URL');
        }
        if (u.protocol !== 'https:') throw deny(`G-EGRESS: only https is allowed (${u.protocol}//${u.hostname})`);
        if (u.port !== '') throw deny(`G-EGRESS: explicit ports are not allowed (${u.hostname})`);
        if (u.username !== '' || u.password !== '') throw deny('G-EGRESS: credentials in URLs are not allowed');
        checkHost(u.hostname);
        if (a.signal.aborted) throw new KernelError('timeout', 'step timed out; no new requests');
        let res;
        let body;
        try {
          res = await a.providers.fetch(u.href, { signal: a.signal, redirect: 'manual' });
          if (res.status >= 300 && res.status < 400) {
            throw deny(`G-EGRESS: redirect from ${u.hostname} refused`);
          }
          if (res.status === 429) throw new KernelError('rate_limit', `429 from ${u.hostname}`);
          if (res.status >= 500) throw new KernelError('overloaded', `${res.status} from ${u.hostname}`);
          body = await res.text();
        } catch (e) {
          throw mapProviderError(e);
        }
        return { status: res.status, body };
      },
    };
  }

  const llmCap = a.contract.capabilities.llm;
  const provider = a.providers.llm;
  if (llmCap) {
    let overCall = false;
    let inFlight = false;
    ctx.llm = {
      async complete(req) {
        if (!provider) throw deny('no LLM provider is configured');
        checkHost(provider.host);
        if (inFlight) throw deny('G-BUDGET: one model call at a time per step');
        if (a.signal.aborted) throw new KernelError('timeout', 'step timed out; no new model calls');
        inFlight = true;
        try {
          if (overCall) throw new KernelError('budget_exceeded', 'G-BUDGET: a previous call exceeded usdPerCall');
          // Every call reserves a full usdPerCall (or the largest call this agent has already
          // been charged in the run, if that was more) before it is made, so the caps are hard:
          // a call that costs more than usdPerCall is latched above, everything else fits.
          // Bounds, stated exactly: (a) a call that returns in time and costs more than usdPerCall is charged
          // in full and stops this agent for the rest of the run, so a run can exceed policy.maxUsdPerRun by at
          // most (actual - usdPerCall) once per LLM agent; (b) a call still in flight when the step times out
          // is recorded at its reservation (max of usdPerCall and the largest call seen); what the provider
          // actually bills for it is not visible to the kernel and is bounded only per call by the provider's
          // pricing for maxTokens; (c) spend of a call in flight when the process itself dies is not recorded
          // and the step re-runs on resume.
          const reserve = Math.max(a.contract.budget.usdPerCall, prior.maxCallUsd ?? 0);
          if (exceeds(usd + reserve, a.contract.budget.usdPerRun)) {
            throw new KernelError('budget_exceeded', 'G-BUDGET: usdPerRun reached in this step');
          }
          if (exceeds(prior.agentUsd + usd + reserve, a.contract.budget.usdPerRun) || exceeds(prior.runUsd + usd + reserve, a.policy.maxUsdPerRun)) {
            throw new KernelError('budget_exceeded', 'G-BUDGET: run budget reached');
          }
          // A call still in flight when the step times out is charged its reservation at once, so the
          // executor (which reads spent() right after the abort) records it. Whatever the provider answers
          // later is discarded, never added a second time. Bound (b) above: accounting for a timed-out call is the
          // reservation, never zero, even though the provider may bill more for it.
          let chargedOnAbort = false;
          const chargeOnAbort = (): void => {
            chargedOnAbort = true;
            usd += reserve;
            maxCallUsd = Math.max(maxCallUsd, reserve);
          };
          a.signal.addEventListener('abort', chargeOnAbort, { once: true });
          let res;
          try {
            res = await provider.complete({ ...req, model: llmCap.model, maxTokens: llmCap.maxTokens }, a.signal);
          } catch (e) {
            // A failed provider call reports no usage, so spend is left unchanged (unless it was charged on abort).
            throw mapProviderError(e);
          } finally {
            a.signal.removeEventListener('abort', chargeOnAbort);
          }
          if (chargedOnAbort) throw new KernelError('timeout', 'step timed out; the late model answer is discarded');
          usd += res.usd;
          inputTokens += res.inputTokens;
          outputTokens += res.outputTokens;
          maxCallUsd = Math.max(maxCallUsd, res.usd);
          if (res.usd > a.contract.budget.usdPerCall) {
            overCall = true;
            throw new KernelError('budget_exceeded', `G-BUDGET: call cost ${res.usd} > usdPerCall ${a.contract.budget.usdPerCall}`);
          }
          return res;
        } finally {
          inFlight = false;
        }
      },
    };
  }
  return ctx;
}
```

`src/kernel/agent.ts`:

```ts
import type { AgentContext } from './broker.js';
import type { Contract } from './contract.js';

export interface Agent<I = unknown, O = unknown> {
  readonly contract: Contract<I, O>;
  /** Return outputs in a deterministic order; the index is part of each envelope id. */
  run(input: I, ctx: AgentContext): Promise<readonly O[]>;
}

/** Registries hold agents of many payload types. */
export type AnyAgent = Agent<any, any>;

export function defineAgent<I, O>(contract: Contract<I, O>, run: (input: I, ctx: AgentContext) => Promise<readonly O[]>): Agent<I, O> {
  return Object.freeze({ contract, run });
}
```
