`tests/kernel/supervisor.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { defineAgent } from '../../src/kernel/agent.js';
import { fixedClock } from '../../src/kernel/clock.js';
import { defineContract } from '../../src/kernel/contract.js';
import { KernelError } from '../../src/kernel/errors.js';
import { fakeLlm } from '../../src/kernel/fakes.js';
import { builtinGuardrails } from '../../src/kernel/guardrails/builtin.js';
import { mergePolicy, policyHash, PRODUCT_POLICY_V1 } from '../../src/kernel/policy.js';
import type { LlmProvider } from '../../src/kernel/providers.js';
import { Registry } from '../../src/kernel/registry.js';
import { verifyChain } from '../../src/kernel/store/audit.js';
import { Store } from '../../src/kernel/store/store.js';
import { StateMachine } from '../../src/kernel/state-machine.js';
import { Supervisor, type SupervisorDeps, type Tracker } from '../../src/kernel/supervisor.js';
import { compileWiring, type Pipeline } from '../../src/kernel/wiring.js';
import { demoScorer, demoScorerContract, demoSource, demoSourceContract } from '../../src/demo/agents.js';
import { demoKernel } from '../../src/demo/kernel.js';
import { demoLlm, demoPipeline, demoTracker } from '../../src/demo/pipeline.js';

const seed = { count: 5, prefix: 'demo' };
const invocations = () => {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  return { calls, onInvoke: (agent: string, stepKey: string) => calls.push(`${agent}:${stepKey}`) };
};

describe('supervisor end to end', () => {
  it('runs the demo pipeline: 1 source step, 5 scorer steps, every obligation discharged', async () => {
    const k = demoKernel();
    const r = await k.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toEqual({ pending: 0, done: 6, failed: 0, quarantined: 0, skipped: 0 });
    expect(r.openObligations).toEqual([]);
    expect(r.costUsd).toBeCloseTo(0.005);
    expect(k.store.entityState('demo-003')).toBe('SCORED');
    expect(verifyChain(k.store)).toMatchObject({ ok: true });
  });

  it('is deterministic: two fresh databases end byte-identical', async () => {
    const a = demoKernel();
    const b = demoKernel();
    await a.supervisor.start('run-a', [seed]);
    await b.supervisor.start('run-b', [seed]);
    expect(a.store.dump('envelopes', 'envelope_id')).toBe(b.store.dump('envelopes', 'envelope_id'));
    expect(a.store.dump('entities', 'kind, entity_id')).toBe(b.store.dump('entities', 'kind, entity_id'));
    expect(a.store.dump('steps', 'step_key')).toBe(b.store.dump('steps', 'step_key'));
  });

  it('replays: a second run on the same database invokes no agent and pays nothing', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    await demoKernel({ store }).supervisor.start('run-1', [seed]);
    const before = store.dump('envelopes', 'envelope_id');
    const inv = invocations();
    const r = await demoKernel({ store, onInvoke: inv.onInvoke }).supervisor.start('run-2', [seed]);
    expect(inv.calls).toEqual([]);
    expect(r.replayed).toBe(6);
    expect(r.costUsd).toBe(0);
    expect(store.dump('envelopes', 'envelope_id')).toBe(before);
  });

  it('survives a crash between a step and its routing: resume runs no finished step twice', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const inv = invocations();
    const crashing = demoKernel({ store, onInvoke: inv.onInvoke, afterStep: (n) => { if (n === 3) throw new Error('simulated crash'); } });
    await expect(crashing.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed])).rejects.toThrow('simulated crash');
    const r = await demoKernel({ store, onInvoke: inv.onInvoke }).supervisor.resume('run-1');
    expect(r.queue.done).toBe(6);
    expect(new Set(inv.calls).size).toBe(inv.calls.length);
    const clean = demoKernel();
    await clean.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(store.dump('envelopes', 'envelope_id')).toBe(clean.store.dump('envelopes', 'envelope_id'));
    expect(store.dump('entities', 'kind, entity_id')).toBe(clean.store.dump('entities', 'kind, entity_id'));
  });

  it('quarantines outputs that break a guarantee and moves the entity to QUARANTINED', async () => {
    const k = demoKernel({ llm: fakeLlm(() => JSON.stringify({ score: 99, reason: 'too keen' })) });
    const r = await k.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 1, quarantined: 5 });
    expect(k.store.entityState('demo-001')).toBe('QUARANTINED');
    const trip = k.store.events('run-1').find((e) => e.kind === 'step_quarantined');
    expect(JSON.parse(trip!.data).verdict).toMatchObject({ id: 'G-POST', verdict: 'trip' });
  });

  it('opens the agent breaker after 3 consecutive failures and skips the rest', async () => {
    let calls = 0;
    const down = { host: 'api.anthropic.com', async complete() { calls += 1; throw new KernelError('overloaded', '529'); } };
    const k = demoKernel({ llm: down });
    const r = await k.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 1, failed: 3, skipped: 2 });
    expect(calls).toBe(9);
    expect(k.store.events('run-1').some((e) => e.kind === 'breaker_open')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('reports the open obligations of an input whose step failed', async () => {
    const k = demoKernel({ llm: fakeLlm(() => 'not json') });
    const r = await k.supervisor.start('run-1', [{ count: 2, prefix: 'demo' }]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ failed: 2 });
    expect(r.openObligations).toHaveLength(2);
    for (const o of r.openObligations) {
      expect(o.ids).toEqual(['demo.scored']);
      expect(k.store.getEnvelope(o.envelopeId).type).toBe('DemoItem@1');
    }
  });

  it('reports the open obligations of inputs skipped by an open breaker', async () => {
    const down = { host: 'api.anthropic.com', async complete() { throw new KernelError('overloaded', '529'); } };
    const r = await demoKernel({ llm: down }).supervisor.start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ failed: 3, skipped: 2 });
    expect(r.openObligations).toHaveLength(5);
  });

  it('gives seed envelopes the class the pipeline declares, so a P0-only source refuses a P1 seed at the door', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const inv = invocations();
    const p1: Pipeline = { ...demoPipeline, seed: { ...demoPipeline.seed, dataClass: 'P1' } };
    const r = await handBuilt({ store, pipeline: p1, scorer: demoScorer, onInvoke: inv.onInvoke }).start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 0, quarantined: 1 });
    expect(inv.calls).toEqual([]);
    const trip = store.events('run-1').find((e) => e.kind === 'step_quarantined');
    expect(JSON.parse(trip!.data).verdict).toMatchObject({ id: 'G-DATACLASS', verdict: 'trip' });
  });

  it('does not retry invalid model output', async () => {
    const llm = fakeLlm(() => 'not json');
    const r = await demoKernel({ llm }).supervisor.start('run-1', [{ count: 2, prefix: 'demo' }]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ failed: 2 });
    expect(llm.calls).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('stops paying once the run budget is spent, and lets the rest wait', async () => {
    const r = await demoKernel({ llm: demoLlm(0.01), userPolicy: { maxUsdPerRun: 0.03 } }).supervisor.start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 4, skipped: 2 });
    expect(r.costUsd).toBeCloseTo(0.03);
  });

  it('refuses an invalid seed before anything runs', async () => {
    const k = demoKernel();
    await expect(k.supervisor.start('run-1', [{ count: 0, prefix: 'demo' }])).rejects.toThrow(/not a valid DemoRequest@1/);
    await expect(k.supervisor.start('run-2', [{ count: 1, prefix: 'demo', extra: 1 }])).rejects.toThrow(/not a valid/);
  });

  it('refuses to resume a run under a different policy', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const crashing = demoKernel({ store, afterStep: (n) => { if (n === 2) throw new Error('simulated crash'); } });
    await expect(crashing.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed])).rejects.toThrow('simulated crash');
    await expect(demoKernel({ store, userPolicy: { maxUsdPerRun: 1 } }).supervisor.resume('run-1')).rejects.toThrow(/different policy/);
  });

  it('survives the same entity arriving twice: the duplicate is audited, the run completes', async () => {
    const k = demoKernel();
    const r = await k.supervisor.start('run-1', [{ count: 2, prefix: 'demo' }, { count: 3, prefix: 'demo' }]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ pending: 0, failed: 0 });
    const rejected = k.store.events('run-1').filter((e) => e.kind === 'transition_rejected').map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data).entity as string);
    expect(rejected).toContain('demo-001');
    expect(rejected).toContain('demo-002');
    expect(k.store.entityState('demo-003')).toBe('SCORED');
  });

  it('keeps open obligations across a resume when the discharger finishes with no outputs', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const crashing = handBuilt({ store, afterStep: (n) => { if (n === 3) throw new Error('simulated crash'); } });
    await expect(crashing.start('run-1', [seed])).rejects.toThrow('simulated crash');
    const r = await handBuilt({ store }).resume('run-1');
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 6, failed: 0, quarantined: 0 });
    for (const id of ['demo-001', 'demo-002', 'demo-003', 'demo-004', 'demo-005']) expect(store.entityState(id)).toBe('DISCOVERED');
    expect(r.openObligations).toHaveLength(5);
    for (const o of r.openObligations) {
      expect(o.ids).toEqual(['demo.scored']);
      expect(store.getEnvelope(o.envelopeId).type).toBe('DemoItem@1');
    }
    expect([...r.openObligations].map((o) => o.envelopeId)).toEqual([...r.openObligations].map((o) => o.envelopeId).sort());
  });

  it('reports the open obligations of inputs whose step is done with zero outputs', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const r = await handBuilt({ store }).start('run-1', [{ count: 3, prefix: 'demo' }]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 4, failed: 0 });
    expect(r.openObligations).toHaveLength(3);
    for (const o of r.openObligations) {
      expect(o.ids).toEqual(['demo.scored']);
      expect(store.getEnvelope(o.envelopeId).type).toBe('DemoItem@1');
    }
    expect(store.entityState('demo-001')).toBe('DISCOVERED');
  });

  it('still blocks the success state while an obligation is open', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const early: Tracker = {
      kind: 'demo-early',
      machine: new StateMachine({ id: 'early', initial: 'NEW', terminal: ['SCORED'], success: ['SCORED'], transitions: [{ from: 'NEW', on: 'scored', to: 'SCORED' }] }),
      keyOf: (env) => (env.type === 'DemoItem@1' ? `early-${(env.payload as { id: string }).id}` : null),
      eventFor: (agentId, outcome) => (outcome === 'done' && agentId === 'demo-source' ? 'scored' : null),
    };
    await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer, trackers: [demoTracker, early] }).start('run-1', [{ count: 2, prefix: 'demo' }]);
    const blocked = store.events('run-1').filter((e) => e.kind === 'obligation_blocked').map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data) as { entity: string; ids: string[] });
    expect(blocked.map((b) => b.entity)).toEqual(['early-demo-001', 'early-demo-002']);
    expect(blocked.every((b) => b.ids.join() === 'demo.scored')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('does not carry one run\'s obligations or replay count into the next run on the same instance', async () => {
    const sup = handBuilt({ store: Store.open(':memory:') });
    const r1 = await sup.start('run-1', [{ count: 2, prefix: 'a' }]);
    expect(r1.openObligations).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(r1.replayed).toBe(0);
    const r2 = await sup.start('run-2', [{ count: 2, prefix: 'a' }]);
    expect(r2.replayed).toBe(3);
    expect(r2.openObligations).toEqual(r1.openObligations);
    const r3 = await sup.start('run-3', [{ count: 1, prefix: 'b' }]);
    expect(r3.replayed).toBe(0);
    expect(r3.openObligations).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(r1.openObligations.map((o) => o.envelopeId)).not.toContain(r3.openObligations[0]!.envelopeId);
  });

  it('survives a crash right after the source step, before its fan-out is routed', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const inv = invocations();
    const crashing = demoKernel({ store, onInvoke: inv.onInvoke, afterStep: (n) => { if (n === 1) throw new Error('simulated crash'); } });
    await expect(crashing.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed])).rejects.toThrow('simulated crash');
    const r = await demoKernel({ store, onInvoke: inv.onInvoke }).supervisor.resume('run-1');
    expect(r.queue.done).toBe(6);
    expect(new Set(inv.calls).size).toBe(inv.calls.length);
    expect(r.replayed).toBe(1);
  });

  it('guards the run lifecycle', async () => {
    const k = demoKernel();
    await k.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed]);
    await expect(k.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed])).rejects.toThrow(/already exists/);
    await expect(k.supervisor.resume('run-1')).rejects.toThrow(/already completed/);
    await expect(k.supervisor.start('run-x', [{ count: 0, prefix: 'demo' }])).rejects.toThrow(/not a valid/);
    expect(k.store.getRun('run-x')).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('opens the team breaker before the agent breaker when the team is stricter', async () => {
    const down = { host: 'api.anthropic.com', async complete() { throw new KernelError('overloaded', '529'); } };
    const strict: Pipeline = { ...demoPipeline, teams: demoPipeline.teams.map((t) => (t.id === 'evaluation' ? { ...t, breaker: { consecutiveFailures: 2 } } : t)) };
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const r = await handBuilt({ store, llm: down, pipeline: strict, scorer: demoScorer }).start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 1, failed: 2, skipped: 3 });
    const opened = store.events('run-1').filter((e) => e.kind === 'breaker_open').map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data).key as string);
    expect(opened).toContain('team:evaluation');
  });

  it('replays across a minor version bump of the source (G-LINEAGE remembers past versions of the same major)', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer }).start('run-1', [seed]);
    const minor = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSourceContract, version: '1.1.0' }), demoSource.run);
    const inv = invocations();
    const r = await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer, source: minor, onInvoke: inv.onInvoke }).start('run-2', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ pending: 0, done: 6, quarantined: 0, failed: 0 });
    expect(inv.calls).toEqual([]);
    expect(store.events('run-2').filter((e) => e.kind === 'step_replayed')).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(store.entityState('demo-003')).toBe('SCORED');
  });

  it('does not trust past outputs of a minor version whose security-relevant fields differ (fail closed)', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer }).start('run-1', [seed]);
    const minor = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSourceContract, version: '1.1.0', dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0', 'P1'] }, emits: 'P1' }), demoSource.run);
    const inv = invocations();
    const r = await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer, source: minor, onInvoke: inv.onInvoke }).start('run-2', [seed]);
    expect(inv.calls.filter((c) => c.startsWith('demo-scorer:'))).toEqual([]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 1, quarantined: 5 });
    const trips = store.events('run-2').filter((e) => e.kind === 'step_quarantined').map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data) as { agent: string; verdict: { id: string } });
    expect(trips.every((t) => t.agent === 'demo-scorer' && t.verdict.id === 'G-LINEAGE')).toBe(true);
    expect(store.contracts().find((c) => c.version === '1.0.0' && c.agent_id === 'demo-source')?.shape).toMatchObject({ version: '1.0.0', kind: 'deterministic' });
  });

  it('re-executes the source after a major bump and still completes without quarantine', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer }).start('run-1', [seed]);
    const major = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSourceContract, version: '2.0.0' }), demoSource.run);
    const inv = invocations();
    const r = await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer, source: major, onInvoke: inv.onInvoke }).start('run-2', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ pending: 0, done: 6, quarantined: 0, failed: 0 });
    expect(inv.calls.map((c) => c.split(':')[0])).toEqual(['demo-source']);
    expect(store.entityState('demo-003')).toBe('SCORED');
  });

  it('releases quarantined steps: a new run with a good model scores every item', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const bad = demoKernel({ store, llm: fakeLlm(() => JSON.stringify({ score: 99, reason: 'too keen' })) });
    await bad.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed]);
    const q = store.quarantinedSteps();
    expect(q).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(q.every((s) => s.agent_id === 'demo-scorer' && (s.detail ?? '').includes('G-POST'))).toBe(true);
    expect(store.entityState('demo-001')).toBe('QUARANTINED');
    for (const s of q) {
      expect(store.releaseQuarantine(s.step_key, 'release-x', '2026-10-08T08:00:00.000Z', bad.policyHash)).toEqual({ released: true, entities: [{ kind: 'demo-item', entityId: expect.any(String) }], skipped: [] });
    }
    expect(store.quarantinedSteps()).toEqual([]);
    expect(store.entityState('demo-001')).toBe('DISCOVERED');
    expect(store.events('release-x').map((e) => e.kind)).toEqual(Array(5).fill('quarantine_released'));
    const good = demoLlm();
    const r = await demoKernel({ store, llm: good }).supervisor.start('run-2', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 6, quarantined: 0 });
    expect(good.calls).toHaveLength(5);
    for (const id of ['demo-001', 'demo-002', 'demo-003', 'demo-004', 'demo-005']) expect(store.entityState(id)).toBe('SCORED');
    expect(verifyChain(store)).toMatchObject({ ok: true });
  });

  it('lists and releases a door trip (no step row): after the cause is fixed a new run scores the item', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const p1src = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSourceContract, dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0', 'P1'] }, emits: 'P1' }), demoSource.run);
    const r1 = await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer, source: p1src }).start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r1.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 1, quarantined: 5 });
    expect(store.entityState('demo-001')).toBe('QUARANTINED');
    const q = store.quarantinedSteps();
    expect(q).toHaveLength(5);
    for (const s of q) {
      expect(store.getStep(s.step_key)).toBeUndefined();
      expect(s.agent_id).toBe('demo-scorer');
      expect(s.detail ?? '').toContain('G-DATACLASS');
    }
    const policy = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, {});
    for (const s of q) {
      expect(store.releaseQuarantine(s.step_key, 'release-x', '2026-10-08T08:00:00.000Z', policyHash(policy))).toEqual({ released: true, entities: [{ kind: 'demo-item', entityId: expect.any(String) }], skipped: [] });
    }
    expect(store.quarantinedSteps()).toEqual([]);
    expect(store.entityState('demo-001')).toBe('DISCOVERED');
    expect(store.releaseQuarantine(q[0]!.step_key, 'release-x', '2026-10-08T08:00:00.000Z', policyHash(policy))).toEqual({ released: false, entities: [], skipped: [] });
    const fixed = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSourceContract, version: '2.0.0' }), demoSource.run);
    const r2 = await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer, source: fixed }).start('run-2', [seed]);
    expect(r2.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 6, quarantined: 0 });
    for (const id of ['demo-001', 'demo-002', 'demo-003', 'demo-004', 'demo-005']) expect(store.entityState(id)).toBe('SCORED');
    expect(verifyChain(store)).toMatchObject({ ok: true });
  });

  it('leaves an entity that moved on since the trip, keeps its transition row and reports it as skipped', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const bad = demoKernel({ store, llm: fakeLlm(() => JSON.stringify({ score: 99, reason: 'too keen' })) });
    await bad.supervisor.start('run-1', [{ count: 2, prefix: 'demo' }]);
    const q = store.quarantinedSteps();
    expect(q).toHaveLength(2);
    const trip = (key: string) =>
      store.db.prepare(`SELECT entity_id, to_state FROM entity_transitions WHERE step_key = ? AND outcome = 'quarantined'`).get(key) as { entity_id: string; to_state: string };
    const [moved, kept] = q.map((s) => s.step_key) as [string, string];
    const movedId = trip(moved).entity_id;
    expect(trip(moved).to_state).toBe('QUARANTINED');
    // someone advanced the entity by hand after the trip
    store.db.prepare(`UPDATE entities SET state = 'SCORED' WHERE kind = 'demo-item' AND entity_id = ?`).run(movedId);
    const movedRef = { kind: 'demo-item', entityId: movedId };
    expect(store.releaseQuarantine(moved, 'release-x', '2026-10-08T08:00:00.000Z', bad.policyHash)).toEqual({ released: true, entities: [], skipped: [movedRef] });
    expect(store.entityState(movedId, 'demo-item')).toBe('SCORED');
    expect(trip(moved)).toEqual({ entity_id: movedId, to_state: 'QUARANTINED' });
    expect(store.getStep(moved)).toBeUndefined();
    const ev = store.events('release-x').at(-1)!;
    expect(JSON.parse(ev.data)).toEqual({ stepKey: moved, entities: [], skipped: [movedRef] });
    // releasing it again reverts nothing and deletes no step row: not released, no event
    const events = store.events().length;
    expect(store.releaseQuarantine(moved, 'release-x', '2026-10-08T08:00:00.000Z', bad.policyHash)).toEqual({ released: false, entities: [], skipped: [movedRef] });
    expect(store.events()).toHaveLength(events);
    // the untouched one is reverted as before
    const keptId = trip(kept).entity_id;
    expect(store.releaseQuarantine(kept, 'release-x', '2026-10-08T08:00:00.000Z', bad.policyHash)).toEqual({ released: true, entities: [{ kind: 'demo-item', entityId: keptId }], skipped: [] });
    expect(store.entityState(keptId, 'demo-item')).toBe('DISCOVERED');
    expect(verifyChain(store)).toMatchObject({ ok: true });
  });

  it('does not release a step that is not quarantined', async () => {
    const k = demoKernel();
    await k.supervisor.start('run-1', [seed]);
    const done = JSON.parse(k.store.events('run-1').find((e) => e.kind === 'step_done')!.data).stepKey as string;
    const before = k.store.events().length;
    expect(k.store.releaseQuarantine(done, 'release-x', '2026-10-08T08:00:00.000Z', k.policyHash)).toEqual({ released: false, entities: [], skipped: [] });
    expect(k.store.releaseQuarantine('no-such-key', 'release-x', '2026-10-08T08:00:00.000Z', k.policyHash)).toEqual({ released: false, entities: [], skipped: [] });
    expect(k.store.getStep(done)?.status).toBe('done');
    expect(k.store.events()).toHaveLength(before);
  });

  /** A second view: one entity per score, whose event depends on the envelope's payload. */
  const scoreTracker: Tracker = {
    kind: 'demo-score',
    machine: new StateMachine({ id: 'demo-score', initial: 'NEW', terminal: ['HIGH', 'LOW'], transitions: [{ from: 'NEW', on: 'high', to: 'HIGH' }, { from: 'NEW', on: 'low', to: 'LOW' }] }),
    keyOf: (env) => (env.type === 'DemoScore@1' ? `score-${(env.payload as { id: string }).id}` : null),
    eventFor: (_agent, outcome, env) => (outcome === 'done' ? ((env.payload as { score: number }).score >= 5 ? 'high' : 'low') : null),
  };

  it('applies every tracker independently, and passes the envelope to eventFor', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const r = await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer, trackers: [demoTracker, scoreTracker] }).start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 6, failed: 0 });
    const scores = store.envelopesOfStep(JSON.parse(store.events('run-1').filter((e) => e.kind === 'step_done').at(-1)!.data).stepKey);
    const s = scores[0]!.payload as { id: string; score: number };
    expect(store.entityState(s.id)).toBe('SCORED');
    expect(store.entityState(`score-${s.id}`)).toBe(s.score >= 5 ? 'HIGH' : 'LOW');
    for (const id of ['demo-001', 'demo-002', 'demo-003', 'demo-004', 'demo-005']) {
      expect(store.entityState(id)).toBe('SCORED');
      expect(['HIGH', 'LOW']).toContain(store.entityState(`score-${id}`));
    }
  });

  it('gives each tracker its own entity namespace, even when two key the same id', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const seen: Tracker = {
      kind: 'seen-item',
      machine: new StateMachine({ id: 'seen-item', initial: 'NEW', terminal: ['SEEN'], transitions: [{ from: 'NEW', on: 'seen', to: 'SEEN' }] }),
      keyOf: (env) => (env.type === 'DemoItem@1' ? (env.payload as { id: string }).id : null),
      eventFor: (agentId, outcome) => (outcome === 'done' && agentId === 'demo-source' ? 'seen' : null),
    };
    const r = await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer, trackers: [demoTracker, seen] }).start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ done: 6, failed: 0 });
    expect(store.events('run-1').filter((e) => e.kind === 'transition_rejected' || e.kind === 'tracker_error')).toEqual([]);
    for (const id of ['demo-001', 'demo-002', 'demo-003', 'demo-004', 'demo-005']) {
      expect(store.entityState(id, 'demo-item')).toBe('SCORED');
      expect(store.entityState(id, 'seen-item')).toBe('SEEN');
    }
    expect(() => store.entityState('demo-001')).toThrow(/ambiguous/);
    expect(store.entityState('no-such-id')).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('a throwing tracker is audited and does not wedge the run or the other trackers', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const broken: Tracker = { ...scoreTracker, kind: 'broken', keyOf: () => { throw new Error('keyOf exploded'); } };
    const badEvent: Tracker = { ...scoreTracker, kind: 'bad-event', eventFor: () => { throw new Error('eventFor exploded'); } };
    const r = await handBuilt({ store, scorer: demoScorer, trackers: [broken, demoTracker, badEvent] }).start('run-1', [seed]);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ pending: 0, done: 6, failed: 0 });
    const errors = store.events('run-1').filter((e) => e.kind === 'tracker_error').map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data) as { tracker: string; agent: string; stepKey: string; message: string });
    expect(errors.filter((e) => e.tracker === 'broken')).toHaveLength(10);
    expect(errors.filter((e) => e.tracker === 'bad-event')).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(errors[0]).toMatchObject({ tracker: 'broken', agent: 'demo-source', message: 'keyOf exploded', stepKey: expect.any(String) });
    expect(store.entityState('demo-003')).toBe('SCORED');
  });
});

/** The demoKernel wiring, by hand, with a scorer that by default declares `demo.scored` but finishes with no outputs, leaving it open. */
function handBuilt(o: {
  store: Store;
  llm?: LlmProvider;
  pipeline?: Pipeline;
  scorer?: typeof demoScorer;
  source?: typeof demoSource;
  onInvoke?: SupervisorDeps['onInvoke'];
  trackers?: readonly Tracker[];
  afterStep?: SupervisorDeps['afterStep'];
}): Supervisor {
  const emptyScorer = defineAgent(demoScorerContract, async () => []);
  const registry = new Registry();
  registry.register(o.source ?? demoSource);
  registry.register(o.scorer ?? emptyScorer);
  const guardrails = builtinGuardrails();
  const compiled = compileWiring(registry, o.pipeline ?? demoPipeline, guardrails);
  const policy = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, {});
  return new Supervisor({
    store: o.store,
    registry,
    compiled,
    guardrails,
    policy,
    policyHash: policyHash(policy),
    providers: { fetch: async () => ({ status: 404, text: async () => '' }), llm: o.llm ?? demoLlm() },
    clock: fixedClock('2026-10-08T07:00:00.000Z'),
    sleep: async () => {},
    trackers: o.trackers ?? [demoTracker],
    ...(o.onInvoke ? { onInvoke: o.onInvoke } : {}),
    ...(o.afterStep ? { afterStep: o.afterStep } : {}),
  });
}

describe('G-BUDGET: an over-priced model call stops that agent for the rest of the run', () => {
  it('pays for exactly one 0.4 call when usdPerCall is 0.15, and skips the other four items', async () => {
    const store = Store.open(':memory:');
    const pricey = defineAgent(
      defineContract({ ...demoScorerContract, budget: { usdPerCall: 0.15, usdPerRun: 1.0 }, breaker: { consecutiveFailures: 10 } }),
      demoScorer.run,
    );
    const r = await handBuilt({ store, scorer: pricey, llm: fakeLlm(() => JSON.stringify({ score: 5, reason: 'ok' }), { usdPerCall: 0.4 }) }).start('r', [
      { count: 5, prefix: 'demo' },
    ]);
    expect(store.runCost('r')).toBeCloseTo(0.4, 10);
    expect(r.queue).toMatchObject({ failed: 1, skipped: 4 });
    const failed = store.events('r').filter((e) => e.kind === 'step_failed').map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data) as { error: string });
    expect(failed).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ error: 'budget_exceeded' })]);
    const skipped = store.events('r').filter((e) => e.kind === 'step_skipped').map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data) as { reason: string; detail?: string });
    expect(skipped).toHaveLength(4);
    for (const s of skipped) {
      expect(s).toMatchObject({ reason: 'budget', detail: 'G-BUDGET: demo-scorer made a call above usdPerCall earlier in this run' });
    }
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`src/kernel/supervisor.ts`:

```ts
import { Breakers } from './breaker.js';
import { canonicalJson, hashOf } from './canonical.js';
import type { Clock } from './clock.js';
import { contractHash, contractShape, securityDiff } from './contract.js';
import { type Envelope, makeEnvelopeId, openObligations, ZERO_COST } from './envelope.js';
import { InvalidTransition } from './errors.js';
import { executeStep, type ExecutorDeps, type StepOutcome } from './executor.js';
import type { GuardrailRegistry } from './guardrails/index.js';
import type { Policy } from './policy.js';
import type { Providers } from './providers.js';
import type { Registry } from './registry.js';
import type { StateMachine } from './state-machine.js';
import type { EventRow, Store } from './store/store.js';
import { type CompiledPipeline, SUPERVISOR_ID, seedClass } from './wiring.js';

/** Maps envelopes and outcomes onto an entity state machine (e.g. one row per job). */
export interface Tracker {
  readonly kind: string;
  readonly machine: StateMachine;
  keyOf(env: Envelope): string | null;
  /** `env` is the tracked envelope: an output when the step is done, its input when it is quarantined. */
  eventFor(agentId: string, outcome: 'done' | 'quarantined', env: Envelope): string | null;
}

export interface SupervisorDeps {
  readonly store: Store;
  readonly registry: Registry;
  readonly compiled: CompiledPipeline;
  readonly guardrails: GuardrailRegistry;
  readonly policy: Policy;
  readonly policyHash: string;
  readonly providers: Providers;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly sleep?: (ms: number) => Promise<void>;
  /** Applied independently, in array order. */
  readonly trackers?: readonly Tracker[];
  readonly onInvoke?: (agentId: string, stepKey: string) => void;
  /** Test hook: runs after a step's own transaction, before routing. Throwing here simulates a crash. */
  readonly afterStep?: (count: number) => void;
}

export interface RunReport {
  readonly runId: string;
  readonly status: 'completed';
  readonly queue: Record<'pending' | 'done' | 'failed' | 'quarantined' | 'skipped', number>;
  readonly replayed: number;
  readonly openObligations: readonly { readonly envelopeId: string; readonly ids: readonly string[] }[];
  readonly costUsd: number;
}

/**
 * The deterministic hub. It never calls a model and never decides anything a
 * table doesn't: routes come from the compiled wiring, order from the queue's
 * total order, state from the tracker's machine.
 */
export class Supervisor {
  /**
   * Breakers are per run and in memory only: their counts restart after a
   * resume, so a resumed run re-learns failures. Everything else a report
   * needs is read back from the store.
   */
  private readonly breakersByRun = new Map<string, Breakers>();

  constructor(private readonly d: SupervisorDeps) {}

  private get exec(): ExecutorDeps {
    const d = this.d;
    return {
      store: d.store,
      policy: d.policy,
      policyHash: d.policyHash,
      providers: d.providers,
      clock: d.clock,
      guardrails: d.guardrails,
      sleep: d.sleep ?? ((ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms))),
      contractHashOf: (id, v) => (id === SUPERVISOR_ID && v === d.compiled.pipeline.version ? d.compiled.seedHash : d.registry.contractHashOf(id, v)),
      ...(d.onInvoke ? { onInvoke: d.onInvoke } : {}),
    };
  }

  private breakers(runId: string): Breakers {
    let b = this.breakersByRun.get(runId);
    if (!b) this.breakersByRun.set(runId, (b = new Breakers()));
    return b;
  }

  private event(runId: string, kind: string, data: unknown): void {
    this.d.store.appendEvent(runId, this.d.clock.now(), kind, data, this.d.policyHash);
  }

  /** Validates seeds, records the run and drains its queue. */
  async start(runId: string, seeds: readonly unknown[]): Promise<RunReport> {
    const { store, compiled, policyHash, clock } = this.d;
    if (store.getRun(runId)) throw new Error(`run ${runId} already exists; resume it instead`);
    const seedType = compiled.pipeline.seed.type;
    const envs: Envelope[] = seeds.map((payload) => {
      const r = seedType.schema.safeParse(payload);
      if (!r.success || canonicalJson(r.data) !== canonicalJson(payload)) throw new TypeError(`seed is not a valid ${seedType.id}`);
      for (const g of compiled.pipeline.seed.guarantees) if (!g.test(payload)) throw new TypeError(`seed fails ${g.id}`);
      const dataClass = seedClass(compiled.pipeline);
      const stepKey = hashOf({ seed: compiled.pipeline.id, version: compiled.pipeline.version, payload, dataClass, policyHash });
      return {
        envelopeId: makeEnvelopeId(stepKey, 0),
        stepKey,
        index: 0,
        type: seedType.id,
        producer: { agentId: SUPERVISOR_ID, version: compiled.pipeline.version, contractHash: compiled.seedHash },
        parents: [],
        payload,
        handoff: { guarantees: compiled.pipeline.seed.guarantees.map((g) => g.id).sort(), dataClass, obligations: [], pins: { policyHash } },
        guardrails: [],
        cost: ZERO_COST,
      };
    });
    store.tx(() => {
      store.startRun(runId, `${compiled.pipeline.id}@${compiled.pipeline.version}`, policyHash, clock.now());
      for (const a of this.d.registry.list()) store.saveContract(a.contract.id, a.contract.version, contractHash(a.contract), contractShape(a.contract));
      for (const e of envs) {
        store.putSeed(e.stepKey, e, compiled.pipeline.version, compiled.seedHash);
        this.route(runId, e);
      }
      this.event(runId, 'run_started', { pipeline: compiled.pipeline.id, seeds: envs.length });
    });
    this.rememberContracts();
    return this.drain(runId);
  }

  /** Continues a crashed run. Finished steps replay from the store; nothing finished runs again. */
  async resume(runId: string): Promise<RunReport> {
    const run = this.d.store.getRun(runId);
    if (!run) throw new Error(`unknown run ${runId}`);
    if (run.status === 'completed') throw new Error(`run ${runId} is already completed`);
    if (run.policy_hash !== this.d.policyHash) throw new Error(`run ${runId} used a different policy; start a new run instead`);
    this.event(runId, 'run_resumed', {});
    this.rememberContracts();
    return this.drain(runId);
  }

  /**
   * Past minor versions recorded by earlier runs stay valid producers (G-LINEAGE), but only
   * when their security-relevant fields match the current contract. Otherwise their outputs
   * fail G-LINEAGE downstream: a taint or capability change must never ride on a replay.
   */
  private rememberContracts(): void {
    const { registry } = this.d;
    for (const c of this.d.store.contracts()) {
      if (!registry.has(c.agent_id)) continue;
      if (securityDiff(c.shape, contractShape(registry.get(c.agent_id).contract)).length > 0) continue;
      registry.rememberVersion(c.agent_id, c.version, c.hash);
    }
  }

  private route(runId: string, env: Envelope): void {
    const consumers = this.d.compiled.routes.get(env.type) ?? [];
    for (const to of consumers) this.d.store.enqueue(runId, this.d.compiled.stage.get(to) ?? 0, to, env.envelopeId);
    if (consumers.length === 0) this.reportOpen(runId, env);
  }

  /** An envelope that goes no further with obligations still open is reported in the run. */
  private reportOpen(runId: string, env: Envelope): void {
    const ids = openObligations(env);
    if (ids.length > 0) this.event(runId, 'obligation_open', { envelopeId: env.envelopeId, ids });
  }

  private failure(key: string, threshold: number, runId: string): void {
    if (this.breakers(runId).failure(key, threshold)) this.event(runId, 'breaker_open', { key });
  }

  private async drain(runId: string): Promise<RunReport> {
    const { store, registry, compiled } = this.d;
    const breakers = this.breakers(runId);
    let count = 0;
    for (let item = store.nextQueued(runId); item; item = store.nextQueued(runId)) {
      const agent = registry.get(item.agent_id);
      const team = compiled.teamOf.get(item.agent_id);
      const agentKey = `agent:${item.agent_id}`;
      // An agent without a team has no team breaker.
      const teamKey = team ? `team:${team.id}` : undefined;
      const input = store.getEnvelope(item.envelope_id);
      if (breakers.isOpen(agentKey) || (teamKey !== undefined && breakers.isOpen(teamKey))) {
        store.tx(() => {
          store.markQueue(runId, item.agent_id, item.envelope_id, 'skipped');
          this.event(runId, 'step_skipped', { agent: item.agent_id, reason: 'breaker_open' });
          this.reportOpen(runId, input);
        });
        continue;
      }
      const outcome: StepOutcome = await executeStep(this.exec, runId, agent, input);
      count += 1;
      this.d.afterStep?.(count);

      store.tx(() => {
        if (outcome.kind === 'done') {
          for (const out of outcome.outputs) {
            this.route(runId, out);
            this.track(runId, out, outcome.stepKey, item.agent_id, 'done');
          }
        } else if (outcome.kind === 'quarantined') {
          this.track(runId, input, outcome.stepKey, item.agent_id, 'quarantined');
        }
        // The input never got past this step (or the step produced nothing to carry it), so whatever it still owes stays open.
        if (outcome.kind !== 'done' || outcome.outputs.length === 0) this.reportOpen(runId, input);
        store.markQueue(runId, item.agent_id, item.envelope_id, outcome.kind);
      });

      if (outcome.kind === 'failed') {
        this.failure(agentKey, agent.contract.breaker.consecutiveFailures, runId);
        if (team && teamKey !== undefined) this.failure(teamKey, team.breaker.consecutiveFailures, runId);
      } else if (outcome.kind === 'done') {
        breakers.success(agentKey);
        if (teamKey !== undefined) breakers.success(teamKey);
      }
    }
    store.finishRun(runId, this.d.clock.now());
    const events = store.events(runId);
    const report: RunReport = {
      runId,
      status: 'completed',
      queue: store.queueCounts(runId),
      replayed: events.filter((e) => e.kind === 'step_replayed').length,
      openObligations: openObligationsOf(events),
      costUsd: store.runCost(runId),
    };
    this.event(runId, 'run_finished', report);
    return report;
  }

  private track(runId: string, env: Envelope, stepKey: string, agentId: string, outcome: 'done' | 'quarantined'): void {
    for (const t of this.d.trackers ?? []) this.trackOne(t, runId, env, stepKey, agentId, outcome);
  }

  private trackOne(t: Tracker, runId: string, env: Envelope, stepKey: string, agentId: string, outcome: 'done' | 'quarantined'): void {
    // Tracker callbacks are pluggable code: one that throws is audited, never allowed to wedge the run.
    let key: string | null;
    let ev: string | null = null;
    try {
      key = t.keyOf(env);
      if (key !== null) ev = t.eventFor(agentId, outcome, env);
    } catch (err) {
      this.event(runId, 'tracker_error', { tracker: t.kind, agent: agentId, stepKey, message: messageOf(err) });
      return;
    }
    if (key === null || ev === null) return;
    const { store } = this.d;
    if (store.hasTransition(key, t.kind, stepKey, ev)) return; // a replayed step never re-applies
    let to: string;
    try {
      to = t.machine.next(store.entityState(key, t.kind) ?? t.machine.def.initial, ev);
    } catch (err) {
      // The same entity can legitimately arrive twice (two sources list the same job): it keeps its state and the duplicate is audited.
      if (!(err instanceof InvalidTransition)) throw err;
      this.event(runId, 'transition_rejected', { entity: key, event: ev, agent: agentId, stepKey });
      return;
    }
    const open = openObligations(env);
    if (t.machine.isSuccess(to) && open.length > 0) {
      this.event(runId, 'obligation_blocked', { entity: key, event: ev, ids: open });
      return;
    }
    store.applyTransition(key, t.kind, stepKey, ev, t.machine, outcome);
  }
}

function messageOf(err: unknown): string {
  try {
    return err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
  } catch {
    return 'unprintable error';
  }
}

/** The run's `obligation_open` events, one per envelope, in envelope order. */
function openObligationsOf(events: readonly EventRow[]): RunReport['openObligations'] {
  const byEnvelope = new Map<string, readonly string[]>();
  for (const e of events) {
    if (e.kind !== 'obligation_open') continue;
    const d = JSON.parse(e.data) as { envelopeId: string; ids: string[] };
    byEnvelope.set(d.envelopeId, d.ids);
  }
  return [...byEnvelope]
    .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0))
    .map(([envelopeId, ids]) => ({ envelopeId, ids }));
}
```

`src/demo/kernel.ts`:

```ts
import { fixedClock, type Clock } from '../kernel/clock.js';
import { builtinGuardrails } from '../kernel/guardrails/builtin.js';
import { mergePolicy, policyHash, PRODUCT_POLICY_V1 } from '../kernel/policy.js';
import type { LlmProvider } from '../kernel/providers.js';
import { Registry } from '../kernel/registry.js';
import { Store } from '../kernel/store/store.js';
import { Supervisor, type SupervisorDeps } from '../kernel/supervisor.js';
import { compileWiring } from '../kernel/wiring.js';
import { demoAgents, demoLlm, demoPipeline, demoTracker } from './pipeline.js';

export interface DemoKernelOptions {
  readonly dbPath?: string;
  readonly store?: Store;
  readonly llm?: LlmProvider;
  readonly userPolicy?: unknown;
  readonly clock?: Clock;
  readonly onInvoke?: SupervisorDeps['onInvoke'];
  readonly afterStep?: SupervisorDeps['afterStep'];
}

/** Wires the demo pipeline end to end; the CLI and the kernel tests both use it. */
export function demoKernel(o: DemoKernelOptions = {}) {
  const store = o.store ?? Store.open(o.dbPath ?? ':memory:');
  const registry = new Registry();
  for (const a of demoAgents) registry.register(a);
  const guardrails = builtinGuardrails();
  const compiled = compileWiring(registry, demoPipeline, guardrails);
  const policy = mergePolicy(PRODUCT_POLICY_V1, o.userPolicy ?? {});
  const pHash = policyHash(policy);
  const supervisor = new Supervisor({
    store,
    registry,
    compiled,
    guardrails,
    policy,
    policyHash: pHash,
    providers: { fetch: async () => ({ status: 404, text: async () => '' }), llm: o.llm ?? demoLlm() },
    clock: o.clock ?? fixedClock('2026-10-08T07:00:00.000Z'),
    sleep: async () => {},
    trackers: [demoTracker],
    ...(o.onInvoke ? { onInvoke: o.onInvoke } : {}),
    ...(o.afterStep ? { afterStep: o.afterStep } : {}),
  });
  return { store, registry, compiled, supervisor, policy, policyHash: pHash };
}
```

### Task 13: Contract export and drift detection

Every contract is exported as JSON Schema into `contracts-out/` and committed. A contract whose shape changes without a version bump fails the build, because other agents rely on the old shape.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/kernel/contracts-export.ts`
- Test: `tests/kernel/contracts-export.test.ts`
- Create (generated): `contracts-out/`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Registry`, `contractHash`, `contractMajor`, `contractShape`, `canonicalJson`.
- Produces: `ContractDoc`, `exportContracts(registry, dir): string[]`, `checkDrift(registry, dir): string[]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`tests/kernel/contracts-export.test.ts`:

```ts
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { defineAgent } from '../../src/kernel/agent.js';
import { checkDrift, exportContracts } from '../../src/kernel/contracts-export.js';
import { defineContract } from '../../src/kernel/contract.js';
import { Registry } from '../../src/kernel/registry.js';
import { demoAgents } from '../../src/demo/pipeline.js';
import { demoSource } from '../../src/demo/agents.js';
import { itemIdSet, scoreInRange } from '../../src/demo/types.js';

const registry = (...agents: Parameters<Registry['register']>[0][]) => {
  const r = new Registry();
  for (const a of agents) r.register(a);
  return r;
};

describe('contract export and drift', () => {
  it('the committed contracts-out/ matches the code', () => {
    expect(checkDrift(registry(...demoAgents), join(import.meta.dirname, '../../contracts-out'))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('writes one file per agent major, with JSON Schemas for both message types', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    const paths = exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    expect(paths.map((p) => p.split('/').at(-1))).toEqual(['demo-scorer@1.json', 'demo-source@1.json']);
    const doc = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths[1]!, 'utf8'));
    expect(doc.shape.produces.schema.additionalProperties).toBe(false);
  });

  it('fails when a guarantee changes but the version does not', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    const changed = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSource.contract, guarantees: [itemIdSet, scoreInRange as never] }), demoSource.run);
    expect(checkDrift(registry(changed, demoAgents[1]), dir)).toEqual(['demo-source@1.0.0: contract changed without a version bump']);
  });

  it('asks for an export when the version was bumped', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    const bumped = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSource.contract, version: '1.1.0' }), demoSource.run);
    expect(checkDrift(registry(bumped, demoAgents[1]), dir)[0]).toMatch(/1\.1\.0 is not exported yet/);
  });

  it('refuses a security-relevant change in a minor version', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    const emits = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSource.contract, version: '1.1.0', emits: 'P0' }), demoSource.run);
    expect(checkDrift(registry(emits, demoAgents[1]), dir)).toEqual(['demo-source: emits changed in a minor version; security-relevant changes need a major bump']);
    const wider = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSource.contract, version: '1.0.1', dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0', 'P1'] }, emits: 'P1' }), demoSource.run);
    expect(checkDrift(registry(wider, demoAgents[1]), dir)).toEqual(['demo-source: dataClasses, emits changed in a minor version; security-relevant changes need a major bump']);
    const timeout = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSource.contract, version: '1.1.0', timeoutMs: 2_000 }), demoSource.run);
    expect(checkDrift(registry(timeout, demoAgents[1]), dir)[0]).toMatch(/1\.1\.0 is not exported yet/);
  });

  it('refuses to export a security-relevant minor change, writing nothing', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    const paths = exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    const before = paths.map((p) => readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
    const wider = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSource.contract, version: '1.1.0', dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0', 'P1'] }, emits: 'P1' }), demoSource.run);
    expect(() => exportContracts(registry(demoAgents[1]!, wider), dir)).toThrow(
      'demo-source: dataClasses, emits changed in a minor version; bump the major version before exporting',
    );
    expect(paths.map((p) => readFileSync(p, 'utf8'))).toEqual(before);
    // a non-security minor change still exports, and a major bump of the security change is a new file
    const timeout = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSource.contract, version: '1.1.0', timeoutMs: 2_000 }), demoSource.run);
    expect(() => exportContracts(registry(timeout, demoAgents[1]!), dir)).not.toThrow();
    const major = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...wider.contract, version: '2.0.0' }), demoSource.run);
    expect(() => exportContracts(registry(major, demoAgents[1]!), dir)).not.toThrow();
    expect(checkDrift(registry(major, demoAgents[1]!), dir)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('refuses to export a security-relevant change at the same version, writing nothing', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    const paths = exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    const before = paths.map((p) => readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
    const wider = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSource.contract, dataClasses: { in: ['P0'], out: ['P0', 'P1'] }, emits: 'P1' }), demoSource.run);
    expect(() => exportContracts(registry(demoAgents[1]!, wider), dir)).toThrow(
      'demo-source@1.0.0: dataClasses, emits changed without a version bump; bump the major version before exporting',
    );
    expect(paths.map((p) => readFileSync(p, 'utf8'))).toEqual(before);
    // a same-version budget/timeout change exports without a drift report: those fields are not security-relevant
    const timeout = defineAgent(defineContract({ ...demoSource.contract, timeoutMs: 2_000 }), demoSource.run);
    expect(() => exportContracts(registry(timeout, demoAgents[1]!), dir)).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('notices a hand-edited contract file', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    const [scorer] = exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    const doc = JSON.parse(readFileSync(scorer!, 'utf8'));
    writeFileSync(scorer!, JSON.stringify({ ...doc, hash: 'tampered' }));
    expect(checkDrift(registry(...demoAgents), dir)).toEqual(['demo-scorer@1.0.0: contract changed without a version bump']);
  });

  it('reports a stale export no agent maps to, and export removes it', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    writeFileSync(join(dir, 'ghost@1.json'), '{}');
    expect(checkDrift(registry(...demoAgents), dir)).toEqual(['ghost@1.json: no registered agent exports this contract; delete it or restore the agent']);
    exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    expect(existsSync(join(dir, 'ghost@1.json'))).toBe(false);
    expect(checkDrift(registry(...demoAgents), dir)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('notices a shape edited by hand while the hash is intact', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    const [, source] = exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    const doc = JSON.parse(readFileSync(source!, 'utf8'));
    writeFileSync(source!, JSON.stringify({ ...doc, shape: { ...doc.shape, kind: 'tampered' } }));
    expect(checkDrift(registry(...demoAgents), dir)).toEqual(['demo-source@1.0.0: contracts-out file was edited by hand; run node --run contracts:export']);
  });

  it('reports a committed version newer than the code', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    const [, source] = exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    const doc = JSON.parse(readFileSync(source!, 'utf8'));
    writeFileSync(source!, JSON.stringify({ ...doc, shape: { ...doc.shape, version: '9.0.0' } }));
    expect(checkDrift(registry(...demoAgents), dir)).toEqual(['demo-source: code version 1.0.0 is older than the exported 9.0.0']);
  });

  it('reports an unreadable contract file instead of throwing', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    const [, source] = exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir);
    writeFileSync(source!, 'not json {');
    expect(checkDrift(registry(...demoAgents), dir)).toEqual(['demo-source@1: contract file is unreadable; run node --run contracts:export']);
  });

  it('reports a missing export directory instead of throwing', () => {
    const dir = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-')), 'nope');
    expect(checkDrift(registry(...demoAgents), dir)).toEqual([
      'demo-scorer@1: no exported contract; run node --run contracts:export',
      'demo-source@1: no exported contract; run node --run contracts:export',
    ]);
  });

  it('leaves a directory named like a json file alone on export', () => {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'contracts-'));
    mkdirSync(join(dir, 'x.json'));
    expect(() => exportContracts(registry(...demoAgents), dir)).not.toThrow();
    expect(existsSync(join(dir, 'x.json'))).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`src/kernel/contracts-export.ts`:

```ts
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, unlinkSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { basename, join } from 'node:path';
import { canonicalJson } from './canonical.js';
import { contractHash, contractMajor, contractShape, securityDiff } from './contract.js';
import type { Registry } from './registry.js';

export interface ContractDoc {
  readonly hash: string;
  readonly shape: Record<string, unknown>;
}

const fileOf = (dir: string, id: string, major: string) => join(dir, `${id}@${major}.json`);

/** Pretty, key-sorted JSON so diffs are reviewable. */
function render(doc: ContractDoc): string {
  return `${JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(canonicalJson(doc)), null, 2)}\n`;
}

export function exportContracts(registry: Registry, dir: string): string[] {
  // Refuse before writing anything: re-exporting would erase the evidence that a minor version,
  // or no version bump at all, changed what the agent may touch (past outputs would still pass G-LINEAGE).
  for (const a of registry.list()) {
    const c = a.contract;
    const path = fileOf(dir, c.id, contractMajor(c));
    const doc = existsSync(path) ? readDoc(path) : undefined;
    if (!doc) continue;
    const sameVersion = (doc.shape as { version?: unknown }).version === c.version;
    if (sameVersion && doc.hash === contractHash(c)) continue;
    const changed = securityDiff(doc.shape, contractShape(c));
    if (changed.length === 0) continue;
    throw new Error(
      sameVersion
        ? `${c.id}@${c.version}: ${changed.join(', ')} changed without a version bump; bump the major version before exporting`
        : `${c.id}: ${changed.join(', ')} changed in a minor version; bump the major version before exporting`,
    );
  }
  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  const paths = registry.list().map((a) => {
    const c = a.contract;
    const path = fileOf(dir, c.id, contractMajor(c));
    writeFileSync(path, render({ hash: contractHash(c), shape: contractShape(c) }));
    return path;
  });
  // The directory is generated: anything this run did not write is stale.
  const written = new Set(paths.map((p) => basename(p)));
  for (const f of readdirSync(dir)) if (f.endsWith('.json') && !written.has(f) && statSync(join(dir, f)).isFile()) unlinkSync(join(dir, f));
  return paths;
}

const semverParts = (v: string): number[] => v.split('.').map((n) => Number.parseInt(n, 10) || 0);

/** Numeric x.y.z comparison: negative when a < b. */
function compareVersions(a: string, b: string): number {
  const [pa, pb] = [semverParts(a), semverParts(b)];
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const d = (pa[i] ?? 0) - (pb[i] ?? 0);
    if (d !== 0) return d;
  }
  return 0;
}

function readDoc(path: string): ContractDoc | undefined {
  try {
    const doc = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, 'utf8')) as Partial<ContractDoc> | null;
    if (doc && typeof doc.hash === 'string' && typeof doc.shape === 'object' && doc.shape !== null) return doc as ContractDoc;
  } catch {
    // fall through: unreadable
  }
  return undefined;
}

/** A contract that changed shape without a version bump is drift: other agents rely on the old shape. */
export function checkDrift(registry: Registry, dir: string): string[] {
  const problems: string[] = [];
  for (const a of registry.list()) {
    const c = a.contract;
    const path = fileOf(dir, c.id, contractMajor(c));
    if (!existsSync(path)) {
      problems.push(`${c.id}@${contractMajor(c)}: no exported contract; run node --run contracts:export`);
      continue;
    }
    const doc = readDoc(path);
    if (!doc) {
      problems.push(`${c.id}@${contractMajor(c)}: contract file is unreadable; run node --run contracts:export`);
      continue;
    }
    const committedVersion = (doc.shape as { version?: string }).version;
    if (committedVersion === c.version && doc.hash !== contractHash(c)) {
      problems.push(`${c.id}@${c.version}: contract changed without a version bump`);
    } else if (committedVersion === c.version && canonicalJson(doc.shape) !== canonicalJson(contractShape(c))) {
      problems.push(`${c.id}@${c.version}: contracts-out file was edited by hand; run node --run contracts:export`);
    } else if (committedVersion !== c.version && typeof committedVersion === 'string' && compareVersions(committedVersion, c.version) > 0) {
      problems.push(`${c.id}: code version ${c.version} is older than the exported ${committedVersion}`);
    } else if (committedVersion !== c.version && securityDiff(doc.shape, contractShape(c)).length > 0) {
      // Same file, so same major: past outputs of the committed version would still pass G-LINEAGE.
      problems.push(`${c.id}: ${securityDiff(doc.shape, contractShape(c)).join(', ')} changed in a minor version; security-relevant changes need a major bump`);
    } else if (committedVersion !== c.version) {
      problems.push(`${c.id}: version ${c.version} is not exported yet (committed ${committedVersion}); run node --run contracts:export`);
    }
  }
  const expected = new Set(registry.list().map((a) => `${a.contract.id}@${contractMajor(a.contract)}.json`));
  const stale = (existsSync(dir) ? readdirSync(dir) : []).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.json') && !expected.has(f)).sort();
  for (const f of stale) problems.push(`${f}: no registered agent exports this contract; delete it or restore the agent`);
  return problems;
}
```

`src/evals/metrics.ts`:

```ts
import { canonicalJson } from '../kernel/canonical.js';

export function exactMatch(pairs: readonly { expected: unknown; actual: unknown }[]): number {
  if (pairs.length === 0) return 0;
  return pairs.filter((p) => canonicalJson(p.expected) === canonicalJson(p.actual)).length / pairs.length;
}

/** For a rule or classifier: positive = "the rule fired". */
export function precisionRecall(pairs: readonly { expected: boolean; actual: boolean }[]): { precision: number; recall: number } {
  const tp = pairs.filter((p) => p.expected && p.actual).length;
  const fp = pairs.filter((p) => !p.expected && p.actual).length;
  const fn = pairs.filter((p) => p.expected && !p.actual).length;
  return { precision: tp + fp === 0 ? 1 : tp / (tp + fp), recall: tp + fn === 0 ? 1 : tp / (tp + fn) };
}

/** Average ranks for ties, then Pearson on the ranks. */
export function spearman(xs: readonly number[], ys: readonly number[]): number {
  if (xs.length !== ys.length || xs.length < 2) throw new RangeError('spearman needs two equal-length series of at least 2');
  const rank = (v: readonly number[]) => {
    const order = v.map((x, i) => ({ x, i })).sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);
    const r = new Array<number>(v.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < order.length; ) {
      let j = i;
      while (j + 1 < order.length && order[j + 1]!.x === order[i]!.x) j++;
      for (let k = i; k <= j; k++) r[order[k]!.i] = (i + j) / 2 + 1;
      i = j + 1;
    }
    return r;
  };
  const rx = rank(xs);
  const ry = rank(ys);
  const mean = (a: number[]) => a.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) / a.length;
  const mx = mean(rx);
  const my = mean(ry);
  let num = 0;
  let dx = 0;
  let dy = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < rx.length; i++) {
    const a = rx[i]! - mx;
    const b = ry[i]! - my;
    num += a * b;
    dx += a * a;
    dy += b * b;
  }
  return dx === 0 || dy === 0 ? 0 : num / Math.sqrt(dx * dy);
}
```

`src/evals/runner.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import type { AnyAgent } from '../kernel/agent.js';
import { issueContext } from '../kernel/broker.js';
import { canonicalJson } from '../kernel/canonical.js';
import { fixedClock } from '../kernel/clock.js';
import { classify } from '../kernel/errors.js';
import type { Policy } from '../kernel/policy.js';
import type { Providers } from '../kernel/providers.js';
import { exactMatch } from './metrics.js';

export interface EvalCase {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly input: unknown;
  readonly expected: unknown;
}

export interface EvalSpec {
  readonly agent: AnyAgent;
  readonly cases: readonly EvalCase[];
  /** Maps the agent's outputs to what the dataset's `expected` describes. */
  readonly project: (outputs: readonly unknown[]) => unknown;
  readonly metric: 'exactMatch';
  readonly min: number;
}

export interface EvalReport {
  readonly agentId: string;
  readonly version: string;
  readonly metric: 'exactMatch';
  readonly n: number;
  readonly score: number;
  readonly min: number;
  readonly pass: boolean;
  readonly failures: readonly { readonly id: string; readonly expected: unknown; readonly actual: unknown }[];
}

export function loadJsonl(path: string): EvalCase[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  return readFileSync(path, 'utf8')
    .split('\n')
    .map((text, i) => ({ text, line: i + 1 }))
    .filter((l) => l.text.trim() !== '')
    .map(({ text, line }) => {
      let raw: unknown;
      try {
        raw = JSON.parse(text);
      } catch {
        throw new TypeError(`${path}:${line}: invalid JSON`);
      }
      const c = raw as Partial<EvalCase> | null;
      if (typeof raw !== 'object' || raw === null || Array.isArray(raw) || typeof c?.id !== 'string' || !('input' in c) || !('expected' in c)) {
        throw new TypeError(`${path}:${line}: needs id, input, expected`);
      }
      if (seen.has(c.id)) throw new TypeError(`${path}:${line}: duplicate id ${c.id}`);
      seen.add(c.id);
      return c as EvalCase;
    });
}

/** Rejects with the signal's reason as soon as it aborts, even if the work never settles. */
function raceAbort<T>(work: Promise<T>, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
    const onAbort = (): void => reject(signal.reason);
    if (signal.aborted) return onAbort();
    signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
    work.then(resolve, reject).finally(() => signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort));
  });
}

const same = (a: unknown, b: unknown): boolean => canonicalJson(a) === canonicalJson(b);

/** Runs each case through the agent with the same broker it gets in production; schema failures count as wrong. */
export async function runEval(spec: EvalSpec, env: { providers: Providers; policy: Policy }): Promise<EvalReport> {
  const c = spec.agent.contract;
  if (spec.cases.length === 0) throw new Error(`eval ${c.id}: no cases`);
  if (!(spec.min > 0 && spec.min <= 1)) throw new Error(`eval ${c.id}: min must be in (0, 1]`);
  const pairs: { id: string; expected: unknown; actual: unknown }[] = [];
  for (const k of spec.cases) {
    let actual: unknown;
    const input = c.consumes.schema.safeParse(k.input);
    if (!input.success) {
      actual = { error: 'input does not match the consumed schema' };
    } else if (!same(input.data, k.input)) {
      actual = { error: 'input would be coerced by the consumed schema' };
    } else {
      try {
        const signal = AbortSignal.timeout(c.timeoutMs);
        const ctx = issueContext({ contract: c, policy: env.policy, inputClass: 'P0', providers: env.providers, clock: fixedClock('2026-01-01T00:00:00Z'), signal });
        const outs = await raceAbort(spec.agent.run(k.input, ctx), signal);
        const parsed = outs.map((o) => ({ o, r: c.produces.schema.safeParse(o) }));
        if (parsed.some((p) => !p.r.success)) actual = { error: 'output does not match the produced schema' };
        else if (parsed.some((p) => !same((p.r as { data: unknown }).data, p.o))) actual = { error: 'output would be coerced by the produced schema' };
        else actual = spec.project(outs);
      } catch (e) {
        actual = { error: classify(e) === 'timeout' ? 'timeout' : (e as Error).message };
      }
    }
    pairs.push({ id: k.id, expected: k.expected, actual });
  }
  const score = exactMatch(pairs);
  return {
    agentId: c.id,
    version: c.version,
    metric: spec.metric,
    n: pairs.length,
    score,
    min: spec.min,
    pass: score >= spec.min,
    failures: pairs.filter((p) => !same(p.expected, p.actual)),
  };
}

export function failedGates(reports: readonly EvalReport[]): string[] {
  return reports.filter((r) => !r.pass).map((r) => `${r.agentId}@${r.version}: ${r.metric} ${r.score.toFixed(3)} < ${r.min}`);
}
```

```ts
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { loadJsonl, type EvalSpec } from '../evals/runner.js';
import { demoSource } from './agents.js';

export function demoEvalSpecs(datasetDir: string): EvalSpec[] {
  return [
    {
      agent: demoSource,
      cases: loadJsonl(join(datasetDir, 'demo-source.jsonl')),
      project: (outs) => outs.map((o) => (o as { id: string }).id),
      metric: 'exactMatch',
      min: 1,
    },
  ];
}
```

### Task 15: CLI, containment scans and full verification

The CLI is how the kernel is run, resumed, audited and checked. The containment tests are G-CONTAINMENT (submit code only under `src/agents/submit/`) and G-NO-EVASION (no stealth, proxy or CAPTCHA packages can be installed or imported).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/bin.ts`
- Create: `src/cli.ts`

- Test: `tests/containment.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/cli.test.ts`
- Create: `tests/support/agent-scan.ts`
- Test: `tests/cli-commands.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `demoKernel`, `verifyChain`, `exportContracts`, `checkDrift`, `runEval`, `failedGates`, `demoEvalSpecs`.
- Produces: commands `demo`, `resume <runId>`, `audit verify`, `contracts export`, `contracts check`, `eval`.

`tests/containment.test.ts`:

```ts
import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative, sep } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { isAgentCode, scanAgentSource } from './support/agent-scan.js';

const root = join(import.meta.dirname, '..');
const SOURCE_EXT = /\.(ts|mts|cts|tsx|js|mjs|cjs)$/;
const walk = (dir: string): string[] =>
  readdirSync(dir).flatMap((f) => {
    const p = join(dir, f);
    return statSync(p).isDirectory() ? walk(p) : SOURCE_EXT.test(p) ? [p] : [];
  });
const rel = (f: string) => relative(root, f).split(sep).join('/');

const submitPatterns = [
  /\.submit\b/,
  /requestSubmit\(/,
  /prototype\.submit/,
  /new\s+(Submit)?Event\(\s*['"`]submit/,
  /type\W{0,3}submit/i,
  /\bclick\([^)]*(apply|submit)/i,
  /getByRole[^;]*(apply|submit)/i,
  /keyboard\.press\(\s*['"`]Enter/,
];

describe('G-CONTAINMENT: submit-capable code lives only in src/agents/submit/', () => {
  it('finds no submit pattern outside the submit directory', () => {
    const offenders = walk(join(root, 'src'))
      .filter((f) => !rel(f).startsWith('src/agents/submit/'))
      .flatMap((f) => (submitPatterns.some((p) => p.test(readFileSync(f, 'utf8'))) ? [rel(f)] : []));
    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('patterns match known submit forms and ignore look-alikes', () => {
    const positives = [
      'form.submit', 'form.submit()', 'f.requestSubmit()', 'HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit.call(f)',
      "el.dispatchEvent(new Event('submit'))", 'el.dispatchEvent(new SubmitEvent("submit"))', "{ type: 'submit' }",
      'type="submit"', "page.getByRole('button', { name: /apply/i }).click()", "btn.click('Apply now')",
      "page.keyboard.press('Enter')",
    ];
    for (const s of positives) expect(submitPatterns.some((p) => p.test(s)), s).toBe(true);
    for (const s of ['submitted', 'submit_failed', 'deniedSubmitHosts']) {
      expect(submitPatterns.some((p) => p.test(s)), s).toBe(false);
    }
    // each pattern must pull its own weight
    const own = [
      'form.submit', 'f.requestSubmit()', 'HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit', "new Event('submit'", "{ type: 'submit' }",
      "x.click('apply')", "page.getByRole('button', { name: /apply/i })", "keyboard.press('Enter')",
    ];
    submitPatterns.forEach((p, i) => expect(p.test(own[i] as string), String(p)).toBe(true));
  });
});

describe('G-CONTAINMENT: agent code reaches the outside world only through the broker', () => {
  const agentFiles = ['src/agents', 'src/demo']
    .map((d) => join(root, d))
    .filter((d) => existsSync(d))
    .flatMap(walk)
    .filter((f) => isAgentCode(rel(f)));
  const AT = 'src/agents/jobs/score.ts';

  it('finds no imports, globals or reflection outside the allow-list in agent code', () => {
    expect(agentFiles.map(rel)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['src/demo/agents.ts', 'src/demo/types.ts']));
    expect(agentFiles.flatMap((f) => scanAgentSource(rel(f), readFileSync(f, 'utf8')))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('flags every bypass found in review', () => {
    const bypasses = [
      'fetch?.(u)', '(fetch)(u)', 'fetch/**/(u)', 'fetch.call(null, u)', 'fetch`u`', 'const f = fetch', 'fetch(u)',
      "process.getBuiltinModule('node:https')", 'const W = WebSocket; new W(u)', 'new (WebSocket)(u)',
      "(async () => {}).constructor('...')()", "import('./' + '../../kernel/providers.js')", "import('./x.js'.replace('x', 'y'))",
      'globalThis.fetch', "Reflect.get(globalThis, 'fetch')", "(0, eval)('1')", "new Function('')", "require('fs')",
      "import x from '../../kernel/providers.js'", "import('node:fs')",
      // earlier rounds, and the forms the regexes missed
      "f\\u0065tch('u')", "\\u0070rocess.getBuiltinModule('node:fs')", 'export { fetch as f }', "new EventSource('u')",
      "const { fetch: f } = globalThis", "const f = global.fetch", "process['binding']('fs')", 'await import(`./${x}`)', 'await import(m)',
      "x['constructor']", 'new.target', 'import.meta.url', "import x = require('fs')", "export * from '../../kernel/executor.js'",
      "import { fakeLlm } from '../../kernel/fakes.js'", "import type { Providers } from '../../kernel/providers.js'",
      "import { join } from 'node:path'", "import nodeFetch from 'node-fetch'", 'Buffer.from(x)', 'Deno.exit()', 'Bun.file(x)',
      'module.exports = 1', "new XMLHttpRequest()", 'function f(process: unknown) {}',
      'const { constructor: AF } = async () => {}', 'const { constructor } = x', "Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(p, 'constructor')",
      'Reflect.get(p, `constructor`)',
    ];
    for (const s of bypasses) expect(scanAgentSource(AT, s), s).not.toEqual([]);
  });

  it('strips a ?/# suffix from a relative specifier before resolving it', () => {
    const f = 'src/agents/x.ts';
    expect(scanAgentSource(f, "import x from '../kernel/providers.js?/../../agents/x.js'")).toEqual([`${f}:1: import of ../kernel/providers.js?/../../agents/x.js`]);
    expect(scanAgentSource(f, "import x from '../kernel/providers.js#/../../agents/x.js'")).not.toEqual([]);
    expect(scanAgentSource(f, "import x from './y.js?v=1'")).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('reports offenders as file:line: reason', () => {
    expect(scanAgentSource(AT, "const a = 1;\nawait fetch?.('u');")).toEqual([`${AT}:2: reference to fetch`]);
    expect(scanAgentSource(AT, "import x from '../../kernel/providers.js'")).toEqual([`${AT}:1: import of ../../kernel/providers.js`]);
    expect(scanAgentSource(AT, 'const = (')).toEqual([expect.stringMatching(new RegExp(`^${AT}:1: does not parse`))]);
  });

  it('does not flag ordinary agent code', () => {
    const ordinary = [
      'function f(obj: { fetch(n: number): void }) { obj.fetch(1) }', 'const o = { fetch: 1 }', "import { z } from 'zod'",
      "await import('../../kernel/errors.js')", "import { defineAgent } from '../../kernel/agent.js'", "import type { DataClass } from '../../kernel/data-class.js'",
      "import { x } from './helpers.js'", 'class A { fetch() {} process = 1 }', 'const processed = 1; prefetch(processed)', "ctx.http?.getText('u')",
      'type T = { fetch: number; process(): void }', 'const n = items[0]', 'x?.fetch?.(1)',
    ];
    for (const s of ordinary) expect(scanAgentSource(AT, s), s).toEqual([]);
    const demo = 'src/demo/agents.ts';
    expect(scanAgentSource(demo, readFileSync(join(root, demo), 'utf8'))).toEqual([]);
    for (const ok of ["import { DemoItem } from './types.js'", "import { x } from '../agents/jobs/score.js'", "import { defineAgent } from '../kernel/agent.js'"]) {
      expect(scanAgentSource(demo, ok), ok).toEqual([]);
    }
    expect(scanAgentSource(demo, "import { demoKernel } from './kernel.js'")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(scanAgentSource(demo, "import x from '../kernel/providers.js'")).toEqual([`${demo}:1: import of ../kernel/providers.js`]);
  });
});

describe('G-NO-EVASION: anti-bot evasion tooling cannot be installed', () => {
  const deniedExact = [
    'puppeteer-extra', 'puppeteer-extra-plugin-stealth', 'playwright-extra', 'playwright-stealth', 'undetected-chromedriver',
    '2captcha', '@2captcha/captcha-solver', 'anti-captcha', 'capsolver', 'capmonster', 'fingerprint-injector', 'fingerprint-generator',
    'proxy-chain', 'rotating-proxy',
  ];
  const deniedPatterns = [
    /^puppeteer-extra/, /^playwright-extra/, /^@extra\//, /stealth/, /captcha/, /fingerprint/, /^patchright/, /^rebrowser-/,
    /^camoufox/, /proxy-chain/, /rotating-proxy/, /luminati/, /brightdata/, /^undetected/,
  ];
  const isDenied = (name: string) => deniedExact.includes(name) || deniedPatterns.some((p) => p.test(name));
  const pkgName = (spec: string) => (spec.startsWith('@') ? spec.split('/').slice(0, 2).join('/') : (spec.split('/')[0] as string));

  it('name matcher flags evasion packages and spares ordinary ones', () => {
    for (const n of ['puppeteer-extra-plugin-recaptcha', '@extra/foo', 'my-stealth-kit', 'patchright', 'rebrowser-playwright', 'camoufox-js', 'brightdata-sdk', 'undetected-playwright']) {
      expect(isDenied(n), n).toBe(true);
    }
    for (const n of ['better-sqlite3', 'zod', 'commander', '@types/node']) expect(isDenied(n), n).toBe(false);
  });

  it('is not a dependency, transitive dependency or import', () => {
    // Out of reach: code that builds a specifier at run time from variables.
    const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8')) as Record<string, unknown>;
    const asObj = (v: unknown) => (v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) ? (v as Record<string, unknown>) : {});
    const asList = (v: unknown) => (Array.isArray(v) ? (v as string[]) : []);
    const names = [
      ...['dependencies', 'devDependencies', 'optionalDependencies', 'peerDependencies', 'overrides'].flatMap((k) => Object.keys(asObj(pkg[k]))),
      ...Object.keys(asObj(asObj(pkg.pnpm).overrides)),
      ...asList(pkg.bundleDependencies),
      ...asList(pkg.bundledDependencies),
    ];
    const manifest = names.filter(isDenied);

    const imports = walk(join(root, 'src')).flatMap((f) =>
      [...readFileSync(f, 'utf8').matchAll(/['"`]((?:@[\w.-]+\/)?[\w.-][\w./-]*)['"`]/g)]
        .map((m) => pkgName(m[1] as string))
        .filter(isDenied)
        .map((d) => `${rel(f)}: ${d}`),
    );

    const lock = join(root, 'pnpm-lock.yaml');
    expect(existsSync(lock), 'pnpm-lock.yaml is missing: the lockfile scan cannot vouch for transitive dependencies').toBe(true);
    const locked = [...readFileSync(lock, 'utf8').matchAll(/^\s*'?\/?(@?[^\s@'/]+(?:\/[^\s@'/]+)?)@/gm)].map((m) => m[1] as string).filter(isDenied);

    expect({ manifest, imports, locked }).toEqual({ manifest: [], imports: [], locked: [] });
  });
});
```
