`tests/support/agent-scan.ts`:

```ts
import { posix } from 'node:path';
import ts from 'typescript';

/**
 * Containment scan for agent code, done on the syntax tree rather than with regexes, so spacing, comments,
 * parentheses, optional calls, tagged templates and unicode escapes cannot hide a reference.
 *
 * Scope: this is a guard against accidental or naive bypass in first-party agent code (it catches every form in
 * its self-tests), not a sandbox. Deliberate obfuscation can defeat any static scan. Untrusted plugin code is
 * isolated by the worker-thread sandbox planned for the plugin SDK (sub-project 11); the scan is only an extra check.
 */

/** Agent code: everything an agent's run() can execute. The kernel, evals, CLI and demo wiring are the trusted side. */
export const isAgentCode = (path: string) =>
  path.startsWith('src/agents/') || path === 'src/demo/agents.ts' || path === 'src/demo/types.ts';

/** The only kernel modules agent code may import: definitions, never providers, broker, store, executor or fakes. */
const KERNEL_ALLOWED = ['agent', 'contract', 'errors', 'types', 'predicates', 'data-class'].map((m) => `src/kernel/${m}.ts`);
const PACKAGES_ALLOWED = ['zod'];

/** Globals that reach the network, the process, or code built from strings. Agent code may not name them at all. */
const FORBIDDEN_GLOBALS = new Set([
  'fetch', 'WebSocket', 'XMLHttpRequest', 'EventSource', 'process', 'global', 'globalThis', 'Reflect', 'eval', 'Function',
  'require', 'module', 'Buffer', 'Deno', 'Bun',
]);

/** Whether agent code at `file` (repo-relative) may import `spec`. */
export const allowedSpecifier = (file: string, spec: string): boolean => {
  if (!spec.startsWith('.')) return PACKAGES_ALLOWED.includes(spec);
  const target = posix.join(posix.dirname(file), spec.replace(/[?#].*$/s, '')).replace(/\.js$/, '.ts');
  return KERNEL_ALLOWED.includes(target) || isAgentCode(target);
};

/** The module specifier of an import/export declaration, if it has a string one. */
const declarationSpecifier = (node: ts.Node): string | undefined => {
  if ((ts.isImportDeclaration(node) || ts.isExportDeclaration(node)) && node.moduleSpecifier && ts.isStringLiteral(node.moduleSpecifier)) {
    return node.moduleSpecifier.text;
  }
  return undefined;
};

/** An identifier that names a property or member, or an imported binding of an allowed module, rather than a value in scope. */
const isMemberName = (file: string, id: ts.Identifier): boolean => {
  const p = id.parent;
  if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(p) && p.name === id) return true;
  if (ts.isQualifiedName(p) && p.right === id) return true;
  if ((ts.isPropertyAssignment(p) || ts.isMethodDeclaration(p) || ts.isGetAccessorDeclaration(p) || ts.isSetAccessorDeclaration(p)) && p.name === id) return true;
  if ((ts.isPropertyDeclaration(p) || ts.isPropertySignature(p) || ts.isMethodSignature(p)) && p.name === id) return true;
  if (ts.isImportSpecifier(p) || ts.isExportSpecifier(p)) {
    const decl = ts.isImportSpecifier(p) ? p.parent.parent.parent : p.parent.parent;
    const spec = declarationSpecifier(decl);
    return spec !== undefined && allowedSpecifier(file, spec);
  }
  return false;
};

/** Every containment problem in agent source `text` at repo-relative `file`, as `<file>:<line>: <reason>`. */
export const scanAgentSource = (file: string, text: string): string[] => {
  const sf = ts.createSourceFile(file, text, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
  const problems: string[] = [];
  const at = (node: ts.Node, reason: string) =>
    problems.push(`${file}:${sf.getLineAndCharacterOfPosition(node.getStart(sf)).line + 1}: ${reason}`);

  const parseErrors = (sf as unknown as { parseDiagnostics?: readonly ts.Diagnostic[] }).parseDiagnostics ?? [];
  if (parseErrors.length > 0) return [`${file}:1: does not parse (${ts.flattenDiagnosticMessageText(parseErrors[0]?.messageText, ' ')})`];

  const checkSpecifier = (node: ts.Node, spec: string) => {
    if (!allowedSpecifier(file, spec)) at(node, `import of ${spec}`);
  };

  const visit = (node: ts.Node): void => {
    if (ts.isImportDeclaration(node) || ts.isExportDeclaration(node)) {
      const spec = declarationSpecifier(node);
      if (spec !== undefined) checkSpecifier(node, spec);
      else if (node.moduleSpecifier) at(node, 'non-literal module specifier');
    } else if (ts.isImportEqualsDeclaration(node)) {
      const ref = node.moduleReference;
      if (ts.isExternalModuleReference(ref) && ts.isStringLiteral(ref.expression)) checkSpecifier(node, ref.expression.text);
      else at(node, 'import-equals without a literal module specifier');
    } else if (ts.isCallExpression(node) && node.expression.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ImportKeyword) {
      const [arg, ...rest] = node.arguments;
      if (arg && rest.length === 0 && ts.isStringLiteral(arg)) checkSpecifier(node, arg.text);
      else at(node, 'dynamic import without a single string literal');
    } else if (ts.isMetaProperty(node)) {
      at(node, `${node.getText(sf)} is not allowed`);
    } else if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node) && node.name.text === 'constructor') {
      at(node, 'access to .constructor');
    } else if (
      ts.isElementAccessExpression(node) &&
      (ts.isStringLiteral(node.argumentExpression) || ts.isNoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral(node.argumentExpression)) &&
      node.argumentExpression.text === 'constructor'
    ) {
      at(node, 'access to [constructor]');
    } else if (ts.isBindingElement(node) && [node.propertyName, node.name].some((n) => n && ts.isIdentifier(n) && n.text === 'constructor')) {
      at(node, 'destructuring of constructor');
    } else if (
      ts.isCallExpression(node) &&
      node.arguments.some((a) => (ts.isStringLiteral(a) || ts.isNoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral(a)) && a.text === 'constructor')
    ) {
      at(node, "'constructor' passed as an argument");
    } else if (ts.isIdentifier(node) && FORBIDDEN_GLOBALS.has(node.text) && !isMemberName(file, node)) {
      at(node, `reference to ${node.text}`);
    }
    ts.forEachChild(node, visit);
  };
  visit(sf);
  return problems;
};
```

```ts
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const bin = readFileSync(join(import.meta.dirname, '..', 'src', 'bin.ts'), 'utf8');

describe('src/bin.ts: the Node version guard runs before anything is loaded', () => {
  it('has no static import of a non-node: specifier', () => {
    const specs = [...bin.matchAll(/^\s*import\s[^;]*?from\s*['"]([^'"]+)['"]/gm)].map((m) => m[1]);
    expect(specs.filter((s) => !s?.startsWith('node:'))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('checks the version before importing the CLI', () => {
    const check = bin.indexOf('process.versions.node');
    const load = bin.indexOf("import('./cli.js')");
    expect(check).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    expect(load).toBeGreaterThan(check);
  });
});

describe('audit verify', () => {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'jobpilot-cli-'));
  afterAll(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  const db = join(dir, 'jp.db');
  const cli = (...args: string[]) =>
    spawnSync(process.execPath, ['--import', 'tsx', 'src/bin.ts', ...args], { cwd: join(import.meta.dirname, '..'), encoding: 'utf8' });

  it('prints the chain head and checks it against --expect-head', () => {
    const demo = cli('demo', '--db', db, '--count', '2', '--run', 'run-1');
    expect(demo.status).toBe(0);

    const ok = cli('audit', 'verify', '--db', db);
    expect(ok.status).toBe(0);
    const m = /^audit chain intact: (\d+) events\nhead ([0-9a-f]+) \((\d+) events\)$/m.exec(ok.stdout);
    expect(m).not.toBeNull();
    const [, n, hash, count] = m as RegExpExecArray;
    expect(count).toBe(n);

    const good = cli('audit', 'verify', '--db', db, '--expect-head', `${hash}:${count}`);
    expect(good.status).toBe(0);
    expect(good.stdout).toContain('audit chain intact');

    const upper = cli('audit', 'verify', '--db', db, '--expect-head', `${hash!.toUpperCase()}:${count}`);
    expect(upper.status).toBe(0);
    expect(upper.stdout).toContain('audit chain intact');

    const bad = cli('audit', 'verify', '--db', db, '--expect-head', 'deadbeef:1');
    expect(bad.status).toBe(1);
    expect(bad.stdout).toContain('chain head mismatch');
    expect(bad.stdout).toContain('chain head differs from the expected head');
    expect(bad.stdout).not.toContain('BROKEN');
  }, 60_000);

  it('rejects a malformed --expect-head', () => {
    const r = cli('audit', 'verify', '--db', db, '--expect-head', 'nonsense');
    expect(r.status).toBe(1);
  }, 30_000);
});

describe('contracts export', () => {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'jobpilot-contracts-'));
  afterAll(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  const cli = (...args: string[]) =>
    spawnSync(process.execPath, ['--import', 'tsx', 'src/bin.ts', ...args], { cwd: join(import.meta.dirname, '..'), encoding: 'utf8' });

  it('prints a refusal as error: <message> with exit code 1 and no stack', () => {
    const ok = cli('contracts', 'export', '--out', dir);
    expect(ok.status).toBe(0);
    expect(ok.stdout).toContain(`wrote ${join(dir, 'demo-source@1.json')}`);
    // The exported 1.0.0 file now records a different egress than the code has at the same version.
    const file = join(dir, 'demo-source@1.json');
    const doc = JSON.parse(readFileSync(file, 'utf8'));
    writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify({ hash: 'other', shape: { ...doc.shape, egress: ['evil.example'] } }));
    const r = cli('contracts', 'export', '--out', dir);
    expect(r.status).toBe(1);
    expect(r.stderr).toBe('error: demo-source@1.0.0: egress changed without a version bump; bump the major version before exporting\n');
    expect(r.stdout).toBe('');
  }, 60_000);
});
```

```ts
import { execFileSync, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { fakeLlm } from '../src/kernel/fakes.js';
import { demoKernel } from '../src/demo/kernel.js';
import { Store } from '../src/kernel/store/store.js';

const root = join(import.meta.dirname, '..');
const cli = (...args: string[]) => spawnSync(process.execPath, ['--import', 'tsx', join(root, 'src/bin.ts'), ...args], { cwd: root, encoding: 'utf8' });

let dir: string;
let db: string;
beforeEach(() => {
  dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'jobpilot-cli-'));
  db = join(dir, 'j.db');
});
afterEach(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));

describe('jobpilot quarantined / release', () => {
  it('lists quarantined steps and releases one by step key', async () => {
    const k = demoKernel({ dbPath: db, llm: fakeLlm(() => JSON.stringify({ score: 99, reason: 'too keen' })) });
    await k.supervisor.start('run-1', [{ count: 2, prefix: 'demo' }]);
    const keys = k.store.quarantinedSteps().map((s) => s.step_key);
    k.store.close();
    expect(keys).toHaveLength(2);

    const listed = cli('quarantined', '--db', db);
    expect(listed.status).toBe(0);
    for (const key of keys) expect(listed.stdout).toContain(key);
    expect(listed.stdout).toContain('demo-scorer');
    expect(listed.stdout).toContain('G-POST');

    const released = cli('release', keys[0]!, '--db', db);
    expect(released.status).toBe(0);
    expect(released.stdout).toContain(keys[0]);
    expect(released.stdout).toMatch(/\(entities back: demo-item\/demo-00\d\)/);
    const again = cli('release', keys[0]!, '--db', db);
    expect(again.status).toBe(1);

    const store = Store.open(db);
    expect(store.quarantinedSteps().map((s) => s.step_key)).toEqual([keys[1]]);
    const ev = store.events().find((e) => e.kind === 'quarantine_released');
    expect(ev?.run_id).toMatch(/^release-\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/);
    store.close();
  });

  it('records the release under the policy hash of the run that quarantined the step', async () => {
    const k = demoKernel({ dbPath: db, userPolicy: { maxUsdPerRun: 1 }, llm: fakeLlm(() => JSON.stringify({ score: 99, reason: 'too keen' })) });
    await k.supervisor.start('run-1', [{ count: 1, prefix: 'demo' }]);
    const [key] = k.store.quarantinedSteps().map((s) => s.step_key);
    const runPolicy = k.policyHash;
    k.store.close();
    expect(runPolicy).not.toBe(demoKernel().policyHash);
    expect(cli('release', key!, '--db', db).status).toBe(0);
    const store = Store.open(db);
    expect(store.quarantinePolicyHash(key!)).toBe(runPolicy);
    expect(store.quarantinePolicyHash('no-such-key')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(store.events().find((e) => e.kind === 'quarantine_released')?.policy_hash).toBe(runPolicy);
    store.close();
  });

  it('says so when nothing is quarantined', () => {
    execFileSync(process.execPath, ['--import', 'tsx', join(root, 'src/bin.ts'), 'demo', '--db', db, '--run', 'r1', '--count', '1'], { cwd: root });
    const listed = cli('quarantined', '--db', db);
    expect(listed.status).toBe(0);
    expect(listed.stdout).toContain('no quarantined steps');
  });
});
```

```ts
// Entry point. Nothing but node: builtins may be imported here: a native addon built for another
// Node ABI segfaults instead of failing, so the version check must run before any module loads.
const major = Number(process.versions.node.split('.')[0]);
if (major < 24) {
  console.error(`jobpilot needs Node 24 or newer; this is Node ${process.versions.node} (${process.execPath}).`);
  process.exit(1);
}

await import('./cli.js');
```

`src/cli.ts`:

```ts
import { join } from 'node:path';

import { Command } from 'commander';
import { checkDrift, exportContracts } from './kernel/contracts-export.js';
import { systemClock } from './kernel/clock.js';
import { PRODUCT_POLICY_V1 } from './kernel/policy.js';
import { Registry } from './kernel/registry.js';
import { verifyChain } from './kernel/store/audit.js';
import { Store } from './kernel/store/store.js';
import { failedGates, runEval } from './evals/runner.js';
import { demoEvalSpecs } from './demo/evals.js';
import { demoKernel } from './demo/kernel.js';
import { demoAgents } from './demo/pipeline.js';

const root = join(import.meta.dirname, '..');
const allAgents = () => {
  const r = new Registry();
  for (const a of demoAgents) r.register(a);
  return r;
};

/** The detail of a quarantined step is its guardrail verdicts as JSON; show the one that tripped. */
function lastVerdict(detail: string | null): string {
  try {
    const v = (JSON.parse(detail ?? '[]') as { id?: string; detail?: string }[]).at(-1);
    return v ? `${v.id ?? '?'}: ${v.detail ?? ''}` : '';
  } catch {
    return detail ?? '';
  }
}

const program = new Command().name('jobpilot').description('Deterministic multi-agent job search kernel');

program
  .command('demo')
  .description('Run the two-agent demo pipeline end to end')
  .option('--db <path>', 'SQLite file', 'jobpilot.db')
  .option('--count <n>', 'items to generate', '5')
  .option('--run <id>', 'run id', `run-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`)
  .action(async (o: { db: string; count: string; run: string }) => {
    const count = Number(o.count);
    if (!Number.isInteger(count) || count < 1 || count > 50) {
      console.error('--count must be an integer from 1 to 50');
      process.exit(1);
    }
    const k = demoKernel({ dbPath: o.db, clock: systemClock });
    try {
      const report = await k.supervisor.start(o.run, [{ count, prefix: 'demo' }]);
      console.log(JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
    } finally {
      k.store.close();
    }
  });

program
  .command('resume <runId>')
  .description('Continue a crashed run; finished steps are replayed, not re-run')
  .option('--db <path>', 'SQLite file', 'jobpilot.db')
  .action(async (runId: string, o: { db: string }) => {
    const k = demoKernel({ dbPath: o.db, clock: systemClock });
    try {
      console.log(JSON.stringify(await k.supervisor.resume(runId), null, 2));
    } finally {
      k.store.close();
    }
  });

program
  .command('quarantined')
  .description('List quarantined steps with their last guardrail verdict')
  .option('--db <path>', 'SQLite file', 'jobpilot.db')
  .action((o: { db: string }) => {
    const store = Store.open(o.db);
    try {
      const steps = store.quarantinedSteps();
      if (steps.length === 0) console.log('no quarantined steps');
      for (const s of steps) console.log(`${s.step_key}  ${s.agent_id}  ${lastVerdict(s.detail)}`);
    } finally {
      store.close();
    }
  });

program
  .command('release <stepKey>')
  .description('Release a quarantined step so the next run executes it again')
  .option('--db <path>', 'SQLite file', 'jobpilot.db')
  .action((stepKey: string, o: { db: string }) => {
    const k = demoKernel({ dbPath: o.db, clock: systemClock });
    try {
      const now = systemClock.now();
      // Audited under the policy that quarantined the step; the current one only if that run is unknown.
      const policyHash = k.store.quarantinePolicyHash(stepKey) ?? k.policyHash;
      const r = k.store.releaseQuarantine(stepKey, `release-${now.slice(0, 10)}`, now, policyHash);
      const refs = (xs: readonly { kind: string; entityId: string }[]) => xs.map((x) => `${x.kind}/${x.entityId}`).join(', ');
      if (!r.released) {
        console.error(`nothing is quarantined under ${stepKey}`);
        process.exitCode = 1;
      } else {
        console.log(`released ${stepKey}${r.entities.length > 0 ? ` (entities back: ${refs(r.entities)})` : ''}`);
      }
      if (r.skipped.length > 0) console.log(`left alone (moved on since the trip): ${refs(r.skipped)}`);
    } finally {
      k.store.close();
    }
  });

const audit = program.command('audit').description('Audit log tools');
audit
  .command('verify')
  .description('Walk the hash-chained event log')
  .option('--db <path>', 'SQLite file', 'jobpilot.db')
  .option('--expect-head <hash:count>', 'fail unless the chain head is exactly this (detects a truncated or rewritten tail)')
  .action((o: { db: string; expectHead?: string }) => {
    let expected: { hash: string; count: number } | undefined;
    if (o.expectHead !== undefined) {
      const m = /^([0-9a-fA-F]+):(\d+)$/.exec(o.expectHead);
      if (!m) {
        console.error('--expect-head must look like <hash>:<count>');
        process.exit(1);
      }
      expected = { hash: (m[1] as string).toLowerCase(), count: Number(m[2]) };
    }
    const store = Store.open(o.db);
    try {
      const r = verifyChain(store, expected);
      if (r.ok) {
        console.log(`audit chain intact: ${r.events} events`);
        const head = store.chainHead();
        if (head) console.log(`head ${head.hash} (${head.count} events)`);
      } else if ('headMismatch' in r) {
        const head = store.chainHead();
        console.log(`audit chain intact (${r.events} events) but chain head mismatch: ${r.reason}`);
        console.log(`head ${head?.hash ?? 'none'} (${head?.count ?? 0} events)`);
        process.exitCode = 1;
      } else {
        console.log(`audit chain BROKEN at event ${r.brokenAt}: ${r.reason}`);
        process.exitCode = 1;
      }
    } finally {
      store.close();
    }
  });

const contracts = program.command('contracts').description('Agent contract tools');
contracts
  .command('export')
  .description('Write every contract as JSON Schema into contracts-out/')
  .option('--out <dir>', 'directory to write into', join(root, 'contracts-out'))
  .action((o: { out: string }) => {
    let paths: string[];
    try {
      paths = exportContracts(allAgents(), o.out);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(`error: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`);
      process.exitCode = 1;
      return;
    }
    for (const p of paths) console.log(`wrote ${p}`);
  });
contracts
  .command('check')
  .description('Fail if any contract changed without a version bump')
  .action(() => {
    const problems = checkDrift(allAgents(), join(root, 'contracts-out'));
    for (const p of problems) console.error(p);
    if (problems.length > 0) process.exitCode = 1;
    else console.log('contracts match contracts-out/');
  });

program
  .command('eval')
  .description('Run every golden-set eval and enforce its gate')
  .action(async () => {
    const reports = [];
    for (const spec of demoEvalSpecs(join(root, 'evals/datasets'))) {
      reports.push(await runEval(spec, { providers: { fetch: async () => ({ status: 404, text: async () => '' }) }, policy: PRODUCT_POLICY_V1 }));
    }
    for (const r of reports) console.log(`${r.pass ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${r.agentId}@${r.version} ${r.metric}=${r.score.toFixed(3)} (min ${r.min}, n=${r.n})`);
    const failed = failedGates(reports);
    if (failed.length > 0) process.exitCode = 1;
  });

await program.parseAsync();
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the tests and the typecheck**

Run: `node --run test -- tests/containment.test.ts tests/cli.test.ts tests/cli-commands.test.ts && node --run typecheck`
Expected: PASS, 17 tests in 3 file(s); typecheck prints nothing.

- [ ] **Step 5: Run the whole verification and a real demo**

```bash
node --run verify
D=$(mktemp -d)
node --run jobpilot -- demo --db $D/jp.db --count 3 --run run-1
node --run jobpilot -- demo --db $D/jp.db --count 3 --run run-2
node --run jobpilot -- audit verify --db $D/jp.db
```

- First demo run: `"done": 4`, `"costUsd": 0.003`.
- Second demo run on the same database: `"replayed": 4`, `"costUsd": 0` (nothing re-executed, nothing paid).
- Audit: `audit chain intact: 12 events` (run 2 records a `step_replayed` event per reused step).

---

## Done when

- `node --run verify` is green: typecheck, 243 tests, contract drift check, eval gate.
- The four acceptance tests in `tests/kernel/supervisor.test.ts` pass:
  - **determinism**: two fresh databases end byte-identical;
  - **replay**: a second run invokes no agent and costs $0;
  - **crash-resume**: no finished step runs twice;
  - **quarantine, breaker and budget** behave as specified.
- `git log --oneline kernel` shows the 15 task commits plus their reviewed fix commits, and nothing is pushed.

## What the next sub-project builds on

Sub-project 2 (user pack and onboarding) and 3 (ATS discovery) add agents through the same interfaces: `defineType`, `definePredicate`, `defineContract`, `defineAgent`, a `Pipeline` with teams and edges, and an `EvalSpec` per agent. They also need specific kernel extensions (a pack capability whose hash joins the step key, per-host rate limits, response size caps, a broker interceptor hook for session agents, and more). Those are scheduled per sub-project in the spec, §16.1, and each is planned and reviewed as kernel work at the start of its sub-project.
